  const char* tr = isEnglish ? "web" : "rvr";
  snprintf(url, sizeof url, "https://bible-api.com/%s+%d?translation=%s", apiBook, chapter, tr);
  String payload;
  int code = httpsGet(url, payload);
  if (code != 200 && code != 202) {
    delay(400);
    code = httpsGet(url, payload);  // un reintento
  }
  if (code != 200 && code != 202) return false;
  StaticJsonDocument<48> filter; filter["text"] = true;
  // El documento se dimensiona con el tamano real de la respuesta (Salmos 119 pasa de 20 KB)
  size_t cap = payload.length() + 256; if (cap > 40000) cap = 40000;
  DynamicJsonDocument doc(cap);
  if (doc.capacity() == 0) { lastHttpCode = -4; return false; }
  DeserializationError err = deserializeJson(doc, payload, DeserializationOption::Filter(filter));
  payload = String();                                // libera memoria antes de copiar el texto
  if (err) return false;
  const char* t = doc["text"] | "";
  if (!t[0]) return false;
  size_t n = strlen(t);
  char* tmp = (char*)malloc(n + 1);
  if (!tmp) return false;
  memcpy(tmp, t, n + 1);
  for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) if (tmp[i] == '\n' || tmp[i] == '\r') tmp[i] = ' ';
  utf8ToCp437(tmp);                                  // conserva n, tildes y signos con la fuente de la pantalla
  outText = tmp; free(tmp);
  outText.trim();
  return true;
}

void runBible() {
  settle();
  if (WiFi.status() != WL_CONNECTED) {
    while (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == HIGH) {
      drawNeedWifi();
      flushScreen(); delay(30);
    }
    currentApp = -1; settle(); return;
  }
  uint8_t step = 0;
  int book = 0, chapter = 1;
  String body = "";
  float scrollY = 0, targetY = 0;
  unsigned long lastMove = 0;
  while (true) {
    if (step == 0) {
      display.clearDisplay();
      display.setTextSize(1); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.setCursor(0, 0); display.print(isEnglish ? F("BIBLE") : F("BIBLIA"));
      char cnt[10]; snprintf(cnt, sizeof cnt, "%d/%d", book + 1, BIBLE_N);
      display.setCursor(128 - (int)strlen(cnt) * 6, 0); display.print(cnt);
      uint8_t st = menuStart((uint8_t)book, BIBLE_N);
      for (uint8_t r = 0; r < 4 && st + r < BIBLE_N; r++) {
        uint8_t i = st + r; int y = 13 + r * 12;
        const char* nm = isEnglish ? BIBLE_EN[i] : BIBLE_ES[i];
        if (i == (uint8_t)book) {
          display.fillRoundRect(4, y, 116, 11, 3, SSD1306_WHITE);
          display.setTextColor(SSD1306_BLACK);
        } else display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
        display.setCursor(62 - (int)strlen(nm) * 3, y + 2); display.print(nm);
      }
      display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
      if (st > 0) display.fillTriangle(124, 15, 121, 19, 127, 19, SSD1306_WHITE);
      if (st + 4 < BIBLE_N) display.fillTriangle(121, 55, 127, 55, 124, 59, SSD1306_WHITE);
      flushScreen();
      if (millis() - lastMove > 160) {
        int vy = getJoyY();
        if (vy < 300) { book = (book + 1) % BIBLE_N; lastMove = millis(); }
        else if (vy > 700) { book = (book + BIBLE_N - 1) % BIBLE_N; lastMove = millis(); }
      }
      if (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) { chapter = 1; step = 1; settle(); }
      if (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == LOW) { settle(); break; }
    } else if (step == 1) {
      uint8_t maxCh = BIBLE_CH[book];
      display.clearDisplay();
      display.setTextSize(1); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.setCursor(0, 0); display.print(isEnglish ? BIBLE_EN[book] : BIBLE_ES[book]);
      char cnt[12]; snprintf(cnt, sizeof cnt, "%d/%d", chapter, maxCh);
      display.setCursor(128 - (int)strlen(cnt) * 6, 0); display.print(cnt);
      display.drawLine(0, 10, 128, 10, SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.setTextSize(2);
      char chs[8]; snprintf(chs, sizeof chs, "%d", chapter);
      display.setCursor(64 - (int)strlen(chs) * 6, 24); display.print(chs);
      display.setTextSize(1);
      drawHint(-1, 50, isEnglish ? "{1}: read  {2}: back" : "{1}: leer  {2}: atras", SSD1306_WHITE);
      flushScreen();
      if (millis() - lastMove > 140) {
        int vx = getJoyX(), vy = getJoyY();
        if (vx < 300 || vy < 300) { chapter++; if (chapter > maxCh) chapter = 1; lastMove = millis(); }
        else if (vx > 700 || vy > 700) { chapter--; if (chapter < 1) chapter = maxCh; lastMove = millis(); }
      }
      if (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) {
        delay(200);
        display.clearDisplay(); display.setCursor(20, 28);
        display.print(isEnglish ? F("Loading...") : F("Cargando...")); flushScreen();
        if (!bibleFetch(BIBLE_API[book], chapter, body))
          netErrorToast();
        else { scrollY = targetY = 0; step = 2; }
        delay(200);
      }
      if (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == LOW) { settle(); step = 0; while (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == LOW) delay(10); }
    } else {                                            // lectura: palabras sin cortar, desplazamiento rapido, L/R = capitulo
      char head[40];
      snprintf(head, sizeof head, "%s %d", isEnglish ? BIBLE_EN[book] : BIBLE_ES[book], chapter);
      uint8_t v = viewText(head, body.c_str(), nullptr, 4);
      if (v == 5 || v == 6) {
        chapter += (v == 5) ? 1 : -1;
        if (chapter > (int)BIBLE_CH[book]) chapter = 1;
        if (chapter < 1) chapter = BIBLE_CH[book];
        display.clearDisplay(); display.setTextSize(1); display.setCursor(20, 28);
        display.print(isEnglish ? F("Loading...") : F("Cargando...")); flushScreen();
        if (!bibleFetch(BIBLE_API[book], chapter, body)) { netErrorToast(); step = 1; body = ""; }
      } else { step = 1; body = ""; }
    }
    delay(15);
  }
  currentApp = -1; settle();
}

// ===================== IA =====================
void runIAApp() {
  settle();
  if (WiFi.status() != WL_CONNECTED) {
    while (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == HIGH) {
      drawNeedWifi();
      flushScreen(); delay(30);
    }
    currentApp = -1; settle();
    return;
  }
  char q[64] = "";
  while (true) {
    if (!textInput(isEnglish ? "Ask:" : "Pregunta:", q, sizeof q)) break;      // {2} = salir de la app
    if (!q[0]) continue;
    display.clearDisplay();
    display.setTextSize(1); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
    {
      const char* m = isEnglish ? "Searching..." : "Buscando...";
      display.setCursor(64 - (int)strlen(m) * 3, 22); display.print(m);
      display.setCursor(10, 36);
      // pregunta recortada
      char prev[22];
      size_t n = strlen(q);
      if (n > 20) { memcpy(prev, q, 17); prev[17] = '.'; prev[18] = '.'; prev[19] = '.'; prev[20] = 0; }
      else { memcpy(prev, q, n + 1); }
      display.print(prev);
    }
    flushScreen();
    String enc; urlEncodeCp(q, enc);
    String url = "https://api.duckduckgo.com/?q=" + enc + "&format=json&no_html=1&skip_disambig=1";
    String payload;
    int code = httpsGet(url.c_str(), payload);
    if (code != 200 && code != 202) {
      delay(500);
      code = httpsGet(url.c_str(), payload);
    }
    if (code != 200 && code != 202) { netErrorToast(); continue; }
    String resp;
    StaticJsonDocument<192> filter;
    filter["AbstractText"] = true; filter["Answer"] = true; filter["Definition"] = true;
    filter["RelatedTopics"][0]["Text"] = true;
    DynamicJsonDocument doc(6144);
    DeserializationError jerr = deserializeJson(doc, payload, DeserializationOption::Filter(filter));
    payload = String();
    if (jerr) resp = isEnglish ? "Parse error." : "Error leyendo la respuesta.";
    else {
      const char* tx = doc["AbstractText"] | "";
      if (!tx[0]) tx = doc["Answer"] | "";
      if (!tx[0]) tx = doc["Definition"] | "";
      if (!tx[0]) tx = doc["RelatedTopics"][0]["Text"] | "";
      resp = tx[0] ? String(tx) : String(isEnglish ? "WOOF! No data for that." : "WAF! No hay datos para eso.");
    }
    size_t n = resp.length();
    char* tmp = (char*)malloc(n + 1);
    if (tmp) { memcpy(tmp, resp.c_str(), n + 1); utf8ToCp437(tmp); resp = tmp; free(tmp); }
    uint8_t r = viewText(isEnglish ? "IA SAYS:" : "PERRO IA RESPONDE:", resp.c_str(),
                         isEnglish ? "{1} edit {4} new" : "{1} editar {4} otra", 2);
    if (r == 4) q[0] = 0;           // otra pregunta
    else if (r == 0) break;         // salir
    // r == 1: vuelve al teclado con la misma pregunta
  }
  currentApp = -1; settle();
}

// ===================== SNAKE =====================
void runSnake() {
  const uint8_t GW = 30, GH = 12, CELL = 4;
  const uint8_t OX = 4, OY = 14;
  static uint8_t sx[360], sy[360];
  static uint16_t best = 0; static bool bestLd = false;
  if (!bestLd) { best = loadBest("snake"); bestLd = true; }
  const int8_t dx[4] = {1, 0, -1, 0};
  const int8_t dy[4] = {0, 1, 0, -1};

  randomSeed(micros());
  settle();

  bool exitGame = false;
  while (!exitGame) {
    uint16_t len = 3;
    uint8_t dir = 0, nextDir = 0;
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < len; i++) {
      sx[i] = 5 - i;
      sy[i] = GH / 2;
    }
    uint8_t fx, fy;
    auto placeFood = [&]() {
      bool ok;
      do {
        fx = random(GW);
        fy = random(GH);
        ok = true;
        for (uint16_t i = 0; i < len; i++)
          if (sx[i] == fx && sy[i] == fy) { ok = false; break; }
      } while (!ok);
    };
    placeFood();

    uint16_t score = 0;
    bool dead = false;
    unsigned long lastTick = millis();

    while (!dead) {
      if (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == LOW) {
        if (confirmExit("Snake", "Snake")) { exitGame = true; break; }
        lastTick = millis();
      }

      int vx = getJoyX(), vy = getJoyY();
      uint8_t want = nextDir;
      if (vx < 300) want = 0;
      else if (vy < 300) want = 1;
      else if (vx > 700) want = 2;
      else if (vy > 700) want = 3;
      if ((want + 2) % 4 != dir) nextDir = want;

      unsigned long interval = max(60, 150 - (int)score * 3);
      if (millis() - lastTick >= interval) {
        lastTick = millis();
        dir = nextDir;
        int8_t nx = (int8_t)sx[0] + dx[dir];
        int8_t ny = (int8_t)sy[0] + dy[dir];

        if (nx < 0 || nx >= GW || ny < 0 || ny >= GH) dead = true;
        else {
          bool eat = (nx == fx && ny == fy);
          uint16_t check = eat ? len : len - 1;
          for (uint16_t i = 0; i < check; i++) if (sx[i] == nx && sy[i] == ny) { dead = true; break; }
          if (!dead) {
            if (eat && len < 360) len++;
            for (int16_t i = len - 1; i > 0; i--) { sx[i] = sx[i-1]; sy[i] = sy[i-1]; }
            sx[0] = nx; sy[0] = ny;
            if (eat) {
              score++;
              if (score > best) best = score;
              if (len >= GW * GH) dead = true; else placeFood();
            }
          }
        }
      }

      display.clearDisplay();
      display.setTextSize(1);
      display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.setCursor(0, 2);
      display.print(isEnglish ? F("SNAKE  Score:") : F("SNAKE  Puntos:"));
      display.print(score);
      display.drawRect(OX - 1, OY - 2, GW * CELL + 2, GH * CELL + 4, SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.fillRect(OX + fx * CELL, OY + fy * CELL, CELL - 1, CELL - 1, SSD1306_WHITE);
      for (uint16_t i = 0; i < len; i++) {
        if (i == 0) display.fillRect(OX + sx[i] * CELL, OY + sy[i] * CELL, CELL, CELL, SSD1306_WHITE);
        else display.fillRect(OX + sx[i] * CELL, OY + sy[i] * CELL, CELL - 1, CELL - 1, SSD1306_WHITE);
      }
      flushScreen();
      delay(5);
    }

    saveBest("snake", best);
    if (exitGame) break;

    if (!gameOverScreen(score, best, "Snake")) exitGame = true;     // con confirmacion y esperando a soltar ESC
  }
  settle();
}

static void drawGameOver(uint16_t score, uint16_t best) {
  display.clearDisplay();
  display.setTextSize(1);
  display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
  display.setCursor(34, 6);  display.print(F("GAME OVER"));
  display.setCursor(20, 22); display.print(isEnglish ? F("Score: ") : F("Puntos: ")); display.print(score);
  display.setCursor(20, 33); display.print(isEnglish ? F("Best:  ") : F("R\202cord: ")); display.print(best);
  drawHint(-1, 48, isEnglish ? "{1}: again  {2}: exit" : "{1}: otra  {2}: salir", SSD1306_WHITE);
  flushScreen();
}

// true = jugar otra vez, false = salir. ESC pide confirmacion y espera a soltar el boton,
// asi el mismo toque no cierra tambien el menu de Juegos.
bool gameOverScreen(uint16_t score, uint16_t best, const char* nm) {
  drawGameOver(score, best);
  delay(500);
  { unsigned long w0 = millis(); while (anyBtn() && millis() - w0 < 1500) delay(5); }   // ignora botones que seguian pulsados del juego
  delay(60);
  while (true) {
    if (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) { settle(); return true; }
    if (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == LOW) {
      if (confirmExit(nm ? nm : (isEnglish ? "Game" : "Juego"), nm ? nm : (isEnglish ? "Game" : "Juego"))) { settle(); return false; }
      drawGameOver(score, best);                      // dijo que no: vuelve a la pantalla de fin
    }
    delay(10);
  }
}

// ===================== DINO =====================
void runDino() {
  static uint16_t best = 0; static bool bestLd = false;
  if (!bestLd) { best = loadBest("dino"); bestLd = true; }
  const uint8_t GROUND = 54;
  const uint8_t DX = 14;
  randomSeed(micros());
  settle();

  bool quit = false;
  while (!quit) {
    float h = 0, vy = 0;
    bool onGround = true;
    float ox[3]; uint8_t ow[3], oh[3];
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      ox[i] = 140 + i * 110 + random(0, 30);
      ow[i] = random(5, 9); oh[i] = random(9, 17);
    }
    uint32_t frames = 0;
    uint16_t score = 0;
    bool dead = false;
    unsigned long last = millis();

    while (!dead && !quit) {
      if (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == LOW) {
        if (confirmExit("Dino Run", "Dino Run")) { quit = true; break; }
        last = millis();
      }
      if (millis() - last < 30) { delay(1); continue; }
      last = millis();
      frames++;

      score = frames / 4;
      if (score > best) best = score;
      float speed = 2.6 + min(1.4f, score * 0.004f);

      bool jump = (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) || (digitalRead(PIN_3) == LOW) || (getJoyY() > 700);
      if (jump && onGround) { vy = 5.6; onGround = false; }
      if (!onGround) {
        h += vy; vy -= 0.5;
        if (h <= 0) { h = 0; vy = 0; onGround = true; }
      }

      for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        ox[i] -= speed;
        if (ox[i] + ow[i] < 0) {
          float farX = ox[0];
          for (uint8_t j = 1; j < 3; j++) if (ox[j] > farX) farX = ox[j];
          if (farX < 128) farX = 128;
          ox[i] = farX + random(100, 150) + (speed - 2.6) * 15;
          ow[i] = random(5, 9); oh[i] = random(9, 17);
        }
      }

      int dTop = GROUND - (int)h - 10, dBot = GROUND - (int)h;
      for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        int cx0 = (int)ox[i], cx1 = cx0 + ow[i], cTop = GROUND - oh[i];
        if (DX + 7 > cx0 && DX + 1 < cx1 && dBot - 1 > cTop && dTop + 1 < GROUND) dead = true;
      }

      display.clearDisplay();
      display.setTextSize(1); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.setCursor(0, 0);
      display.print(F("HI ")); display.print(best);
      display.setCursor(80, 0);
      display.print(score);
      display.drawFastHLine(0, GROUND, 128, SSD1306_WHITE);
      for (int x = -(int)((frames * 3) % 16); x < 128; x += 16) display.drawPixel(x + 6, GROUND + 3, SSD1306_WHITE);

      display.fillRect(DX, dTop + 2, 6, 6, SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.fillRect(DX + 3, dTop, 5, 4, SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.drawPixel(DX + 6, dTop + 1, SSD1306_BLACK);
      display.drawPixel(DX - 1, dTop + 3, SSD1306_WHITE);
      if (!onGround || (frames & 4)) display.fillRect(DX + 1, dTop + 8, 2, 2, SSD1306_WHITE);
      else display.fillRect(DX + 4, dTop + 8, 2, 2, SSD1306_WHITE);

      for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        int x = (int)ox[i];
        if (x > 127) continue;
        int top = GROUND - oh[i];
        display.fillRect(x + ow[i] / 2 - 1, top, 3, oh[i], SSD1306_WHITE);
        display.fillRect(x, top + 3, 2, 4, SSD1306_WHITE);
        display.fillRect(x + ow[i] - 2, top + 5, 2, 4, SSD1306_WHITE);
      }
      flushScreen();
    }

    if (quit) break;
    saveBest("dino", best); if (!gameOverScreen(score, best, "Dino Run")) quit = true;
  }
  settle();
}

// ===================== BREAKOUT =====================
void runBreakout() {
  static uint16_t best = 0; static bool bestLd = false;
  if (!bestLd) { best = loadBest("breakout"); bestLd = true; }
  const uint8_t PW = 20;
  randomSeed(micros());
  settle();

  bool quit = false;
  while (!quit) {
    bool bricks[4][10];
    for (uint8_t r = 0; r < 4; r++) for (uint8_t c = 0; c < 10; c++) bricks[r][c] = true;
    uint8_t left = 40, lives = 3, level = 1;
    uint16_t score = 0;
    float spd = 1.8, px = 54;
    float bx = 64, by = 57, vx = 0, vy = 0;
    bool launched = false, dead = false;
    unsigned long last = millis();

    while (!dead && !quit) {
      if (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == LOW) {
        if (confirmExit("Breakout", "Breakout")) { quit = true; break; }
        last = millis();
      }
      if (millis() - last < 25) { delay(1); continue; }
      last = millis();

      int jx = getJoyX();
      if (jx < 300) px += 3.5; else if (jx > 700) px -= 3.5;
      px = constrain(px, 1, 127 - PW);

      if (!launched) {
        bx = px + PW / 2; by = 57;
        if (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) {
          launched = true;
          vx = (random(2) ? 0.8 : -0.8);
          vy = -sqrt(spd * spd - vx * vx);
        }
      } else {
        bx += vx; by += vy;
        if (bx < 1)   { bx = 1;   vx = -vx; }
        if (bx > 125) { bx = 125; vx = -vx; }
        if (by < 10)  { by = 10;  vy = -vy; }

        if (vy > 0 && by + 2 >= 60 && by <= 62 && bx + 2 >= px && bx <= px + PW) {
          float rel = (bx + 1 - (px + PW / 2.0)) / (PW / 2.0);
          vx = constrain(rel * spd * 0.85, -spd * 0.85, spd * 0.85);
          vy = -sqrt(spd * spd - vx * vx);
          by = 58;
        }

        if (bx >= 4 && bx < 124 && by >= 12 && by < 32) {
          uint8_t c = (uint8_t)((bx - 4) / 12), r = (uint8_t)((by - 12) / 5);
          if (c < 10 && r < 4 && bricks[r][c]) {
            bricks[r][c] = false; left--;
            score += 10;
            if (score > best) best = score;
            vy = -vy;
          }
        }

        if (by > 63) {
          lives--;
          launched = false;
          delay(400);
          if (lives == 0) dead = true;
        }

        if (left == 0) {
          level++;
          spd = min(3.0f, spd + 0.3f);
          for (uint8_t r = 0; r < 4; r++) for (uint8_t c = 0; c < 10; c++) bricks[r][c] = true;
          left = 40; launched = false;
          delay(500);
        }
      }

      display.clearDisplay();
      display.setTextSize(1); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.setCursor(0, 1);  display.print(score);
      display.setCursor(50, 1); display.print(F("Lv")); display.print(level);
      display.setCursor(96, 1); display.print(F("L:")); display.print(lives);
      display.drawFastHLine(0, 9, 128, SSD1306_WHITE);
      for (uint8_t r = 0; r < 4; r++)
        for (uint8_t c = 0; c < 10; c++)
          if (bricks[r][c]) display.fillRect(4 + c * 12, 12 + r * 5, 11, 4, SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.fillRect((int)px, 60, PW, 3, SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.fillRect((int)bx, (int)by, 2, 2, SSD1306_WHITE);
      if (!launched && !dead) {
        drawHint(-1, 40, isEnglish ? "{1}: launch" : "{1}: lanzar", SSD1306_WHITE);
      }
      flushScreen();
    }

    if (quit) break;
    saveBest("breakout", best); if (!gameOverScreen(score, best, "Breakout")) quit = true;
  }
  settle();
}

// ===================== FLAPPY (saltos más bajos) =====================
void runFlappy() {
  static uint16_t best = 0; static bool bestLd = false;
  if (!bestLd) { best = loadBest("flappy"); bestLd = true; }
  const uint8_t PIPEW = 10, GAP = 27, BX = 28, BW = 7, BH = 5;
  randomSeed(micros());
  settle();

  bool quit = false;
  while (!quit) {
    float by = 28, vy = 0;
    float px[3]; uint8_t gy[3]; bool passed[3];
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      px[i] = 128 + i * 52; gy[i] = random(10, 33); passed[i] = false;
    }
    uint16_t score = 0;
    uint32_t frames = 0;
    bool started = false, dead = false, prev = true;
    unsigned long last = millis();

    while (!dead && !quit) {
      if (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == LOW) {
        if (confirmExit("Flappy Bird", "Flappy Bird")) { quit = true; break; }
        last = millis();
      }
      if (millis() - last < 30) { delay(1); continue; }
      last = millis();
      frames++;

      bool flap = (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) || (digitalRead(PIN_3) == LOW) || (getJoyY() > 700);
      bool edge = flap && !prev;
      prev = flap;

      if (!started) {
        by = 28 + sin(millis() / 200.0) * 3;
        if (edge) { started = true; vy = -2.15; }
      } else {
        if (edge) vy = -2.15;
        vy += 0.19; if (vy > 3.8) vy = 3.8;
        by += vy;

        for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
          px[i] -= 1.45;
          if (px[i] < -PIPEW - 2) {
            float farX = px[0];
            for (uint8_t j = 1; j < 3; j++) if (px[j] > farX) farX = px[j];
            px[i] = farX + 52; gy[i] = random(10, 33); passed[i] = false;
          }
          if (!passed[i] && px[i] + PIPEW < BX) {
            passed[i] = true; score++;
            if (score > best) best = score;
          }
          if (BX + BW - 1 > px[i] && BX + 1 < px[i] + PIPEW && 
              (by + 1 < gy[i] || by + BH - 1 > gy[i] + GAP)) {
            dead = true;
          }
        }
        if (by < 0 || by + BH >= 62) dead = true;
      }

      display.clearDisplay();
      for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        int x = (int)px[i];
        if (x > 127 || x < -PIPEW - 2) continue;
        display.drawRect(x, -1, PIPEW, gy[i] + 1, SSD1306_WHITE);
        display.drawRect(x - 1, gy[i] - 4, PIPEW + 2, 4, SSD1306_WHITE);
        display.drawRect(x, gy[i] + GAP, PIPEW, 64 - gy[i] - GAP, SSD1306_WHITE);
        display.drawRect(x - 1, gy[i] + GAP, PIPEW + 2, 4, SSD1306_WHITE);
      }
      display.drawFastHLine(0, 62, 128, SSD1306_WHITE);
      int off = (frames * 2) % 8;
      for (int x = -off; x < 128; x += 8) display.drawFastHLine(x, 63, 4, SSD1306_WHITE);

      int iy = (int)by;
      display.fillRect(BX, iy, BW, BH, SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.drawPixel(BX + 5, iy + 1, SSD1306_BLACK);
      display.drawPixel(BX + 2, iy + 2, SSD1306_BLACK);
      display.fillRect(BX + BW, iy + 2, 2, 1, SSD1306_WHITE);

      display.setTextSize(1); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE, SSD1306_BLACK);
      display.setCursor(1, 1);
      display.print(score);
      display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
      if (!started) {
        drawHint(-1, 46, isEnglish ? "{1}: flap!" : "{1}: aletear", SSD1306_WHITE);
      }
      flushScreen();
    }

    if (quit) break;
    saveBest("flappy", best); if (!gameOverScreen(score, best, "Flappy Bird")) quit = true;
  }
  settle();
}

// ===================== MORTAL KOMBAT: MOTOR DE SPRITES =====================
// Cada luchador se arma en una mascara de 27x24 (1 = blanco, 2 = detalle negro).
// Mira a la derecha; se voltea al dibujar. El clon de Noob se dibuja solo con el borde.
static uint8_t mkM[MK_CH][MK_CW];

static void mkPut(int x, int y, uint8_t v) {
  x += 8;                                   // x local: 0..10 = cuerpo
  if (x < 0 || x >= MK_CW || y < 0 || y >= MK_CH) return;
  mkM[y][x] = v;
}
static void mkBox(int x, int y, int w, int h, uint8_t v) {
  for (int j = 0; j < h; j++) for (int i = 0; i < w; i++) mkPut(x + i, y + j, v);
}
static void mkLn(int x0, int y0, int x1, int y1, uint8_t v) {
  int dx = abs(x1 - x0), sx = x0 < x1 ? 1 : -1;
  int dy = -abs(y1 - y0), sy = y0 < y1 ? 1 : -1;
  int err = dx + dy;
  while (true) {
    mkPut(x0, y0, v);
    if (x0 == x1 && y0 == y1) break;
    int e2 = 2 * err;
    if (e2 >= dy) { err += dy; x0 += sx; }
    if (e2 <= dx) { err += dx; y0 += sy; }
  }
}
static void mkTh(int x0, int y0, int x1, int y1, uint8_t v) {   // linea de 2 px de grosor
  mkLn(x0, y0, x1, y1, v);
  mkLn(x0 + 1, y0, x1 + 1, y1, v);
}

// ---------- Piernas ----------
static void mkLegs(uint8_t pose, uint8_t fr) {
  if (pose == PO_FALL) {                    // piernas abiertas (tumbo en el portal)
    mkTh(3, 17, 0, 23, 1); mkTh(6, 17, 9, 23, 1);
    return;
  }
  if (pose == PO_KICK || pose == PO_AIRKICK) {
    mkBox(2, 17, 2, 6, 1); mkBox(2, 23, 3, 1, 1);               // pierna de apoyo
    bool ext = (pose == PO_KICK) ? (fr == 1) : (fr == 0);
    if (pose == PO_AIRKICK && fr == 1) {                         // patada baja
      mkTh(6, 17, 12, 21, 1); mkBox(12, 21, 3, 2, 1);
    } else if (ext) {                                            // patada extendida
      mkBox(6, 16, 9, 2, 1); mkBox(14, 14, 2, 5, 1);
    } else {                                                     // rodilla arriba
      mkBox(6, 15, 3, 2, 1); mkBox(8, 15, 2, 6, 1); mkBox(8, 21, 3, 1, 1);
    }
    return;
  }
  if (pose == PO_WALK && fr == 1) {                              // zancada larga
    mkTh(3, 17, 1, 23, 1); mkBox(0, 23, 3, 1, 1);
    mkTh(6, 17, 8, 23, 1); mkBox(7, 23, 3, 1, 1);
    return;
  }
  if (pose == PO_WALK && fr == 3) {                              // piernas juntas
    mkBox(3, 17, 2, 6, 1); mkBox(3, 23, 2, 1, 1);
    mkBox(6, 17, 2, 6, 1); mkBox(6, 23, 2, 1, 1);
    return;
  }
  if (pose == PO_HIT) {                                          // echado hacia atras
    mkBox(1, 17, 2, 6, 1); mkBox(1, 23, 3, 1, 1);
    mkBox(5, 17, 2, 6, 1); mkBox(5, 23, 3, 1, 1);
    return;
  }
  mkBox(2, 17, 2, 6, 1); mkBox(2, 23, 3, 1, 1);                  // postura base
  mkBox(6, 17, 2, 6, 1); mkBox(6, 23, 3, 1, 1);
}

// ---------- Torso (con el detalle de cada personaje) ----------
static void mkTorsoF(uint8_t id, int L) {
  mkBox(3 + L, 10, 5, 2, 1);                    // hombros finos
  mkBox(4 + L, 12, 3, 2, 1);                    // cintura marcada
  mkBox(3 + L, 14, 5, 1, 1);
  mkBox(2 + L, 15, 7, 2, 1);                    // caderas anchas
  if (id == 8) {            // KITANA: leotardo con escote en V, fajin y cadera
    mkPut(5 + L, 10, 2); mkPut(4 + L, 11, 2); mkPut(6 + L, 11, 2);
    mkBox(4 + L, 13, 3, 1, 2);
    mkBox(2 + L, 15, 7, 1, 2); mkPut(5 + L, 16, 2);
  } else {                  // SONYA: top corto oscuro con placa, abdomen al aire, cinturon militar
    mkBox(3 + L, 10, 5, 2, 2); mkPut(5 + L, 11, 1);
    mkPut(5 + L, 13, 2);
    mkBox(2 + L, 15, 7, 1, 2); mkPut(5 + L, 15, 1);
  }
}

static void mkTorso(uint8_t id, int L) {
  if (id == 8 || id == 9) { mkTorsoF(id, L); return; }
  mkBox(2 + L, 10, 7, 7, 1);
  switch (id) {
    case 0:   // SCORPION: banda cruzada gruesa, cinturon con calavera, cuerda de kunai
      mkLn(3 + L, 10, 7 + L, 14, 2); mkLn(3 + L, 11, 7 + L, 15, 2); mkLn(4 + L, 10, 8 + L, 14, 2);
      mkBox(2 + L, 16, 7, 1, 2); mkPut(5 + L, 16, 1);
      mkPut(1 + L, 15, 1); mkPut(0 + L, 16, 1); mkPut(0 + L, 17, 1); mkPut(1 + L, 18, 1);
      break;
    case 1:   // SUB-ZERO: hombreras anchas con picos de hielo, copo en el pecho
      mkBox(0 + L, 10, 11, 2, 1); mkPut(0 + L, 9, 1); mkPut(10 + L, 9, 1); mkPut(0 + L, 8, 1); mkPut(10 + L, 8, 1);
      mkBox(5 + L, 12, 1, 3, 2); mkBox(4 + L, 13, 3, 1, 2);
      mkBox(2 + L, 16, 7, 1, 2); mkBox(0 + L, 11, 1, 1, 2); mkBox(10 + L, 11, 1, 1, 2);
      break;
    case 2:   // LIU KANG: pecho descubierto (pectorales y abdomen), faja de pantalon
      mkBox(3 + L, 12, 2, 1, 2); mkBox(6 + L, 12, 2, 1, 2);
      mkBox(4 + L, 14, 3, 1, 2);
      mkBox(2 + L, 16, 7, 1, 2);
      break;
    case 3:   // KUNG LAO: chaleco abierto en V, faja
      mkLn(3 + L, 10, 5 + L, 14, 2); mkLn(7 + L, 10, 5 + L, 14, 2);
      mkBox(2 + L, 16, 7, 1, 2); mkPut(5 + L, 15, 2);
      break;
    case 4:   // RAIDEN: tunica con rayo, hombreras
      mkBox(0 + L, 10, 11, 1, 1);
      mkLn(6 + L, 11, 4 + L, 13, 2); mkLn(4 + L, 13, 6 + L, 13, 2); mkLn(6 + L, 13, 4 + L, 16, 2);
      mkBox(2 + L, 16, 7, 1, 2);
      break;
    case 5:   // NOOB: arnes en X y bufanda
      mkLn(3 + L, 10, 7 + L, 15, 2); mkLn(7 + L, 10, 3 + L, 15, 2);
      mkBox(2 + L, 16, 7, 1, 2);
      mkPut(2 + L, 9, 1); mkPut(1 + L, 10, 1); mkPut(0 + L, 10, 1); mkPut(-1 + L, 11, 1);
      break;
    case 6:   // TERMINATOR: caja toracica metalica y hombros enormes
      mkBox(0 + L, 10, 11, 2, 1);
      mkBox(3 + L, 12, 5, 1, 2); mkBox(3 + L, 14, 5, 1, 2);
      mkBox(2 + L, 16, 7, 1, 2); mkPut(5 + L, 12, 1); mkPut(5 + L, 14, 1);
      break;
    case 7:   // KANO: chaleco abierto, pectorales y cinturon ancho
      mkBox(2 + L, 11, 1, 5, 2); mkBox(8 + L, 11, 1, 5, 2);
      mkBox(3 + L, 12, 2, 1, 2); mkBox(6 + L, 12, 2, 1, 2); mkBox(4 + L, 14, 3, 1, 2);
      mkBox(2 + L, 16, 7, 1, 2); mkPut(5 + L, 16, 1);
      break;
    case 10:  // JOHNNY CAGE: torso desnudo de estrella de cine, pectorales, abdomen, cinturon con hebilla
      mkBox(3 + L, 12, 2, 1, 2); mkBox(6 + L, 12, 2, 1, 2);
      mkBox(5 + L, 11, 1, 5, 2); mkBox(3 + L, 14, 5, 1, 2);
      mkBox(2 + L, 16, 7, 1, 2); mkBox(4 + L, 16, 3, 1, 1);
      mkPut(2 + L, 10, 2); mkPut(8 + L, 10, 2);
      break;
    case 11:  // JAX: hombros enormes, pectorales y cinturon ancho
      mkBox(0 + L, 10, 11, 2, 1); mkPut(0 + L, 9, 1); mkPut(10 + L, 9, 1);
      mkBox(3 + L, 12, 2, 1, 2); mkBox(6 + L, 12, 2, 1, 2); mkBox(5 + L, 13, 1, 3, 2);
      mkBox(2 + L, 16, 7, 1, 2); mkPut(5 + L, 16, 1);
      mkPut(1 + L, 11, 2); mkPut(9 + L, 11, 2);
      break;
    case 12:  // REPTILE: escamas en el pecho y cinturon
      for (int yy = 11; yy <= 15; yy++) for (int xx = 3; xx <= 7; xx++) if (((xx + yy) & 1) == 0) mkPut(xx + L, yy, 2);
      mkBox(2 + L, 16, 7, 1, 2); mkPut(5 + L, 16, 1);
      break;
    case 13:  // BARAKA: pecho de guerrero, costillas y hombros con pinchos
      mkPut(0 + L, 9, 1); mkPut(10 + L, 9, 1); mkBox(0 + L, 10, 11, 1, 1);
      mkBox(3 + L, 12, 2, 1, 2); mkBox(6 + L, 12, 2, 1, 2);
      mkBox(4 + L, 14, 3, 1, 2); mkPut(5 + L, 13, 2);
      mkBox(2 + L, 16, 7, 1, 2);
      break;
    case 14:  // SHANG TSUNG: tunica con cuello alto, medallon y hombreras
      mkBox(0 + L, 10, 11, 1, 1);
      mkLn(3 + L, 11, 5 + L, 14, 2); mkLn(7 + L, 11, 5 + L, 14, 2);
      mkPut(5 + L, 15, 1); mkBox(2 + L, 16, 7, 1, 2);
      break;
  }
}

// ---------- Cabeza (7x6, filas 4..9) ----------
static void mkHeadF(uint8_t id, int L) {
  mkBox(2 + L, 4, 7, 6, 1);                      // rostro
  mkPut(2 + L, 9, 0); mkPut(8 + L, 9, 0);         // barbilla mas fina
  if (id == 8) {            // KITANA: coronilla, cabello negro largo, mascara en la boca
    mkPut(3 + L, 3, 1); mkPut(5 + L, 2, 1); mkPut(5 + L, 3, 1); mkPut(7 + L, 3, 1);
    mkBox(2 + L, 4, 7, 1, 2); mkPut(2 + L, 5, 2); mkPut(8 + L, 5, 2);
    mkBox(0 + L, 4, 2, 9, 1); mkPut(1 + L, 7, 2); mkPut(1 + L, 10, 2);   // melena larga detras
    mkPut(5 + L, 6, 2); mkPut(7 + L, 6, 2);
    mkBox(4 + L, 8, 5, 2, 2);
  } else {                  // SONYA: coleta alta rubia, cinta, labios
    mkBox(3 + L, 3, 5, 1, 1);
    mkBox(2 + L, 5, 7, 1, 2);
    mkBox(0 + L, 3, 2, 2, 1); mkBox(-1 + L, 5, 2, 3, 1); mkBox(-1 + L, 8, 2, 3, 1); mkPut(0 + L, 11, 1);   // cola de caballo
    mkPut(5 + L, 7, 2); mkPut(7 + L, 7, 2);
    mkBox(6 + L, 9, 2, 1, 2);
  }
}

static void mkHead(uint8_t id, int L) {
  if (id == 8 || id == 9) { mkHeadF(id, L); return; }
  mkBox(2 + L, 4, 7, 6, 1);
  switch (id) {
    case 0:   // SCORPION: capucha con antifaz, solo se ven los ojos
      mkBox(2 + L, 6, 7, 2, 2); mkPut(4 + L, 6, 1); mkPut(6 + L, 6, 1);
      mkBox(2 + L, 9, 7, 1, 2);
      break;
    case 1:   // SUB-ZERO: capucha oscura, ojos de hielo y mascara
      mkBox(2 + L, 4, 7, 1, 2); mkPut(4 + L, 6, 2); mkPut(6 + L, 6, 2);
      mkBox(2 + L, 8, 7, 2, 2); mkPut(5 + L, 8, 1);
      break;
    case 2:   // LIU KANG: pelo negro, cinta y cola
      mkBox(2 + L, 5, 7, 1, 2); mkBox(2 + L, 6, 7, 1, 2);
      mkPut(4 + L, 7, 2); mkPut(6 + L, 7, 2);
      mkPut(1 + L, 6, 1); mkPut(0 + L, 7, 1); mkPut(0 + L, 8, 1); mkPut(-1 + L, 8, 1);
      break;
    case 3:   // KUNG LAO: ojos y bigote
      mkPut(4 + L, 7, 2); mkPut(6 + L, 7, 2); mkBox(4 + L, 9, 4, 1, 2);
      break;
    case 4:   // RAIDEN: rostro en sombra con ojos brillantes y barba
      mkBox(3 + L, 5, 5, 4, 2); mkPut(4 + L, 6, 1); mkPut(6 + L, 6, 1);
      break;
    case 5:   // NOOB: capucha puntiaguda, rostro negro con ojos blancos
      mkBox(4 + L, 3, 3, 1, 1); mkPut(5 + L, 2, 1);
      mkBox(3 + L, 5, 5, 4, 2); mkPut(4 + L, 6, 1); mkPut(6 + L, 6, 1);
      break;
    case 6:   // TERMINATOR: craneo metalico, cuencas y dientes
      mkBox(3 + L, 6, 2, 2, 2); mkBox(6 + L, 6, 2, 2, 2); mkPut(6 + L, 7, 1);
      mkPut(5 + L, 8, 2);
      mkPut(2 + L, 9, 2); mkPut(4 + L, 9, 2); mkPut(6 + L, 9, 2); mkPut(8 + L, 9, 2);
      break;
    case 7:   // KANO: pelo negro peinado atras, placa cibernetica en el ojo y barba
      mkBox(2 + L, 4, 7, 2, 2);
      mkBox(3 + L, 6, 3, 2, 2); mkPut(4 + L, 6, 1); mkPut(7 + L, 7, 2);
      mkBox(3 + L, 9, 5, 1, 2);
      break;
    case 10:  // JOHNNY CAGE: pelo de punta, gafas de sol y sonrisa
      mkPut(3 + L, 2, 1); mkPut(5 + L, 2, 1); mkPut(7 + L, 2, 1); mkBox(2 + L, 3, 7, 1, 1);
      mkBox(3 + L, 6, 5, 2, 2); mkPut(5 + L, 6, 1);
      mkBox(4 + L, 9, 4, 1, 2);
      break;
    case 11:  // JAX: pelo corto, cinta negra y barba
      mkBox(2 + L, 3, 7, 1, 1); mkBox(2 + L, 5, 7, 1, 2);
      mkPut(4 + L, 7, 2); mkPut(6 + L, 7, 2);
      mkBox(3 + L, 9, 5, 1, 2); mkPut(5 + L, 8, 2);
      break;
    case 12:  // REPTILE: cresta de reptil, ojos rasgados y mascara
      mkPut(3 + L, 3, 1); mkPut(5 + L, 2, 1); mkPut(7 + L, 3, 1);
      mkBox(3 + L, 5, 5, 1, 2); mkBox(3 + L, 7, 5, 1, 2);
      mkBox(3 + L, 8, 5, 2, 2); mkPut(4 + L, 9, 1); mkPut(6 + L, 9, 1);
      break;
    case 13:  // BARAKA: cresta de pinchos, ceja pesada, colmillos
      mkPut(3 + L, 3, 1); mkPut(5 + L, 2, 1); mkPut(7 + L, 3, 1); mkPut(5 + L, 3, 1);
      mkBox(3 + L, 5, 5, 1, 2); mkPut(4 + L, 6, 2); mkPut(6 + L, 6, 2);
      mkBox(3 + L, 8, 5, 1, 2); mkPut(3 + L, 8, 1); mkPut(5 + L, 8, 1); mkPut(7 + L, 8, 1);
      mkPut(2 + L, 10, 1); mkPut(8 + L, 10, 1);
      break;
    case 14:  // SHANG TSUNG: pelo recogido con coleta, perilla
      mkBox(3 + L, 4, 5, 1, 2); mkBox(0 + L, 3, 2, 2, 1); mkBox(-1 + L, 5, 2, 4, 1);
      mkPut(4 + L, 6, 2); mkPut(6 + L, 6, 2);
      mkBox(4 + L, 9, 3, 1, 2); mkPut(5 + L, 10, 2);
      break;
  }
}

// ---------- Sombrero / pelo (filas 0..4) ----------
static void mkHat(uint8_t id, bool hat, int L) {
  if (id == 3) {
    if (hat) {                                // sombrero con punta y aro metalico
      mkPut(5 + L, 0, 1); mkBox(4 + L, 1, 3, 1, 1); mkBox(3 + L, 2, 5, 1, 1);
      mkBox(1 + L, 3, 9, 1, 1); mkBox(-1 + L, 4, 13, 1, 1);
      mkPut(1 + L, 4, 2); mkPut(5 + L, 4, 2); mkPut(9 + L, 4, 2); mkPut(5 + L, 2, 2);
    } else {                                  // mono
      mkPut(5 + L, 2, 1); mkBox(4 + L, 3, 3, 1, 1);
    }
  } else if (id == 4) {
    if (hat) {                                // sombrero de paja grande con rayo
      mkBox(4 + L, 0, 3, 1, 1); mkBox(3 + L, 1, 5, 1, 1); mkBox(1 + L, 2, 9, 1, 1);
      mkBox(0 + L, 3, 11, 1, 1); mkBox(-2 + L, 4, 15, 1, 1);
      mkPut(6 + L, 1, 2); mkPut(5 + L, 2, 2); mkPut(6 + L, 2, 2); mkPut(5 + L, 3, 2);
    } else {                                  // pelo canoso
      mkBox(4 + L, 2, 3, 1, 1); mkBox(3 + L, 3, 5, 1, 1);
    }
  }
}

// ---------- Guardia: una animacion distinta por luchador ----------
// fr 0 = levantando la guardia, fr 1 = guardia completa
static void mkBlockArms(uint8_t id, uint8_t fr, int L) {
  if (fr == 0) {                                             // subiendo los brazos
    mkBox(8 + L, 9, 2, 6, 1); mkBox(1 + L, 12, 2, 5, 1);
    return;
  }
  switch (id) {
    case 0:   // SCORPION: brazos cruzados en X sobre el pecho
      mkTh(7 + L, 10, 10 + L, 16, 1); mkTh(7 + L, 16, 10 + L, 10, 1);
      mkPut(9 + L, 13, 2);
      break;
    case 1:   // SUB-ZERO: escudo de hielo (diamante) delante
      mkBox(8 + L, 8, 2, 7, 1);
      mkLn(12 + L, 5, 15 + L, 10, 1); mkLn(15 + L, 10, 12 + L, 16, 1);
      mkLn(12 + L, 16, 10 + L, 10, 1); mkLn(10 + L, 10, 12 + L, 5, 1);
      mkPut(12 + L, 8, 1); mkPut(13 + L, 11, 1);
      break;
    case 2:   // LIU KANG: guardia baja de kung fu, un brazo arriba y otro cruzando la cintura
      mkBox(8 + L, 5, 2, 8, 1); mkBox(8 + L, 4, 3, 2, 1);
      mkBox(4 + L, 14, 7, 2, 1);
      break;
    case 3:   // KUNG LAO: postura de grulla, palma abierta delante y otro brazo en alto detras
      mkBox(8 + L, 6, 2, 8, 1); mkPut(10 + L, 4, 1); mkPut(11 + L, 5, 1); mkPut(11 + L, 7, 1); mkBox(10 + L, 5, 1, 3, 1);
      mkTh(2 + L, 11, 0 + L, 5, 1); mkPut(-1 + L, 4, 1);
      break;
    case 4:   // RAIDEN: barrera de rayos
      mkBox(8 + L, 8, 2, 6, 1);
      mkLn(12 + L, 4, 14 + L, 8, 1); mkLn(14 + L, 8, 12 + L, 11, 1);
      mkLn(12 + L, 11, 14 + L, 15, 1); mkLn(14 + L, 15, 12 + L, 17, 1);
      break;
    case 5:   // NOOB: brazos en X y estela de sombra detras
      mkTh(7 + L, 10, 10 + L, 16, 1); mkTh(7 + L, 16, 10 + L, 10, 1);
      mkPut(-1 + L, 10, 1); mkPut(-1 + L, 12, 1); mkPut(-1 + L, 14, 1);
      mkPut(-3 + L, 11, 1); mkPut(-3 + L, 13, 1);
      break;
    case 8:   // KITANA: abanico de acero abierto delante
      mkBox(8 + L, 8, 2, 6, 1);
      mkLn(11 + L, 12, 11 + L, 4, 1); mkLn(11 + L, 12, 14 + L, 5, 1); mkLn(11 + L, 12, 15 + L, 8, 1); mkLn(11 + L, 12, 15 + L, 11, 1);
      mkLn(11 + L, 4, 14 + L, 5, 1); mkLn(14 + L, 5, 15 + L, 8, 1); mkLn(15 + L, 8, 15 + L, 11, 1);
      mkPut(12 + L, 8, 2); mkPut(13 + L, 9, 2);
      break;
    case 9:   // SONYA: guardia de boxeo, punos delante de la cara
      mkBox(8 + L, 6, 3, 3, 1); mkBox(7 + L, 9, 2, 5, 1); mkBox(11 + L, 7, 2, 2, 1);
      mkBox(5 + L, 12, 3, 3, 1); mkPut(9 + L, 7, 2);
      break;
    case 10:  // JOHNNY CAGE: palma abierta delante, pose de pelicula
      mkBox(8 + L, 3, 3, 3, 1); mkBox(8 + L, 6, 2, 7, 1); mkPut(9 + L, 8, 2);
      mkBox(2 + L, 12, 2, 4, 1);
      break;
    case 11:  // JAX: antebrazos metalicos cruzados con remaches
      mkBox(8 + L, 5, 5, 5, 1); mkBox(8 + L, 10, 3, 6, 1);
      mkPut(10 + L, 7, 2); mkPut(9 + L, 12, 2); mkPut(9 + L, 14, 2);
      break;
    case 12:  // REPTILE: brazos cruzados en X
      mkTh(7 + L, 10, 10 + L, 16, 1); mkTh(7 + L, 16, 10 + L, 10, 1);
      mkPut(9 + L, 13, 2); mkPut(11 + L, 9, 1);
      break;
    case 13:  // BARAKA: cuchillas de los antebrazos cruzadas delante
      mkBox(8 + L, 9, 2, 7, 1);
      mkLn(10 + L, 8, 16 + L, 2, 1); mkLn(10 + L, 9, 16 + L, 3, 1);
      mkLn(10 + L, 13, 16 + L, 13, 1); mkLn(10 + L, 14, 16 + L, 14, 1);
      break;
    case 14:  // SHANG TSUNG: manos juntas con llama de hechicero
      mkBox(8 + L, 6, 2, 8, 1); mkBox(10 + L, 7, 3, 2, 1);
      mkPut(13 + L, 6, 1); mkPut(12 + L, 4, 1); mkPut(13 + L, 5, 1); mkPut(14 + L, 3, 1);
      break;
    case 7:   // KANO: se cubre la cara con los antebrazos cruzados
      mkBox(8 + L, 3, 2, 9, 1); mkBox(10 + L, 5, 2, 9, 1); mkPut(9 + L, 2, 1);
      mkPut(9 + L, 8, 2); mkPut(11 + L, 10, 2);
      break;
    default:  // TERMINATOR: placa de blindaje en el antebrazo
      mkBox(8 + L, 6, 4, 10, 1);
      mkBox(9 + L, 8, 2, 1, 2); mkBox(9 + L, 11, 2, 1, 2); mkBox(9 + L, 14, 2, 1, 2);
      break;
  }
}

// ---------- Brazos (hombro en fila 11) ----------
static void mkArms(uint8_t id, uint8_t pose, uint8_t fr, int L) {
  switch (pose) {
    case PO_PUNCH:
      if (fr == 0) { mkBox(7 + L, 11, 3, 2, 1); mkBox(1 + L, 12, 2, 5, 1); }
      else if (fr == 1) { mkBox(8 + L, 11, 5, 2, 1); mkBox(12 + L, 10, 3, 3, 1); mkBox(1 + L, 12, 2, 5, 1); }
      else { mkBox(8 + L, 11, 3, 2, 1); mkBox(10 + L, 10, 2, 3, 1); mkBox(1 + L, 12, 2, 5, 1); }
      break;
    case PO_KICK: case PO_AIRKICK:
      mkTh(8 + L, 12, 10 + L, 9, 1); mkTh(1 + L, 12, -1 + L, 14, 1);
      break;
    case PO_BLOCK:
      mkBlockArms(id, fr, L);
      break;
    case PO_HIT:
      mkTh(2 + L, 11, -1 + L, 8, 1); mkTh(8 + L, 12, 10 + L, 15, 1);
      break;
    case PO_WIN:
      mkBox(9 + L, 0, 2, 11, 1); mkBox(0 + L, 1, 2, 10, 1);
      break;
    case PO_CAST:
      mkBox(8 + L, 11, 5, 2, 1); mkBox(12 + L, 10, 3, 3, 1);
      mkBox(7 + L, 14, 4, 2, 1); mkBox(1 + L, 12, 2, 5, 1);
      break;
    case PO_FALL:
      mkTh(2 + L, 11, -1 + L, 4, 1); mkTh(8 + L, 11, 10 + L, 4, 1);
      break;
    default:                                  // guardia
      mkTh(8 + L, 12, 10 + L, 10, 1); mkBox(10 + L, 9, 2, 2, 1);
      mkBox(1 + L, 12, 2, 5, 1);
      break;
  }
}

// ---------- Derribado (KO) ----------
static void mkBuildKO(uint8_t id, bool hat) {
  mkBox(-5, 19, 5, 5, 1);                      // cabeza
  mkBox(0, 20, 7, 4, 1);                       // torso
  mkBox(7, 21, 8, 3, 1);                       // piernas
  mkBox(2, 18, 5, 2, 1);                       // brazo
  mkPut(-4, 21, 2); mkPut(-2, 21, 2);          // ojos en X
  mkPut(-3, 22, 2);
  mkBox(1, 22, 5, 1, 2);                       // cinturon
  mkBox(13, 22, 2, 1, 2);
  if (id == 3 && hat) { mkBox(-8, 22, 3, 1, 1); mkBox(-9, 23, 5, 1, 1); }
  if (id == 4 && hat) { mkBox(-8, 22, 3, 1, 1); mkBox(-10, 23, 7, 1, 1); }
  if (id == 5) { mkPut(-6, 20, 1); mkPut(-7, 21, 1); }
}

// Jax: punos y antebrazos metalicos gigantes
static void mkJaxFists(uint8_t pose, uint8_t fr, int L) {
  switch (pose) {
    case PO_PUNCH:
      if (fr == 1) { mkBox(13 + L, 9, 4, 5, 1); mkPut(14 + L, 11, 2); }
      else if (fr == 2) mkBox(11 + L, 9, 3, 4, 1);
      else mkBox(9 + L, 10, 3, 3, 1);
      break;
    case PO_CAST:
      mkBox(14 + L, 9, 4, 5, 1); mkPut(15 + L, 11, 2);
      break;
    case PO_IDLE: case PO_WALK:
      mkBox(10 + L, 8, 4, 4, 1); mkPut(11 + L, 10, 2);
      break;
    default: break;
  }
}

// Baraka: cuchillas largas en los antebrazos
static void mkBarakaBlades(uint8_t pose, uint8_t fr, int L) {
  switch (pose) {
    case PO_PUNCH:
      if (fr == 1) { mkLn(15 + L, 10, 21 + L, 8, 1); mkLn(15 + L, 11, 21 + L, 9, 1); }
      else if (fr == 2) mkLn(12 + L, 10, 16 + L, 8, 1);
      break;
    case PO_CAST:
      mkLn(15 + L, 10, 21 + L, 6, 1); mkLn(15 + L, 11, 21 + L, 7, 1);
      mkLn(11 + L, 15, 17 + L, 17, 1);
      break;
    case PO_IDLE: case PO_WALK:
      mkLn(11 + L, 9, 15 + L, 4, 1); mkLn(11 + L, 10, 15 + L, 5, 1);
      break;
    default: break;
  }
}

static void mkBuild(uint8_t id, uint8_t pose, uint8_t fr, bool hat) {
  memset(mkM, 0, sizeof(mkM));
  if (pose == PO_KO) { mkBuildKO(id, hat); return; }
  int L = 0;
  if (pose == PO_HIT) L = -2;
  else if (pose == PO_PUNCH && fr == 1) L = 1;
  else if (pose == PO_KICK || pose == PO_AIRKICK) L = -1;
  else if (pose == PO_BLOCK && fr == 1) L = -1;
  mkLegs(pose, fr);
  mkTorso(id, L);
  mkHead(id, L);
  mkHat(id, hat, L);
  mkArms(id, pose, fr, L);
  if (id == 11) mkJaxFists(pose, fr, L);
  if (id == 13) mkBarakaBlades(pose, fr, L);
  if (pose == PO_IDLE && (fr == 1 || fr == 2)) {              // respiracion: el torso baja 1 px
    for (int y = 16; y >= 1; y--) memcpy(mkM[y], mkM[y - 1], MK_CW);
    memset(mkM[0], 0, MK_CW);
  }
}

static uint8_t mkAt(int x, int y) {
  if (x < 0 || x >= MK_CW || y < 0 || y >= MK_CH) return 0;
  return mkM[y][x];
}

static inline int mkSX(int cx, bool right, int x) { return cx - MK_CX + (right ? x : (MK_CW - 1 - x)); }

// Dibuja la mascara en pantalla. mode 0 = normal, mode 1 = solo borde (clon de Noob)
static void mkBlit(int cx, int top, bool right, uint8_t mode, int clipT, int clipB, bool flipY) {
  for (int y = 0; y < MK_CH; y++) {
    int sy = flipY ? top + (MK_CH - 1 - y) : top + y;
    if (sy < clipT || sy >= clipB || sy < 0 || sy >= 64) continue;
    for (int x = 0; x < MK_CW; x++) {
      int sx = cx - MK_CX + (right ? x : (MK_CW - 1 - x));
      if (sx < 0 || sx >= 128) continue;
      uint8_t v = mkM[y][x];
      if (v) {
        if (mode == 1) {
          bool edge = !mkAt(x - 1, y) || !mkAt(x + 1, y) || !mkAt(x, y - 1) || !mkAt(x, y + 1);
          display.drawPixel(sx, sy, edge ? SSD1306_WHITE : SSD1306_BLACK);
        } else {
          display.drawPixel(sx, sy, v == 1 ? SSD1306_WHITE : SSD1306_BLACK);
        }
      } else if (mkAt(x - 1, y) || mkAt(x + 1, y) || mkAt(x, y - 1) || mkAt(x, y + 1)) {
        display.drawPixel(sx, sy, SSD1306_BLACK);          // halo negro para separar del fondo
      }
    }
  }
}

// ===================== MORTAL KOMBAT: JUEGO =====================
#define MKS_MENU 0
#define MKS_SELECT 1
#define MKS_INTRO 2
#define MKS_FIGHT 3
#define MKS_FINISH 4
#define MKS_KO 5
#define MKS_ROUND 6
#define MKS_WIN 7
#define MKS_LOSE 8
#define MKS_DIFF 9
#define MKS_FATAL 10
#define MKS_FATWIN 11
#define MK_NEED 2          // rondas para ganar
#define MK_NUM 15           // luchadores
#define MK_CD_FRAMES 450   // 15 s de espera del especial (30 ms por cuadro)

static const char* const mkNames[MK_NUM] = {"SCORPION", "SUB-ZERO", "LIU KANG", "KUNG LAO", "RAIDEN", "NOOB", "TERMINATOR", "KANO", "KITANA", "SONYA", "JOHNNY CAGE", "JAX", "REPTILE", "BARAKA", "SHANG TSUNG"};
static const char* const mkSpName[MK_NUM] = {"SPEAR", "ICE BALL", "HEAD KICKS", "HAT TOSS", "LIGHTNING", "SHADOW", "SHOTGUN", "BALL ROLL", "FAN TOSS", "RINGS", "DASH KICK", "QUAKE", "ACID SPIT", "BLADES", "SKULLS"};
static const uint8_t mkSpType[MK_NUM] = {1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 6, 3, 8, 5, 9, 10, 11, 12, 13, 14};       // 1 lanza 2 hielo 3 escopeta 4 patadas 5 sombrero 6 sombra 7 rayos 8 bala de canon 9 anillos 10 patada veloz 11 terremoto 12 acido 13 cuchillas 14 calaveras
static const uint8_t mkHpTab[MK_NUM] = {100, 115, 95, 100, 110, 105, 130, 105, 95, 105, 100, 125, 100, 120, 100};
static const uint8_t mkSpdTab[MK_NUM] = {20, 17, 24, 20, 18, 20, 16, 21, 23, 21, 22, 17, 22, 19, 20};  // px por cuadro x10

// Dificultad: 0 FACIL (la de antes), 1 NORMAL, 2 DIFICIL, 3 TRYHARD
static const char* const mkDiffName[4] = {"EASY", "NORMAL", "HARD", "TRYHARD"};
static const uint8_t mkDiffDmg[4] = {10, 12, 15, 18};     // dano del enemigo x10
static const uint8_t mkDiffSpd[4] = {8, 9, 10, 12};       // velocidad del enemigo x10
static const uint8_t mkDiffCd[4]  = {10, 15, 22, 32};     // divisor de espera entre golpes x10
static const uint8_t mkDiffBlk[4] = {7, 12, 20, 35};      // % por cuadro de bloquear un golpe tuyo
static const uint8_t mkDiffRet[4] = {40, 45, 55, 70};     // % de retroceder si lo pegas encima
static const uint8_t mkDiffHp[4]  = {100, 100, 112, 125}; // vida del enemigo en %
static uint8_t mkDiff = 0;
static MKFighter mkF[2];            // 0 = jugador, 1 = enemigo
#define mkP mkF[0]
#define mkE mkF[1]
static uint8_t mkRound = 1, mkPW = 0, mkEW = 0, mkSel = 0, mkSp = 0;
static float mkTimer = 60000;
static int16_t mkSpark = 0, mkSparkX = 0, mkSparkY = 0;
static int16_t mkCD = 0;
static uint8_t mkSpState = 0;
static int16_t mkSpT = 0;
static float mkLkX0 = 0;
static bool mkProjOn = false;
static uint8_t mkProjKind = 0;
static float mkProjX = 0, mkProjDir = 1;
static bool mkHatFly = false, mkHatBack = false, mkHatHit = false;
static float mkHatX = 0, mkHatDir = 1, mkHatX0 = 0;
static uint8_t mkHatFr = 0;
static int16_t mkFrozenT = 0;
static int16_t mkBurn = 0;
static bool mkCloneOn = false, mkCloneRight = true;
static float mkCloneX = 0;
static uint8_t mkClonePose = PO_IDLE, mkCloneWalk = 0;
static bool mkPortals = false;
static float mkPortalX = 0, mkPortalR = 0, mkFallV = 0;
static uint16_t mkFrame = 0;
static int16_t mkBoltX = 64;
static uint8_t mkCmbN = 0, mkCmbShowN = 0;      // combo L-1-L: paso actual / numero mostrado
static int16_t mkCmbT = 0, mkCmbShow = 0;
static uint8_t finCmbN = 0; static int16_t finCmbT = 0; static bool finKPrev = false, finPPrev = false;

// ---------- utilidades ----------
static void mkCenter(const char* s, int y, uint8_t size) {
  int w = (int)strlen(s) * 6 * size;
  display.setTextSize(size);
  display.setCursor((128 - w) / 2, y);
  display.print(s);
  display.setTextSize(1);
}

static void mkWaitRelease(uint8_t pin) {
  unsigned long t0 = millis();
  while (digitalRead(pin) == LOW && millis() - t0 < 1500) delay(5);
}

static void mkClampFighter(uint8_t fi) {
  MKFighter &f = mkF[fi];
  if (f.x < 10) f.x = 10;
  if (f.x > 118) f.x = 118;
}

static void mkInitFighter(uint8_t fi, uint8_t id) {
  MKFighter &f = mkF[fi];
  f.id = id;
  f.maxHp = mkHpTab[id];
  f.spd = mkSpdTab[id] / 10.0f;
}

static void mkCancelSpecial() {
  mkSpState = 0; mkSpT = 0; mkBurn = 0;
  mkProjOn = false; mkHatFly = false; mkFrozenT = 0;
  mkCloneOn = false; mkPortals = false;
  mkP.y = MK_SY; mkE.y = MK_SY; mkP.flip = false; mkE.flip = false;
  mkP.hat = (mkP.id == 3 || mkP.id == 4);
  if (mkP.act == MA_BUSY || mkP.act == MA_CAST) mkP.act = MA_IDLE;
  if (mkE.act == MA_CAPT) mkE.act = MA_IDLE;
}

static void mkInitRound() {
  mkP.x = 30; mkE.x = 98; mkP.right = true; mkE.right = false;
  MKFighter* fs[2] = {&mkP, &mkE};
  for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
    MKFighter &f = *fs[i];
    f.y = MK_SY; f.hp = f.maxHp; f.act = MA_IDLE; f.t = 0; f.cd = 0; f.wf = 0; f.ov = PO_IDLE;
    f.flip = false; f.damaged = false; f.hat = (f.id == 3 || f.id == 4);
  }
  mkCancelSpecial();
  mkSpark = 0; mkCD = 0; mkTimer = 60000;
  mkCmbN = 0; mkCmbT = 0; mkCmbShow = 0;
}

// Dano directo sin reaccion (para ticks de patadas, caida del portal)
static void mkDamageRaw(uint8_t fi, float d) {
  MKFighter &f = mkF[fi];
  f.hp -= d; if (f.hp < 0) f.hp = 0;
  f.damaged = true;
}

static void mkHurt(uint8_t ti, uint8_t si, float dmg, int push, bool unblockable) {
  MKFighter &tgt = mkF[ti], &src = mkF[si];
  if (tgt.act == MA_CAPT || tgt.act == MA_KO) return;
  bool blocked = (!unblockable && tgt.act == MA_BLOCK && (tgt.right == (src.x > tgt.x)));
  if (&tgt == &mkE && mkFrozenT > 0) dmg *= 1.2f;
  if (blocked) { dmg *= 0.2f; push = 1; }
  else tgt.damaged = true;
  tgt.hp -= dmg; if (tgt.hp < 0) tgt.hp = 0;
  if (!blocked && !(&tgt == &mkE && mkFrozenT > 0)) { tgt.act = MA_HIT; tgt.t = 7; }
  tgt.x += (tgt.x >= src.x) ? push : -push;
  mkClampFighter(ti);
  mkSpark = 5; mkSparkX = (int)((tgt.x + src.x) / 2) + (tgt.x >= src.x ? 4 : -4); mkSparkY = (int)tgt.y + 12;
}

static void mkStartAttack(uint8_t ai, bool kick) {
  MKFighter &a = mkF[ai];
  a.act = kick ? MA_KICK : MA_PUNCH;
  a.t = kick ? 14 : 10;
}

// Avanza un golpe en curso. El golpe conecta en un cuadro fijo (puno: t==6, patada: t==9).
static void mkAttackStep(uint8_t ai, uint8_t bi, float dp, float dk) {
  MKFighter &a = mkF[ai], &b = mkF[bi];
  a.t--;
  if (a.act == MA_PUNCH && a.t == 6) {
    if (fabsf(a.x - b.x) <= 14) { mkHurt(bi, ai, dp, 4, false); }
  } else if (a.act == MA_KICK && a.t == 9) {
    if (fabsf(a.x - b.x) <= 17) { mkHurt(bi, ai, dk, 6, false); }
  }
  if (a.t <= 0) { a.act = MA_IDLE; a.cd = 4; }
}

static void mkPoseOf(uint8_t fi, uint8_t &pose, uint8_t &fr) {
  MKFighter &f = mkF[fi];
  fr = 0;
  switch (f.act) {
    case MA_WALK:  pose = PO_WALK; fr = (f.wf / 3) & 3; break;
    case MA_PUNCH: pose = PO_PUNCH; fr = (f.t >= 7) ? 0 : ((f.t >= 4) ? 1 : 2); break;
    case MA_KICK:  pose = PO_KICK;  fr = (f.t >= 10) ? 0 : ((f.t >= 6) ? 1 : 2); break;
    case MA_BLOCK: pose = PO_BLOCK; fr = (f.wf < 3) ? 0 : 1; break;
    case MA_HIT:   pose = PO_HIT; break;
    case MA_KO:    pose = PO_KO; break;
    case MA_WIN:   pose = PO_WIN; break;
    case MA_CAST:  pose = PO_CAST; break;
    case MA_BUSY:  pose = PO_AIRKICK; fr = (f.wf / 3) & 1; break;
    case MA_CAPT:  pose = f.ov; fr = (f.wf / 3) & 1; break;
    default:       pose = PO_IDLE; fr = (uint8_t)((((mkFrame + fi * 17) / 14) % 4) == 2 ? 1 : 0); break;   // respira despacio: 1 px cada ~1,7 s
  }
}

// ---------- jugador ----------
static bool mkStartSpecial() {
  MKFighter &P = mkP;
  switch (mkSp) {
    case 1: case 2: case 3:                  // lanza / hielo / escopeta
      mkCD = MK_CD_FRAMES;
      P.act = MA_CAST; P.t = 9;
      mkProjOn = true; mkProjKind = mkSp;
      mkProjDir = P.right ? 1 : -1;
      mkProjX = P.x + mkProjDir * 12;
      return true;
    case 4:                                  // Liu Kang: salta a la cabeza y patea 3 s
      mkCD = MK_CD_FRAMES;
      mkSpState = 1; mkSpT = 0; mkLkX0 = P.x;
      P.act = MA_BUSY; P.wf = 0;
      mkE.act = MA_CAPT; mkE.ov = PO_IDLE;
      return true;
    case 5:                                  // Raiden / Kung Lao: lanzan el sombrero
      if (!P.hat && P.id != 8) return false;
      mkCD = MK_CD_FRAMES;
      P.hat = false;
      mkHatFly = true; mkHatBack = false; mkHatHit = false; mkHatFr = 0;
      mkHatDir = P.right ? 1 : -1;
      mkHatX = P.x + mkHatDir * 8; mkHatX0 = P.x;
      P.act = MA_CAST; P.t = 8;
      return true;
    case 9:                                  // Sonya: anillos de energia
      mkCD = MK_CD_FRAMES;
      P.act = MA_CAST; P.t = 9;
      mkProjOn = true; mkProjKind = 4;
      mkProjDir = P.right ? 1 : -1;
      mkProjX = P.x + mkProjDir * 12;
      return true;
    case 10:                                 // Johnny Cage: patada veloz con estela de imagenes
      mkCD = MK_CD_FRAMES;
      mkSpState = 1; mkSpT = 0;
      P.act = MA_CAST; P.t = 999;
      return true;
    case 11:                                 // Jax: golpe de terremoto, onda por el suelo
      mkCD = MK_CD_FRAMES;
      P.act = MA_CAST; P.t = 10;
      mkProjOn = true; mkProjKind = 5;
      mkProjDir = P.right ? 1 : -1;
      mkProjX = P.x + mkProjDir * 14;
      mkSpark = 5; mkSparkX = (int)mkProjX; mkSparkY = MK_GROUND - 3;
      return true;
    case 12: case 14:                        // Reptile: escupitajo de acido / Shang Tsung: calavera en llamas
      mkCD = MK_CD_FRAMES;
      P.act = MA_CAST; P.t = 9;
      mkProjOn = true; mkProjKind = (mkSp == 12) ? 6 : 7;
      mkProjDir = P.right ? 1 : -1;
      mkProjX = P.x + mkProjDir * 12;
      return true;
    case 13:                                 // Baraka: torbellino de cuchillas
      mkCD = MK_CD_FRAMES;
      mkSpState = 1; mkSpT = 0;
      P.act = MA_CAST; P.t = 999;
      return true;
    case 7:                                  // Raiden: llueven rayos 5 s sobre el rival
      mkCD = MK_CD_FRAMES;
      mkSpState = 1; mkSpT = 0;
      P.act = MA_CAST; P.t = 999;
      mkE.act = MA_CAPT; mkE.ov = PO_HIT;
      return true;
    case 8:                                  // Kano: bala de canon
      mkCD = MK_CD_FRAMES;
      mkSpState = 1; mkSpT = 0;
      P.act = MA_CAST; P.t = 999;
      return true;
    case 6:                                  // Noob: clon de sombra + portales
      mkCD = MK_CD_FRAMES;
      mkSpState = 1; mkSpT = 0;
      P.act = MA_CAST; P.t = 10;
      return true;
  }
  return false;
}

static void mkPlayerStep() {
  MKFighter &P = mkP, &E = mkE;
  if (P.cd > 0) P.cd--;
  if (mkSpState != 0 && (mkSp == 4 || mkSp == 7 || mkSp == 8 || mkSp == 10 || mkSp == 13)) { P.wf++; return; }    // Liu Kang: animacion controlada por el especial
  if (P.act == MA_KO || P.act == MA_WIN) return;
  if (P.act == MA_HIT) { if (--P.t <= 0) P.act = MA_IDLE; return; }
  if (P.act == MA_CAST) { if (--P.t <= 0) P.act = MA_IDLE; return; }
  if (P.act == MA_PUNCH || P.act == MA_KICK) { mkAttackStep(0, 1, 7.0f, 11.0f); return; }

  P.right = (E.x > P.x);                                  // siempre mira al rival
  bool blk = (digitalRead(PIN_R) == LOW) || (digitalRead(PIN_4) == LOW);   // R (o B4) bloquea
  int jx = getJoyX();
  float vx = 0;
  if (jx < 300) vx = P.spd; else if (jx > 700) vx = -P.spd;   // convencion del proyecto: X<300 = derecha

  if (blk) {
    if (P.act != MA_BLOCK) P.wf = 0; else if (P.wf < 100) P.wf++;
    P.act = MA_BLOCK; vx = 0;
  } else {
    if (P.act == MA_BLOCK) P.act = MA_IDLE;
    if (P.cd == 0 && digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) { mkStartAttack(0, false); return; }
    if (P.cd == 0 && digitalRead(PIN_L) == LOW) { mkStartAttack(0, true); return; }       // L = patada
    if (digitalRead(PIN_3) == LOW && mkCD == 0 && mkSpState == 0 && !mkProjOn && !mkHatFly) {
      if (mkStartSpecial()) return;
    }
  }
  if (vx != 0) { P.x += vx; P.act = MA_WALK; P.wf++; }
  else if (P.act != MA_BLOCK) P.act = MA_IDLE;

  if (E.act != MA_CAPT && fabsf(P.x - E.x) < 9) P.x = (P.x < E.x) ? E.x - 9 : E.x + 9;   // no se atraviesan
  mkClampFighter(0);
}

// ---------- enemigo (IA) ----------
static void mkEnemyStep() {
  MKFighter &E = mkE, &P = mkP;
  if (E.act == MA_KO || E.act == MA_WIN || E.act == MA_CAPT) { E.wf++; return; }
  if (mkFrozenT > 0) { E.act = MA_IDLE; return; }           // congelado: no hace nada
  if (E.act == MA_HIT) { if (--E.t <= 0) E.act = MA_IDLE; return; }
  if (E.cd > 0) E.cd--;
  if (E.act == MA_PUNCH || E.act == MA_KICK) {
    float k = (1.0f + 0.15f * (mkRound - 1)) * mkDiffDmg[mkDiff] / 10.0f;
    mkAttackStep(1, 0, 6.0f * k, 9.0f * k);
    if (E.act == MA_IDLE) E.cd = (int16_t)(random(14, 34) * 10.0f / mkDiffCd[mkDiff] / (1.0f + 0.2f * (mkRound - 1)));
    return;
  }
  float d = fabsf(P.x - E.x);
  if (E.act == MA_BLOCK) { if (E.wf < 100) E.wf++; if (--E.t <= 0) { E.act = MA_IDLE; if (mkDiff >= 2 && E.cd > 4) E.cd = 4; } return; }   // dificil: contraataca al bajar la guardia
  E.right = (P.x > E.x);
  float dir = E.right ? 1 : -1;

  bool pAtk = (P.act == MA_PUNCH || P.act == MA_KICK) && d < 22;
  if (pAtk && random(100) < mkDiffBlk[mkDiff]) { E.act = MA_BLOCK; E.wf = 0; E.t = 10 + random(8); return; }

  float sp = E.spd * mkDiffSpd[mkDiff] / 10.0f;
  E.act = MA_IDLE;
  if (d > 14) { E.x += dir * sp; E.act = MA_WALK; E.wf++; }
  else if (d < 10 && random(100) < mkDiffRet[mkDiff]) { E.x -= dir * sp; E.act = MA_WALK; E.wf++; }
  if (E.cd == 0 && d <= 17 && P.act != MA_CAPT) {
    bool kick = (d > 14) || random(100) < 30;
    mkStartAttack(1, kick);
  }
  mkClampFighter(1);
}

// ---------- especiales ----------
static void mkHatCheck() {
  if (mkHatHit || mkE.act == MA_CAPT || mkE.act == MA_KO) return;
  if (fabsf(mkHatX - mkE.x) < 9) {
    mkHatHit = true;
    mkHurt(1, 0, mkE.maxHp * 0.20f, 4, true);   // 20 %
    mkHatBack = true;
  }
}

static void mkSpecialStep() {
  MKFighter &P = mkP, &E = mkE;

  // --- proyectiles: lanza, hielo, escopeta ---
  if (mkProjOn) {
    float sp = (mkProjKind == 1) ? 5.5f : (mkProjKind == 2 ? 3.8f : (mkProjKind == 4 ? 4.6f : (mkProjKind == 5 ? 3.6f : (mkProjKind == 6 ? 4.2f : (mkProjKind == 7 ? 5.2f : 6.5f)))));
    mkProjX += mkProjDir * sp;
    if (mkProjX < -6 || mkProjX > 134) mkProjOn = false;
    else if (E.act != MA_CAPT && E.act != MA_KO && fabsf(mkProjX - E.x) < 8) {
      mkProjOn = false;
      if (mkProjKind == 1) {                              // lanza: 25 % y lo jala hacia Scorpion
        mkHurt(1, 0, E.maxHp * 0.25f, 0, true);
        E.x = P.x + mkProjDir * 15; mkClampFighter(1);
        E.act = MA_HIT; E.t = 14;
      } else if (mkProjKind == 2) {                       // hielo: congela 3 s
        mkFrozenT = 90; E.act = MA_IDLE;
        mkSpark = 5; mkSparkX = (int)E.x; mkSparkY = (int)E.y + 10;
      } else if (mkProjKind == 4) {                       // anillos: 22 %
        mkHurt(1, 0, E.maxHp * 0.22f, 7, true);
      } else if (mkProjKind == 6) {                       // acido de Reptile: 14 % + quemadura 15 %
        mkHurt(1, 0, E.maxHp * 0.14f, 5, true);
        mkBurn = 72;
      } else if (mkProjKind == 7) {                       // calavera en llamas de Shang Tsung: 27 %
        mkHurt(1, 0, E.maxHp * 0.27f, 8, true);
      } else if (mkProjKind == 5) {                       // terremoto de Jax: 28 %
        mkHurt(1, 0, E.maxHp * 0.28f, 9, true);
        mkSpark = 6; mkSparkX = (int)E.x; mkSparkY = MK_GROUND - 4;
      } else {                                            // escopeta: 30 %
        mkHurt(1, 0, E.maxHp * 0.30f, 6, true);
      }
    }
  }
  if (mkFrozenT > 0) mkFrozenT--;
  if (mkBurn > 0) {                                        // acido: dano por tiempo
    mkBurn--;
    if (mkBurn % 12 == 0 && E.hp > 0 && E.act != MA_KO) {
      mkDamageRaw(1, E.maxHp * 0.025f);
      mkSpark = 2; mkSparkX = (int)E.x; mkSparkY = (int)E.y + 6;
    }
  }

  // --- Baraka: torbellino de cuchillas, 6 cortes de 6 % mientras avanza ---
  if (mkSp == 13 && mkSpState != 0) {
    float d = P.right ? 1 : -1;
    P.act = MA_CAST; P.t = 999;
    if (mkSpState == 1) { if (++mkSpT >= 5) { mkSpState = 2; mkSpT = 0; } }
    else {
      mkSpT++;
      if (fabsf(P.x - E.x) > 14) { P.x += d * 2.2f; mkClampFighter(0); }
      if (mkSpT % 8 == 0 && fabsf(P.x - E.x) < 24 && E.act != MA_KO && E.act != MA_CAPT) mkHurt(1, 0, E.maxHp * 0.06f, 3, true);
      if (mkSpT >= 48 || E.hp <= 0) { mkSpState = 0; mkSpT = 0; P.act = MA_IDLE; P.t = 0; P.cd = 8; }
    }
  }

  // --- sombrero (Raiden / Kung Lao) ---
  if (mkHatFly) {
    mkHatFr++;
    if (!mkHatBack) {
      mkHatX += mkHatDir * 4.5f;
      mkHatCheck();
      if (fabsf(mkHatX - mkHatX0) > 72 || mkHatX < 4 || mkHatX > 124) mkHatBack = true;
    } else {
      float dir = (P.x > mkHatX) ? 1 : -1;
      mkHatX += dir * 5.0f;
      mkHatCheck();
      if (fabsf(mkHatX - P.x) < 6) { mkHatFly = false; P.hat = true; }
    }
  }

  // --- Liu Kang: salta a la cabeza, patadas 3 s (25 %), vuelve ---
  if (mkSp == 4 && mkSpState != 0) {
    if (mkSpState == 1) {
      float k = (float)(mkSpT + 1) / 14.0f; if (k > 1) k = 1;
      P.x = mkLkX0 + (E.x - mkLkX0) * k;
      P.y = MK_SY - 20 * k - 14 * sinf(3.14159f * k);
      if (++mkSpT >= 14) { mkSpState = 2; mkSpT = 0; P.x = E.x; P.y = MK_SY - 20; }
    } else if (mkSpState == 2) {
      P.x = E.x; P.y = MK_SY - 20;
      E.act = MA_CAPT; E.ov = PO_HIT;
      if (mkSpT % 3 == 0) {                                 // 30 patadas = 25 % de vida
        mkDamageRaw(1, E.maxHp * 0.25f / 30.0f);
        mkSpark = 3; mkSparkX = (int)E.x + (P.right ? 3 : -3); mkSparkY = (int)E.y + 4;
      }
      if (++mkSpT >= 90 || E.hp <= 0) { mkSpState = 3; mkSpT = 0; }
    } else {
      float k = (float)(mkSpT + 1) / 14.0f; if (k > 1) k = 1;
      P.x = E.x + (mkLkX0 - E.x) * k;
      P.y = MK_SY - 20 + 20 * k - 14 * sinf(3.14159f * k);
      if (++mkSpT >= 14) {
        P.x = mkLkX0; P.y = MK_SY; P.act = MA_IDLE; P.cd = 4;
        E.act = MA_HIT; E.t = 8;
        mkSpState = 0; mkSpT = 0;
      }
    }
  }

  // --- Raiden: rayos del cielo durante 5 s (30 %) ---
  if (mkSp == 7 && mkSpState != 0) {
    P.act = MA_CAST; P.t = 999;
    E.act = MA_CAPT; E.ov = PO_HIT;
    if (mkSpT % 6 == 0) {                                   // 25 rayos = 30 % de vida
      mkBoltX = (int)E.x + (int)random(-7, 8);
      mkDamageRaw(1, E.maxHp * 0.30f / 25.0f);
      mkSpark = 3; mkSparkX = (int)E.x; mkSparkY = (int)E.y + 8;
    }
    if (++mkSpT >= 167 || E.hp <= 0) {
      mkSpState = 0; mkSpT = 0; P.act = MA_IDLE; P.t = 0; P.cd = 4;
      E.act = MA_HIT; E.t = 8;
    }
  }

  // --- Kano: se enrolla y rueda hacia el rival (25 %) ---
  if (mkSp == 8 && mkSpState != 0) {
    float d = P.right ? 1 : -1;
    if (mkSpState == 1) {                                   // se agacha
      P.act = MA_CAST; P.t = 999;
      if (++mkSpT >= 7) { mkSpState = 2; mkSpT = 0; P.act = MA_IDLE; P.t = 0; }
    } else {
      P.x += d * 6.0f; mkSpT++;
      bool end = false;
      if (E.act != MA_KO && E.act != MA_CAPT && fabsf(P.x - E.x) < 11) {
        mkHurt(1, 0, E.maxHp * 0.25f, 9, true);
        P.x = E.x - d * 11; end = true;
      } else if (P.x <= 10 || P.x >= 118 || mkSpT > 45) end = true;
      if (end) {
        mkClampFighter(0);
        mkSpState = 0; mkSpT = 0; P.act = MA_IDLE; P.cd = 6;
      }
    }
  }

  // --- Johnny Cage: se agacha y sale disparado en patada voladora (26 %) ---
  if (mkSp == 10 && mkSpState != 0) {
    float d = P.right ? 1 : -1;
    if (mkSpState == 1) {
      P.act = MA_CAST; P.t = 999;
      if (++mkSpT >= 6) { mkSpState = 2; mkSpT = 0; mkLkX0 = P.x; }
    } else {
      P.act = MA_BUSY; P.wf = 0;                            // patada extendida
      P.x += d * 7.0f; mkSpT++;
      bool end = false;
      if (E.act != MA_KO && E.act != MA_CAPT && fabsf(P.x - E.x) < 15) {
        mkHurt(1, 0, E.maxHp * 0.26f, 9, true);
        P.x = E.x - d * 15; end = true;
      } else if (P.x <= 10 || P.x >= 118 || mkSpT > 30) end = true;
      if (end) {
        mkClampFighter(0);
        mkSpState = 0; mkSpT = 0; P.act = MA_IDLE; P.t = 0; P.cd = 6;
      }
    }
  }

  // --- Noob: clon, portales, caida infinita, 40 % ---
  if (mkSp == 6 && mkSpState != 0) {
    switch (mkSpState) {
      case 1:                                              // el clon aparece detras del enemigo
        if (mkSpT == 0) {
          mkCloneOn = true;
          mkCloneX = E.x + ((P.x < E.x) ? 16 : -16);
          if (mkCloneX < 8) mkCloneX = 8;
          if (mkCloneX > 120) mkCloneX = 120;
          mkCloneRight = (E.x > mkCloneX);
          mkClonePose = PO_CAST;
        }
        if (++mkSpT >= 12) {
          mkSpState = 2; mkSpT = 0;
          mkPortals = true; mkPortalX = E.x; mkPortalR = 2;
          E.act = MA_CAPT; E.ov = PO_HIT; E.y = MK_SY;
        }
        break;
      case 2:                                              // se abren los portales
        if (mkPortalR < 12) mkPortalR += 0.8f;
        if (++mkSpT >= 14) { mkSpState = 3; mkSpT = 0; E.ov = PO_FALL; E.flip = false; }
        break;
      case 3:                                              // sube: entra arriba, sale por abajo
        E.wf++;
        E.y -= 3.4f;
        if (E.y + MK_CH <= MK_PT) E.y = MK_GROUND;
        if (++mkSpT >= 75) { mkSpState = 4; mkSpT = 0; E.flip = true; }
        break;
      case 4:                                              // cae: entra abajo, sale por arriba
        E.wf++;
        E.y += 3.6f;
        if (E.y >= MK_GROUND) E.y = MK_PT - MK_CH;
        if (mkSpT < 75) mkSpT++;
        if (mkSpT >= 75 && E.y < 28) {                     // a los 5 s, cuando esta arriba, desaparecen
          mkPortals = false; mkSpState = 5; mkFallV = 3.6f; E.flip = false;
        }
        break;
      case 5:                                              // cae al piso: 40 %
        mkFallV += 0.6f;
        E.y += mkFallV;
        if (E.y >= MK_SY) {
          E.y = MK_SY;
          mkDamageRaw(1, E.maxHp * 0.40f);
          E.act = MA_HIT; E.t = 20;
          mkSpark = 6; mkSparkX = (int)E.x; mkSparkY = MK_GROUND - 3;
          mkSpState = 6; mkSpT = 0; mkClonePose = PO_WALK; mkCloneWalk = 0;
        }
        break;
      case 6:                                              // el clon corre hacia el jugador
        mkCloneRight = (P.x > mkCloneX);
        mkCloneX += (mkCloneRight ? 1 : -1) * 3.2f;
        mkCloneWalk++;
        if (fabsf(mkCloneX - P.x) < 6) { mkSpState = 7; mkSpT = 0; }
        break;
      case 7:                                              // y se une a el
        if (++mkSpT >= 6) { mkCloneOn = false; mkSpState = 0; mkSpT = 0; }
        break;
    }
  }
}

// ---------- dibujo ----------
static void mkDotLine(int x0, int y0, int x1, int y1) {   // linea punteada para el fondo
  int dx = abs(x1 - x0), sx = x0 < x1 ? 1 : -1;
  int dy = -abs(y1 - y0), sy = y0 < y1 ? 1 : -1;
  int err = dx + dy;
  while (true) {
    if (((x0 + y0) & 1) == 0) display.drawPixel(x0, y0, SSD1306_WHITE);
    if (x0 == x1 && y0 == y1) break;
    int e2 = 2 * err;
    if (e2 >= dy) { err += dy; x0 += sx; }
    if (e2 <= dx) { err += dx; y0 += sy; }
  }
}

static void mkEllipse(int cx, int cy, int rx, int ry) {
  int px = cx + rx, py = cy;
  for (int i = 1; i <= 20; i++) {
    float a = i * 6.28318f / 20.0f;
    int x = cx + (int)lroundf(rx * cosf(a)), y = cy + (int)lroundf(ry * sinf(a));
    display.drawLine(px, py, x, y, SSD1306_WHITE);
    px = x; py = y;
  }
}

static void mkDrawBackground() {
  // luna, montañas punteadas y piso de piedra
  display.drawCircle(106, 24, 6, SSD1306_WHITE);
  display.fillCircle(108, 22, 5, SSD1306_BLACK);
  display.drawCircle(106, 24, 6, SSD1306_WHITE);
  static const int8_t mx[9] = {0, 16, 30, 48, 64, 82, 98, 114, 127};
  static const int8_t my[9] = {46, 38, 44, 33, 42, 36, 45, 38, 44};
  for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) mkDotLine(mx[i], my[i], mx[i + 1], my[i + 1]);
  display.drawFastHLine(0, MK_GROUND, 128, SSD1306_WHITE);
  for (int x = 0; x < 128; x += 8) display.drawFastHLine(x + ((x / 8) & 1) * 3, 62, 4, SSD1306_WHITE);
}

static void mkDrawHud(bool showTimer) {
  int pw = (int)(54.0f * mkP.hp / mkP.maxHp);
  int ew = (int)(54.0f * mkE.hp / mkE.maxHp);
  display.drawRect(0, 0, 56, 7, SSD1306_WHITE);
  display.fillRect(1, 1, pw, 5, SSD1306_WHITE);
  display.drawRect(72, 0, 56, 7, SSD1306_WHITE);
  display.fillRect(127 - ew, 1, ew, 5, SSD1306_WHITE);
  if (showTimer) {
    int s = (int)(mkTimer / 1000.0f + 0.99f); if (s > 60) s = 60; if (s < 0) s = 0;
    char b[6]; sprintf(b, "%02d", s);
    display.setTextSize(1); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.setCursor(58, 0); display.print(b);
  }
  for (int i = 0; i < MK_NEED; i++) {                       // rondas ganadas
    if (i < mkPW) display.fillRect(1 + i * 6, 9, 4, 3, SSD1306_WHITE); else display.drawRect(1 + i * 6, 9, 4, 3, SSD1306_WHITE);
    if (i < mkEW) display.fillRect(123 - i * 6, 9, 4, 3, SSD1306_WHITE); else display.drawRect(123 - i * 6, 9, 4, 3, SSD1306_WHITE);
  }
  // barra del especial (abajo a la izquierda)
  int w = (mkCD <= 0) ? 40 : (int)(40.0f * (MK_CD_FRAMES - mkCD) / MK_CD_FRAMES);
  display.fillRect(0, 62, 42, 2, SSD1306_BLACK);
  if (mkCD <= 0) display.fillRect(1, 62, 40, 2, SSD1306_WHITE);
  else { display.drawFastHLine(1, 62, 40, SSD1306_WHITE); display.fillRect(1, 62, w, 2, SSD1306_WHITE); }
}

static void mkDrawFighter(uint8_t fi, bool isEnemy) {
  MKFighter &f = mkF[fi];
  uint8_t pose, fr;
  if (!isEnemy && mkSp == 10 && mkSpState == 2) {                           // estela de imagenes de Cage
    int gd = f.right ? -1 : 1;
    for (int g = 3; g >= 1; g--) {
      mkBuild(f.id, PO_AIRKICK, 0, false);
      mkBlit((int)f.x + gd * g * 9, (int)f.y, f.right, 1, 11, 64, false);
    }
  }
  mkPoseOf(fi, pose, fr);
  mkBuild(f.id, pose, fr, f.hat);
  int clipT = 11, clipB = 64;
  if (isEnemy && mkPortals) { clipT = MK_PT; clipB = MK_GROUND; }
  int x = (int)f.x;
  if (!isEnemy && mkSp == 8 && mkSpState == 2) {                            // Kano hecho bola
    int cy = (int)f.y + 15;
    display.fillCircle(x, cy, 8, SSD1306_BLACK);
    display.drawCircle(x, cy, 8, SSD1306_WHITE); display.drawCircle(x, cy, 7, SSD1306_WHITE);
    float a = mkFrame * 0.9f;
    display.drawLine(x, cy, x + (int)(6 * cosf(a)), cy + (int)(6 * sinf(a)), SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.drawLine(x, cy, x - (int)(6 * cosf(a)), cy - (int)(6 * sinf(a)), SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.fillCircle(x + (int)(4 * cosf(a + 1.6f)), cy + (int)(4 * sinf(a + 1.6f)), 2, SSD1306_WHITE);
    int d = f.right ? -1 : 1;                                               // estela
    display.drawFastHLine(x + d * 10, cy - 3, 6, SSD1306_WHITE); display.drawFastHLine(x + d * 12, cy + 2, 8, SSD1306_WHITE);
    return;
  }
  if (isEnemy && mkSp == 4 && mkSpState == 2 && (mkFrame & 1)) x += 1;      // tiembla bajo las patadas
  mkBlit(x, (int)f.y, f.right, 0, clipT, clipB, f.flip);
  if (isEnemy && mkFrozenT > 0) {                                           // bloque de hielo
    int x0 = (int)f.x - 8, y0 = (int)f.y - 1;
    display.drawRect(x0, y0, 17, 26, SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.drawLine(x0 + 1, y0 + 6, x0 + 6, y0 + 1, SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.drawLine(x0 + 10, y0 + 24, x0 + 15, y0 + 19, SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.drawLine(x0 + 2, y0 + 17, x0 + 4, y0 + 14, SSD1306_WHITE);
  }
}

static void mkDrawHatShape(int x, int y, uint8_t fr) {
  display.fillRect(x - 8, y - 5, 17, 11, SSD1306_BLACK);
  switch (fr & 3) {
    case 0: case 2:
      display.fillRect(x - 6, y - 1, 13, 2, SSD1306_WHITE); display.fillRect(x - 2, y - 3, 5, 2, SSD1306_WHITE); break;
    case 1:
      display.fillTriangle(x - 6, y + 2, x + 6, y + 2, x, y - 4, SSD1306_WHITE); break;
    default:
      display.fillTriangle(x - 6, y - 3, x + 6, y - 3, x, y + 3, SSD1306_WHITE); break;
  }
}

static void mkDrawFanShape(int x, int y, uint8_t fr) {      // abanico girando
  display.fillRect(x - 8, y - 6, 17, 13, SSD1306_BLACK);
  float base = (fr & 3) * 1.5708f;
  int px[5], py[5];
  for (int k = 0; k < 5; k++) {
    float a = base - 0.9f + k * 0.45f;
    px[k] = x + (int)lroundf(7 * cosf(a)); py[k] = y + (int)lroundf(7 * sinf(a));
    display.drawLine(x, y, px[k], py[k], SSD1306_WHITE);
    if (k) display.drawLine(px[k - 1], py[k - 1], px[k], py[k], SSD1306_WHITE);
  }
}

static void mkDrawEffects() {
  // portales (encima del enemigo para que parezca que entra/sale)
  if (mkPortals) {
    int r = (int)mkPortalR; if (r < 2) r = 2;
    int px = (int)mkPortalX;
    mkEllipse(px, MK_PT, r, 3); mkEllipse(px, MK_PT, r > 4 ? r - 4 : 1, 1);
    mkEllipse(px, MK_GROUND, r, 3); mkEllipse(px, MK_GROUND, r > 4 ? r - 4 : 1, 1);
  }
  // clon de Noob: invisible con solo el borde en blanco
  if (mkCloneOn && !((mkSpState == 1 || mkSpState == 7) && (mkSpT & 1))) {
    uint8_t fr = (mkClonePose == PO_WALK) ? ((mkCloneWalk / 3) & 3) : 0;
    mkBuild(mkP.id, mkClonePose, fr, false);
    mkBlit((int)mkCloneX, MK_SY, mkCloneRight, 1, 0, 64, false);
  }
  // Raiden: nubes y rayos
  if (mkSp == 7 && mkSpState != 0) {
    for (int c = 0; c < 3; c++) { mkEllipse(40 + c * 24, 15, 10, 3); mkEllipse(46 + c * 24, 13, 6, 2); }
    if ((mkSpT % 6) < 3) {
      int tx = (int)mkE.x, x = constrain(mkBoltX, 34, 94), y = 18;
      int n = 0;
      while (y < MK_GROUND - 1 && n < 10) {
        int ny = y + 7, nx = x + ((n & 1) ? 4 : -4);
        if (ny >= MK_GROUND - 1) { ny = MK_GROUND - 1; nx = tx; }
        display.drawLine(x, y, nx, ny, SSD1306_WHITE); display.drawLine(x + 1, y, nx + 1, ny, SSD1306_WHITE);
        x = nx; y = ny; n++;
      }
    }
  }
  // proyectiles
  if (mkProjOn) {
    int y = (int)mkP.y + 12, x = (int)mkProjX, d = (mkProjDir > 0) ? 1 : -1;
    if (mkProjKind == 1) {
      display.drawLine((int)mkP.x + d * 10, y, x, y, SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.fillTriangle(x, y - 3, x, y + 3, x + d * 7, y, SSD1306_WHITE);
    } else if (mkProjKind == 2) {
      display.fillCircle(x, y, 3, SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.drawCircle(x, y, 5, SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.drawPixel(x - d * 7, y - 2, SSD1306_WHITE); display.drawPixel(x - d * 9, y + 2, SSD1306_WHITE);
    } else if (mkProjKind == 6) {                                 // acido: gota verde con salpicaduras
      display.fillCircle(x, y, 2, SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.drawPixel(x - d * 4, y - 1, SSD1306_WHITE); display.drawPixel(x - d * 6, y + 1, SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.drawPixel(x - d * 3, y + 3, SSD1306_WHITE); display.drawPixel(x + d * 3, y - 3, SSD1306_WHITE);
    } else if (mkProjKind == 7) {                                 // calavera en llamas con estela
      int yy = y + (int)(2.5f * sinf(mkFrame * 0.6f));
      display.fillCircle(x, yy, 4, SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.fillRect(x - 2, yy - 2, 2, 2, SSD1306_BLACK); display.fillRect(x + 1, yy - 2, 2, 2, SSD1306_BLACK);
      display.drawPixel(x, yy + 1, SSD1306_BLACK); display.drawPixel(x - 1, yy + 3, SSD1306_BLACK); display.drawPixel(x + 1, yy + 3, SSD1306_BLACK);
      display.drawLine(x - d * 5, yy - 2, x - d * 13, yy - 4 + (mkFrame & 1), SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.drawLine(x - d * 5, yy, x - d * 15, yy, SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.drawLine(x - d * 5, yy + 2, x - d * 12, yy + 4 - (mkFrame & 1), SSD1306_WHITE);
    } else if (mkProjKind == 5) {                                 // terremoto: crestas de roca en el suelo
      for (int k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
        int xx = x - d * k * 6, h = 8 - k * 2;
        display.fillTriangle(xx - 3, MK_GROUND - 1, xx + 3, MK_GROUND - 1, xx, MK_GROUND - 1 - h, SSD1306_WHITE);
      }
      display.drawFastHLine(x - d * 20, MK_GROUND - 1, 8, SSD1306_WHITE);
    } else if (mkProjKind == 4) {                                 // anillos de energia
      mkEllipse(x, y, 3, 6); mkEllipse(x, y, 1, 3);
      mkEllipse(x - d * 6, y, 2, 4); mkEllipse(x - d * 11, y, 1, 2);
    } else {
      display.fillRect(x, y - 2, 3, 1, SSD1306_WHITE); display.fillRect(x - d * 2, y, 3, 1, SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.fillRect(x, y + 2, 3, 1, SSD1306_WHITE);
    }
  }
  if (mkBurn > 0) {                                                       // burbujas de acido sobre el rival
    for (int k = 0; k < 3; k++) display.drawCircle((int)mkE.x - 5 + k * 5, (int)mkE.y + 3 - ((mkFrame / 2 + k * 4) % 12), 1, SSD1306_WHITE);
  }
  if (mkSp == 13 && mkSpState == 2) {                                     // cuchillas girando
    int cx = (int)mkP.x, cy = (int)mkP.y + 12;
    float a = mkSpT * 0.9f;
    for (int b = 0; b < 2; b++) {
      float t = a + b * 3.14159f;
      int x2 = cx + (int)(17 * cosf(t)), y2 = cy + (int)(11 * sinf(t));
      display.drawLine(cx, cy, x2, y2, SSD1306_WHITE); display.drawLine(cx, cy + 1, x2, y2 + 1, SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.fillCircle(x2, y2, 1, SSD1306_WHITE);
    }
  }
  if (mkSp == 3 && mkP.act == MA_CAST && mkP.t >= 7) {                    // fogonazo
    int d = mkP.right ? 1 : -1, x = (int)mkP.x + d * 16, y = (int)mkP.y + 12;
    display.drawLine(x - 3, y, x + 3, y, SSD1306_WHITE); display.drawLine(x, y - 3, x, y + 3, SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.drawLine(x - 2, y - 2, x + 2, y + 2, SSD1306_WHITE); display.drawLine(x - 2, y + 2, x + 2, y - 2, SSD1306_WHITE);
  }
  if (mkHatFly) { if (mkP.id == 8) mkDrawFanShape((int)mkHatX, (int)mkP.y + 6, mkHatFr / 2); else mkDrawHatShape((int)mkHatX, (int)mkP.y + 6, mkHatFr / 2); }
  if (mkSpark > 0) {
    int x = mkSparkX, y = mkSparkY;
    display.drawLine(x - 4, y, x + 4, y, SSD1306_WHITE); display.drawLine(x, y - 4, x, y + 4, SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.drawLine(x - 3, y - 3, x + 3, y + 3, SSD1306_WHITE); display.drawLine(x - 3, y + 3, x + 3, y - 3, SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.fillRect(x - 1, y - 1, 3, 3, SSD1306_BLACK); display.drawPixel(x, y, SSD1306_WHITE);
  }
}

static void mkDrawScene(bool showTimer) {
  display.clearDisplay();
  display.setTextSize(1); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
  mkDrawBackground();
  mkDrawFighter(1, true);
  mkDrawFighter(0, false);
  mkDrawEffects();
  mkDrawHud(showTimer);
  if (mkCmbShow > 0) {                                      // contador de combo X1 / X2 / X3
    char cb[4]; sprintf(cb, "X%d", mkCmbShowN);
    display.fillRect(0, 14, 28, 16, SSD1306_BLACK);
    display.setTextSize(2); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.setCursor(2, 15); display.print(cb);
    display.setTextSize(1);
