      drawSpr(ssx - (int8_t)(BMP_IMP_WIDTH * 0.5f / ty), ssy - (int8_t)(8 / ty),
              bmp_imp_bits, bmp_imp_mask, BMP_IMP_WIDTH, BMP_IMP_HEIGHT, sp, ty);
    } else if (type == E_FIREBALL) {
      drawSpr(ssx - (int8_t)(BMP_FIREBALL_WIDTH / 2 / ty), ssy - (int8_t)(BMP_FIREBALL_HEIGHT / 2 / ty),
              bmp_fireball_bits, bmp_fireball_mask, BMP_FIREBALL_WIDTH, BMP_FIREBALL_HEIGHT, 0, ty);
    } else if (type == E_MEDIKIT) {
      drawSpr(ssx - (int8_t)(BMP_ITEMS_WIDTH / 2 / ty), ssy + (int8_t)(5 / ty),
              bmp_items_bits, bmp_items_mask, BMP_ITEMS_WIDTH, BMP_ITEMS_HEIGHT, 0, ty);
    } else if (type == E_KEY) {
      drawSpr(ssx - (int8_t)(BMP_ITEMS_WIDTH / 2 / ty), ssy + (int8_t)(5 / ty),
              bmp_items_bits, bmp_items_mask, BMP_ITEMS_WIDTH, BMP_ITEMS_HEIGHT, 1, ty);
    }
  }
}

static void renderGun(uint8_t gunPos, float jogging) {
  int x = 48 + (int)(sinf(millis() * JOG_SPD) * 10 * jogging);
  int y = DRH - gunPos + (int)(fabsf(cosf(millis() * JOG_SPD)) * 8 * jogging);
  if (gunPos > GUN_SHOT - 2) {
    // muzzle flash bitmap
    display.drawBitmap(x + 6, y - 11, bmp_fire_bits, BMP_FIRE_WIDTH, BMP_FIRE_HEIGHT, SSD1306_WHITE);
  }
  uint8_t clip = 0;
  if (y < DRH) {
    int bottom = y + BMP_GUN_HEIGHT;
    if (bottom > DRH) clip = BMP_GUN_HEIGHT - (bottom - DRH);
    else clip = BMP_GUN_HEIGHT;
  }
  // gun mask (black) + bits (white) — draw into buffer via display helpers
  display.drawBitmap(x, y, bmp_gun_mask, BMP_GUN_WIDTH, clip, SSD1306_BLACK);
  display.drawBitmap(x, y, bmp_gun_bits, BMP_GUN_WIDTH, clip, SSD1306_WHITE);
}

} // namespace DoomGame

void runDoom() {
  using namespace DoomGame;
  buf = display.getBuffer();
  settle();

  // ----- INTRO con logo original -----
  display.clearDisplay();
  display.drawBitmap((DW - BMP_LOGO_WIDTH) / 2, (DH - BMP_LOGO_HEIGHT) / 3,
                     bmp_logo_bits, BMP_LOGO_WIDTH, BMP_LOGO_HEIGHT, SSD1306_WHITE);
  display.setTextSize(1);
  display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
  display.setCursor(DW / 2 - 30, DH - 10);
  display.print(isEnglish ? F("PRESS FIRE") : F("PULSA OK"));
  flushScreen();
  while (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == HIGH && digitalRead(PIN_OK) == HIGH) delay(10);
  if (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == LOW) { settle(); return; }
  settle();

  // Fade-out del logo (efecto original)
  for (uint8_t i = 0; i < GRADIENT_COUNT; i++) {
    fadeScreen(i, false);
    flushScreen();
    delay(35);
  }

  bool exitGame = false;
  while (!exitGame) {
    initLevel();
    bool gunFired = false;
    uint8_t gunPos = 0;
    float viewH = 0, jogging = 0;
    flash = 0; inv = false;
    lastFt = millis();
    nent = 0;
    uint8_t fade = GRADIENT_COUNT - 1;
    memset(buf, 0, 1024);  // limpia todo al empezar (HUD incluido)

    while (pl.health > 0) {
      while (millis() - lastFt < 50) delay(1);
      delta = (float)(millis() - lastFt) / 66.666f;
      if (delta < 0.5f) delta = 0.5f;
      if (delta > 3.f) delta = 3.f;
      lastFt = millis();

      if (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == LOW) {
        if (confirmExit("Doom", "Doom")) { exitGame = true; break; }
        lastFt = millis();
      }

      int jx = getJoyX(), jy = getJoyY();
      // Stick: mismo sentido que el escritorio (vy alto = adelante, vx alto = izquierda)
      if (jy > 700) {
        pl.velocity += (MOV_SPD - pl.velocity) * 0.4f;
        jogging = fabsf(pl.velocity) * 5.f;
      } else if (jy < 300) {
        pl.velocity += (-MOV_SPD - pl.velocity) * 0.4f;
        jogging = fabsf(pl.velocity) * 5.f;
      } else {
        pl.velocity *= 0.5f;
        jogging = fabsf(pl.velocity) * 5.f;
      }

      float rot = ROT_SPD * delta;
      auto rotate = [&](float s) {
        float odx = pl.dir.x;
        pl.dir.x = pl.dir.x * cosf(s) - pl.dir.y * sinf(s);
        pl.dir.y = odx * sinf(s) + pl.dir.y * cosf(s);
        float opx = pl.plane.x;
        pl.plane.x = pl.plane.x * cosf(s) - pl.plane.y * sinf(s);
        pl.plane.y = opx * sinf(s) + pl.plane.y * cosf(s);
      };
      // Girar: stick izquierda = izquierda, derecha = derecha
      if (jx < 300) rotate(-rot);
      else if (jx > 700) rotate(rot);

      viewH = fabsf(sinf(millis() * JOG_SPD)) * 6 * jogging;

      if (gunPos > GUN_TGT) gunPos--;
      else if (gunPos < GUN_TGT) gunPos += 2;
      else if (!gunFired && digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) {
        gunPos = GUN_SHOT; gunFired = true; fire();
      } else if (gunFired && digitalRead(PIN_OK) == HIGH) gunFired = false;

      if (fabsf(pl.velocity) > 0.003f)
        movePos(&pl.pos, pl.dir.x * pl.velocity * delta, pl.dir.y * pl.velocity * delta, false);
      else pl.velocity = 0;

      updateEnts();

      // Solo limpia la vista 3D (como el original), el HUD vive en y=56..63
      memset(buf, 0, DW * (DRH / 8));
      renderMap(viewH);
      renderEnts(viewH);
      renderGun(gunPos, jogging);

      // HUD identico al original: {} salud  [] llaves  + stats
      if (fade == 0) {
        renderHud();
        renderStats();
      }

      // Fade-in al empezar partida
      if (fade > 0) { fadeScreen(fade, false); fade--; }

      // Destello daño (invert como original)
      if (flash > 0) { inv = !inv; flash--; }
      else inv = false;
      display.invertDisplay(inv);
      flushScreen();
    }

    display.invertDisplay(false);
    if (exitGame) break;

    display.clearDisplay();
    display.setTextSize(2);
    display.setCursor(22, 18); display.print(F("YOU DIED"));
    display.setTextSize(1);
    display.setCursor(8, 48);
    display.print(isEnglish ? F("OK again  ESC exit") : F("OK otra  ESC salir"));
    flushScreen();
    delay(400);
    while (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == HIGH && digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == HIGH) delay(10);
    if (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == LOW) { settle(); exitGame = true; }
    settle();

    // Fade-out antes de reiniciar
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < GRADIENT_COUNT; i++) {
      fadeScreen(i, false);
      flushScreen();
      delay(30);
    }
  }
  display.invertDisplay(false);
  settle();
}



void runGames() {
  const char* names[] = {"Snake", "Dino Run", "Breakout", "Flappy Bird", "Mortal Kombat", "Chess", "Tetris", "Doom"};
  const int NUM_GAMES = 8;
  int8_t sel = 0;
  unsigned long lastMove = 0;
  settle();

  while (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == HIGH) {
    int vy = getJoyY();
    if (millis() - lastMove > 180) {
      if (vy > 700)      { sel = (sel + NUM_GAMES - 1) % NUM_GAMES; lastMove = millis(); }
      else if (vy < 300) { sel = (sel + 1) % NUM_GAMES; lastMove = millis(); }
    }

    drawMenu(isEnglish ? "GAMES" : "JUEGOS", names, NUM_GAMES, sel);
    flushScreen();

    if (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) {
      settle();
      switch (sel) {
        case 0: runSnake(); break;
        case 1: runDino(); break;
        case 2: runBreakout(); break;
        case 3: runFlappy(); break;
        case 4: runMortalKombat(); break;
        case 5: runChess(); break;
        case 6: runTetris(); break;
        case 7: runDoom(); break;
      }
      while (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == LOW) delay(10);
      delay(200);
      lastMove = millis();
    }
  }
  currentApp = -1;
  settle();
}


// ===================== BLOC DE NOTAS =====================
// Hasta 10 notas en NVS namespace "notes": keys t0..t9 (titulo 16), b0..b9 (cuerpo 160)
#define NOTE_MAX 10
#define NOTE_TITLE_LEN 17
#define NOTE_BODY_LEN 161

static void noteLoad(uint8_t i, char* title, char* body) {
  Preferences p; p.begin("notes", true);
  char kt[4], kb[4];
  snprintf(kt, sizeof kt, "t%u", i);
  snprintf(kb, sizeof kb, "b%u", i);
  String ts = p.getString(kt, "");
  String bs = p.getString(kb, "");
  p.end();
  strncpy(title, ts.c_str(), NOTE_TITLE_LEN - 1); title[NOTE_TITLE_LEN - 1] = 0;
  strncpy(body, bs.c_str(), NOTE_BODY_LEN - 1); body[NOTE_BODY_LEN - 1] = 0;
}

static void noteSave(uint8_t i, const char* title, const char* body) {
  Preferences p; p.begin("notes", false);
  char kt[4], kb[4];
  snprintf(kt, sizeof kt, "t%u", i);
  snprintf(kb, sizeof kb, "b%u", i);
  p.putString(kt, title);
  p.putString(kb, body);
  p.end();
}

static void noteClear(uint8_t i) {
  Preferences p; p.begin("notes", false);
  char kt[4], kb[4];
  snprintf(kt, sizeof kt, "t%u", i);
  snprintf(kb, sizeof kb, "b%u", i);
  p.remove(kt); p.remove(kb);
  p.end();
}

static bool noteExists(uint8_t i) {
  char t[NOTE_TITLE_LEN], b[NOTE_BODY_LEN];
  noteLoad(i, t, b);
  return t[0] || b[0];
}

static void noteEdit(uint8_t i, const char* title, const char* body) {
  char t[NOTE_TITLE_LEN], b[NOTE_BODY_LEN];
  strncpy(t, title, NOTE_TITLE_LEN - 1); t[NOTE_TITLE_LEN - 1] = 0;
  strncpy(b, body, NOTE_BODY_LEN - 1); b[NOTE_BODY_LEN - 1] = 0;
  if (!textInput(TR("T\241tulo:", "Title:"), t, NOTE_TITLE_LEN)) return;      // {2} cancela
  if (!textInput(TR("Nota:", "Note:"), b, NOTE_BODY_LEN)) return;
  if (!t[0] && b[0]) { strncpy(t, TR("Nota", "Note"), NOTE_TITLE_LEN - 1); t[NOTE_TITLE_LEN - 1] = 0; }
  if (t[0] || b[0]) noteSave(i, t, b);
}

void runNotes() {
  int8_t sel = 0;
  unsigned long lastMove = 0;
  char title[NOTE_TITLE_LEN], body[NOTE_BODY_LEN];
  const char* items[NOTE_MAX];
  char labels[NOTE_MAX][18];
  bool dirty = true;
  settle();

  while (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == HIGH) {
    if (dirty) {                                       // lee la flash solo cuando algo cambio
      for (uint8_t i = 0; i < NOTE_MAX; i++) {
        char t[NOTE_TITLE_LEN], b[NOTE_BODY_LEN];
        noteLoad(i, t, b);
        if (t[0]) snprintf(labels[i], sizeof labels[i], "%s", t);
        else if (b[0]) snprintf(labels[i], sizeof labels[i], isEnglish ? "(untitled)" : "(sin t\241tulo)");
        else snprintf(labels[i], sizeof labels[i], isEnglish ? "[ empty ]" : "[ vac\241a ]");
        items[i] = labels[i];
      }
      dirty = false;
    }
    drawMenu(isEnglish ? "NOTES" : "NOTAS", items, NOTE_MAX, (uint8_t)sel);
    drawHint(34, 2, isEnglish ? "{1} Open" : "{1} Abrir", SSD1306_WHITE);
    flushScreen();

    int vy = getJoyY();
    if (millis() - lastMove > 160) {
      if (vy < 300) { sel = (sel + 1) % NOTE_MAX; lastMove = millis(); }
      else if (vy > 700) { sel = (sel + NOTE_MAX - 1) % NOTE_MAX; lastMove = millis(); }
    }

    if (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) {
      settle();
      noteLoad((uint8_t)sel, title, body);
      if (!title[0] && !body[0]) noteEdit((uint8_t)sel, "", "");               // vacia -> escribir
      else {
        uint8_t v = viewText(title[0] ? title : (isEnglish ? "(untitled)" : "(sin t\241tulo)"),
                             body[0] ? body : (isEnglish ? "(empty)" : "(vac\241a)"),
                             isEnglish ? "{3} edit {4} del" : "{3} editar {4} borrar", 3);
        if (v == 3) noteEdit((uint8_t)sel, title, body);
        else if (v == 4) noteClear((uint8_t)sel);
      }
      dirty = true; lastMove = millis();
    }
    delay(8);
  }
  currentApp = -1;
  settle();
}

// ===================== ADMINISTRADOR DE ARCHIVOS =====================
// Categorias: Configuracion (sys/wifi/clock), Records (scores), Notas (notes)
static char fmNames[16][18];
static char fmVals[16][28];
static uint8_t fmCount = 0;

static void fmAdd(const char* name, const char* val) {
  if (fmCount >= 16) return;
  strncpy(fmNames[fmCount], name, 17); fmNames[fmCount][17] = 0;
  strncpy(fmVals[fmCount], val, 27); fmVals[fmCount][27] = 0;
  fmCount++;
}

static void fmLoadCat(int c) {
  fmCount = 0;
  if (c == 0) {
    Preferences p;
    p.begin("sys", true);
    char b[28];
    snprintf(b, sizeof b, "%u", (unsigned)p.getUChar("br", 150)); fmAdd("brillo", b);
    snprintf(b, sizeof b, "%.1f", p.getFloat("sens", 4.0f)); fmAdd("sensib.", b);
    fmAdd("idioma", p.getBool("en", false) ? "EN" : "ES");
    fmAdd("tema", p.getBool("light", false) ? "light" : "dark");
    {
      uint16_t s = p.getUShort("sleep", 30);
      if (s == 0) snprintf(b, sizeof b, "OFF"); else snprintf(b, sizeof b, "%us", s);
      fmAdd("apagado", b);
    }
    p.end();
    p.begin("wifi", true);
    {
      String ss = p.getString("ssid", "");
      if (ss.length() > 22) ss = ss.substring(0, 22);
      fmAdd("wifi.ssid", ss.length() ? ss.c_str() : "-");
      String pw = p.getString("pe", "");
      if (!pw.length()) pw = p.getString("pass", "");
      fmAdd("wifi.pass", pw.length() ? "********" : "-");
    }
    p.end();
    p.begin("clock", true);
    fmAdd("clock.24h", p.getBool("f24", true) ? "24h" : "12h");
    {
      long tz = p.getLong("tz", 0);
      long a = tz < 0 ? -tz : tz;
      char b[28];
      snprintf(b, sizeof b, "UTC%c%ld:%02ld", tz < 0 ? '-' : '+', a / 3600, (a % 3600) / 60);
      fmAdd("clock.tz", b);
    }
    p.end();
  } else if (c == 1) {
    Preferences p; p.begin("scores", true);
    const char* sk[] = {"snake", "dino", "breakout", "flappy", "mkwin", "mkloss", "mkfat", "chwin", "chloss", "chdraw"};
    const char* snE[] = {"snake", "dino", "breakout", "flappy", "MK wins", "MK loss", "Fatality", "Ch wins", "Ch loss", "Ch draw"};
    const char* snS[] = {"snake", "dino", "breakout", "flappy", "MK gan.", "MK perd.", "Fatality", "Aj gan.", "Aj perd.", "Aj tabl."};
    const char* const* sn = isEnglish ? snE : snS;
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
      char b[28];
      snprintf(b, sizeof b, "%u", (unsigned)p.getUShort(sk[i], 0));
      fmAdd(sn[i], b);
    }
    p.end();
  } else if (c == 2) {
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < NOTE_MAX; i++) {
      char t[NOTE_TITLE_LEN], body[NOTE_BODY_LEN];
      noteLoad(i, t, body);
      if (!t[0] && !body[0]) continue;
      char nm[18];
      snprintf(nm, sizeof nm, "nota %u", i + 1);
      fmAdd(nm, t[0] ? t : (isEnglish ? "(body)" : "(texto)"));
    }
    if (fmCount == 0) fmAdd(isEnglish ? "(no notes)" : "(sin notas)", "");
  } else if (c == 3) {  // dibujos
    for (uint8_t s = 0; s < 3; s++) {
      char nm[18];
      snprintf(nm, sizeof nm, isEnglish ? "Drawing %u" : "Dibujo %u", s + 1);
      fmAdd(nm, dSlotUsed(s) ? (isEnglish ? "saved" : "guardado") : (isEnglish ? "(empty)" : "(vacio)"));
    }
  } else {  // calendario: dias con eventos
    calLoad();
    static const char* const mEs[12] = {"enero","febrero","marzo","abril","mayo","junio","julio","agosto","septiembre","octubre","noviembre","diciembre"};
    static const char* const mEn[12] = {"January","February","March","April","May","June","July","August","September","October","November","December"};
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < CAL_MAX; i++) {
      if (!calDate[i] || !calTxt[i][0]) continue;
      uint32_t k = calDate[i];
      int yy = (int)(k / 10000UL);
      int mm = (int)((k / 100) % 100);
      int dd = (int)(k % 100);
      if (mm < 1 || mm > 12) continue;
      char nm[18];
      if (isEnglish) snprintf(nm, sizeof nm, "%d %s", dd, mEn[mm - 1]);
      else {
        // "10 de enero" (recortado a 17 chars)
        snprintf(nm, sizeof nm, "%d de %.8s", dd, mEs[mm - 1]);
      }
      fmAdd(nm, calTxt[i]);
    }
    if (fmCount == 0) fmAdd(isEnglish ? "(nothing)" : "(no hay nada)", "");
  }
}

void runFileManager() {
  uint8_t step = 0;
  int8_t cat = 0, sel = 0;
  unsigned long lastMove = 0;
  settle();

  while (true) {
    if (step == 0) {
      const char* itES[5] = {"Configuraci\242n", "R\202cords", "Bloc de notas", "Dibujos", "Calendario"};
      const char* itEN[5] = {"Settings data", "Records", "Notes", "Drawings", "Calendar"};
      drawMenu(isEnglish ? "FILES" : "ARCHIVOS", isEnglish ? itEN : itES, 5, (uint8_t)cat);
      flushScreen();
      int vy = getJoyY();
      if (millis() - lastMove > 160) {
        if (vy < 300) { cat = (cat + 1) % 5; lastMove = millis(); }
        if (vy > 700) { cat = (cat + 4) % 5; lastMove = millis(); }
      }
      if (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) {
        settle();
        fmLoadCat(cat);
        sel = 0; step = 1;
        while (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) delay(10);
      }
      if (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == LOW) { settle(); break; }
    } else if (step == 1) {
      const char* items[16];
      for (uint8_t i = 0; i < fmCount; i++) items[i] = fmNames[i];
      const char* title = cat == 0 ? (isEnglish ? "CONFIG" : "CONFIG")
                         : cat == 1 ? (isEnglish ? "RECORDS" : "RECORDS")
                         : cat == 2 ? (isEnglish ? "NOTES" : "NOTAS")
                         : cat == 3 ? (isEnglish ? "DRAWINGS" : "DIBUJOS")
                                    : (isEnglish ? "CALENDAR" : "CALENDARIO");
      drawMenu(title, items, fmCount, (uint8_t)sel);
      if (fmCount > 0 && fmVals[sel][0]) {
        uint8_t st = menuStart((uint8_t)sel, fmCount);
        uint8_t row = (uint8_t)sel - st;
        if (row < 4) {
          int y = 13 + row * 12;
          char vv[12];
          snprintf(vv, sizeof vv, "%s", fmVals[sel]);
          if (strlen(vv) > 8) { vv[7] = '.'; vv[8] = 0; }
          display.fillRect(90, y + 1, 28, 9, SSD1306_WHITE);
          display.setTextColor(SSD1306_BLACK);
          display.setCursor(92, y + 2); display.print(vv);
          display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
        }
      }
      flushScreen();
      int vy = getJoyY();
      if (millis() - lastMove > 160) {
        if (vy < 300 && sel < (int)fmCount - 1) { sel++; lastMove = millis(); }
        if (vy > 700 && sel > 0) { sel--; lastMove = millis(); }
      }
      if (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) {
        settle(); step = 2;
        while (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) delay(10);
      }
      if (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == LOW) {
        settle(); step = 0;
        while (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == LOW) delay(10);
      }
    } else {
      if (cat == 3) {                                   // ver dibujo a pantalla completa
        int s = -1;
        if (sscanf(fmNames[sel], isEnglish ? "Drawing %d" : "Dibujo %d", &s) == 1 && s >= 1 && s <= 3) {
          if (dSlotUsed((uint8_t)(s - 1)) && dLoadSlot((uint8_t)(s - 1))) {
            memcpy(display.getBuffer(), dcv, 1024);
            display.setTextSize(1); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
            // marco sutil y pista
            display.drawRect(0, 0, 128, 64, SSD1306_WHITE);
            display.fillRect(0, 54, 128, 10, SSD1306_BLACK);
            drawHint(-1, 55, isEnglish ? "{1}/{2}: back" : "{1}/{2}: atras", SSD1306_WHITE);
            flushScreen();
            while (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == HIGH && digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == HIGH) delay(20);
            settle();
          } else {
            clockMsg(isEnglish ? "Empty slot" : "Ranura vacia", "", 1000);
          }
          step = 1; continue;
        }
      }
      if (cat == 4) {                                   // evento de calendario
        // titulo = fmNames[sel] (ej "10 de enero"), cuerpo = fmVals[sel]
        if (!fmVals[sel][0] || strcmp(fmNames[sel], isEnglish ? "(nothing)" : "(no hay nada)") == 0) {
          clockMsg(isEnglish ? "Nothing here" : "No hay nada", "", 1100);
        } else {
          viewText(fmNames[sel], fmVals[sel], isEnglish ? "{1}/{2}: back" : "{1}/{2}: atras", 0);
        }
        step = 1; continue;
      }
      if (cat == 2) {                                   // nota completa con desplazamiento
        int nidx = -1;
        if (sscanf(fmNames[sel], "nota %d", &nidx) == 1 && nidx >= 1 && nidx <= NOTE_MAX) {
          char nt[NOTE_TITLE_LEN], nb[NOTE_BODY_LEN];
          noteLoad((uint8_t)(nidx - 1), nt, nb);
          viewText(nt[0] ? nt : fmNames[sel], nb[0] ? nb : (isEnglish ? "(empty)" : "(vac\241o)"), nullptr, 0);
          step = 1; continue;
        }
      }
      display.clearDisplay();
      display.setTextSize(1); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.setCursor(0, 0); display.print(fmNames[sel]);
      display.drawLine(0, 9, 128, 9, SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.setTextWrap(true);
      if (cat == 2) {
        int idx = -1;
        if (sscanf(fmNames[sel], "nota %d", &idx) == 1 && idx >= 1 && idx <= NOTE_MAX) {
          char t[NOTE_TITLE_LEN], body[NOTE_BODY_LEN];
          noteLoad((uint8_t)(idx - 1), t, body);
          display.setCursor(0, 12); display.print(t);
          display.setCursor(0, 24); display.print(body);
        } else {
          display.setCursor(0, 14);
          display.print(fmVals[sel][0] ? fmVals[sel] : (isEnglish ? "(empty)" : "(vacio)"));
        }
      } else {
        display.setCursor(0, 14);
        display.print(fmVals[sel][0] ? fmVals[sel] : (isEnglish ? "(empty)" : "(vacio)"));
      }
      display.setTextWrap(false);
      display.fillRect(0, 52, 128, 12, SSD1306_WHITE);
      drawHint(-1, 54, isEnglish ? "{1}/{2}: back" : "{1}/{2}: atras", SSD1306_BLACK);
      flushScreen();
      if (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW || digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == LOW) {
        settle(); step = 1;
        while (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW || digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == LOW) delay(10);
      }
    }
    delay(12);
  }
  currentApp = -1; settle();
}

void runProximamente() {
  while(digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == HIGH) {
    display.clearDisplay();
    display.setCursor(isEnglish ? 22 : 20, 25);
    display.setTextSize(1);
    display.print(isEnglish ? F("COMING SOON...") : F("PROXIMAMENTE..."));
    flushScreen();
  }
  currentApp = -1; settle();
}

// ===================== CALCULADORA =====================
// ---- Evaluador de expresiones: + - * / ^ % ( ) pi e ans sin cos tan sqrt ln log abs exp ----
static const char* cpP;
static bool cpErr;
static bool cDeg = true;          // true = grados, false = radianes
static double cAns = 0;

static double cpExpr();
static double cpUnary();
static void cpSkip() { while (*cpP == ' ') cpP++; }

static double cpPrimary() {
  cpSkip();
  char c = *cpP;
  double v = 0;
  if (c == '(') {                                   // el parentesis sin cerrar se perdona
    cpP++; v = cpExpr(); cpSkip(); if (*cpP == ')') cpP++;
  } else if ((c >= '0' && c <= '9') || c == '.') {
    char* e; v = strtod(cpP, &e);
    if (e == cpP) { cpErr = true; return 0; }
    cpP = e;
  } else if (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') {
    char nm[8]; uint8_t n = 0;
    while (*cpP >= 'a' && *cpP <= 'z' && n < 7) nm[n++] = *cpP++;
    nm[n] = 0;
    if (!strcmp(nm, "pi")) v = 3.14159265358979;
    else if (!strcmp(nm, "e")) v = 2.71828182845905;
    else if (!strcmp(nm, "ans")) v = cAns;
    else {
      cpSkip();
      if (*cpP != '(') { cpErr = true; return 0; }
      cpP++; double a = cpExpr(); cpSkip(); if (*cpP == ')') cpP++;
      double k = cDeg ? 0.017453292519943295 : 1.0;
      if (!strcmp(nm, "sin")) v = sin(a * k);
      else if (!strcmp(nm, "cos")) v = cos(a * k);
      else if (!strcmp(nm, "tan")) v = tan(a * k);
      else if (!strcmp(nm, "sqrt")) { if (a < 0) { cpErr = true; return 0; } v = sqrt(a); }
      else if (!strcmp(nm, "ln")) { if (a <= 0) { cpErr = true; return 0; } v = log(a); }
      else if (!strcmp(nm, "log")) { if (a <= 0) { cpErr = true; return 0; } v = log10(a); }
      else if (!strcmp(nm, "abs")) v = fabs(a);
      else if (!strcmp(nm, "exp")) v = exp(a);
      else { cpErr = true; return 0; }
    }
  } else { cpErr = true; return 0; }
  cpSkip();
  while (*cpP == '%') { v /= 100.0; cpP++; cpSkip(); }
  return v;
}

static double cpPow() {
  double b = cpPrimary();
  cpSkip();
  if (*cpP == '^') { cpP++; double e = cpUnary(); return pow(b, e); }
  return b;
}

static double cpUnary() {
  cpSkip();
  if (*cpP == '-') { cpP++; return -cpUnary(); }
  if (*cpP == '+') { cpP++; return cpUnary(); }
  return cpPow();
}

static double cpTerm() {
  double v = cpUnary();
  for (;;) {
    cpSkip(); char c = *cpP;
    if (c == '*') { cpP++; v *= cpUnary(); }
    else if (c == '/') { cpP++; double d = cpUnary(); if (d == 0) { cpErr = true; return 0; } v /= d; }
    else if (c == '(' || (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= '0' && c <= '9') || c == '.') v *= cpPow();   // 2(3), 2pi, 3sin(30)
    else break;
    if (cpErr) return 0;
  }
  return v;
}

static double cpExpr() {
  double v = cpTerm();
  for (;;) {
    cpSkip(); char c = *cpP;
    if (c == '+') { cpP++; v += cpTerm(); }
    else if (c == '-') { cpP++; v -= cpTerm(); }
    else break;
    if (cpErr) return 0;
  }
  return v;
}

static bool calcEval(const char* e, double* out) {
  cpP = e; cpErr = false;
  double v = cpExpr();
  cpSkip();
  if (*cpP != 0) cpErr = true;
  if (cpErr || v != v || fabs(v) > 1e300) return false;       // NaN o infinito
  *out = v;
  return true;
}

static void calcFmt(double v, char* b) {            // b >= 24
  if (fabs(v) < 1e-12) v = 0;
  snprintf(b, 24, "%.10g", v);
}

static bool calcIsOpCh(char c) { return c == '+' || c == '-' || c == '*' || c == '/' || c == '^' || c == '%'; }

// Inserta un trozo en la expresion con las reglas de multiplicacion implicita
static void calcInsert(char* e, const char* tok, bool* just) {
  char t0 = tok[0];
  if (*just) {
    if (!calcIsOpCh(t0)) e[0] = 0;                  // tras un resultado, un numero empieza de cero
    *just = false;
  }
  size_t n = strlen(e);
  if (n == 0 && (t0 == '*' || t0 == '/' || t0 == '^' || t0 == '%')) { strcpy(e, "ans"); n = 3; }
  if (n + strlen(tok) + 2 > 58) return;
  char last = n ? e[n - 1] : 0;
  bool lastLetter = (last >= 'a' && last <= 'z');
  bool tokLetter = (t0 >= 'a' && t0 <= 'z');
  bool tokDigit = (t0 >= '0' && t0 <= '9') || t0 == '.';
  if (tokLetter && (lastLetter || (last >= '0' && last <= '9') || last == '.' || last == ')')) { e[n++] = '*'; e[n] = 0; }
  else if (tokDigit && (lastLetter || last == ')')) { e[n++] = '*'; e[n] = 0; }
  strcat(e, tok);
}

static void calcBack(char* e) {
  size_t n = strlen(e);
  if (!n) return;
  char c = e[n - 1];
  if (c == '(') {                                    // borra "sin(" completo
    n--; while (n > 0 && e[n - 1] >= 'a' && e[n - 1] <= 'z') n--;
  } else if (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') {                 // borra "pi", "ans"...
    while (n > 0 && e[n - 1] >= 'a' && e[n - 1] <= 'z') n--;
  } else n--;
  e[n] = 0;
}

void runFullCalc() {
  struct CK { const char* lab; const char* ins; };
  static const CK pg[2][24] = {
    {
      {"7","7"},{"8","8"},{"9","9"},{"/","/"},{"C","@C"},{"<","@B"},
      {"4","4"},{"5","5"},{"6","6"},{"*","*"},{"(","("},{")",")"},
      {"1","1"},{"2","2"},{"3","3"},{"-","-"},{"ANS","ans"},{"fn","@F"},
      {"0","0"},{".","."},{"=","@="},{"+","+"},{"%","%"},{"^","^"}
    }, {
      {"sin","sin("},{"cos","cos("},{"tan","tan("},{"sq","sqrt("},{"C","@C"},{"<","@B"},
      {"ln","ln("},{"log","log("},{"x2","^2"},{"1/x","1/("},{"(","("},{")",")"},
      {"pi","pi"},{"e","e"},{"abs","abs("},{"exp","exp("},{"ANS","ans"},{"123","@F"},
      {"0","0"},{".","."},{"=","@="},{"+","+"},{"%","%"},{"DEG","@D"}
    }
  };
  char expr[64] = "";         // lo que se esta escribiendo
  char last[64] = "";         // expresion anterior (se muestra al dar =)
  char res[24] = "";          // resultado o vista previa
  bool just = false;          // acaba de darse "="
  bool err = false;
  uint8_t page = 0; int8_t kx = 0, ky = 0;
  bool dirty = true;
  unsigned long lastMove = 0, lastOk = 0;
  delay(200);

  while (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == HIGH) {
    if (dirty) {                                      // vista previa en vivo
      dirty = false; res[0] = 0;
      if (!just && !err && expr[0]) {
        double v;
        bool complex = (expr[0] < '0' || expr[0] > '9') && expr[0] != '.';
        if (!complex) for (const char* q = expr; *q; q++) if (!((*q >= '0' && *q <= '9') || *q == '.')) { complex = true; break; }
        if (complex && calcEval(expr, &v)) calcFmt(v, res);
      }
    }

    display.clearDisplay(); display.setTextSize(1); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.drawRoundRect(0, 0, 128, 18, 3, SSD1306_WHITE);
    {
      char line1[64]; const char* src = just ? last : expr;
      size_t sl = strlen(src); size_t from = sl > 19 ? sl - 19 : 0;
      size_t j = 0; if (from > 0) line1[j++] = '<';
      for (size_t i = from + (from > 0 ? 1 : 0); i < sl && j < 62; i++) line1[j++] = (src[i] == '*') ? 'x' : src[i];
      if (just && j < 62) line1[j++] = '=';
      line1[j] = 0;
      display.setCursor(124 - (int)strlen(line1) * 6, 2); display.print(line1);
      const char* l2 = err ? "Error" : (just ? expr : res);
      display.setCursor(124 - (int)strlen(l2) * 6, 10); display.print(l2);
    }

    int vx = getJoyX(), vy = getJoyY();
    if (millis() - lastMove > 140) {
      if (vx < 300) { kx = (kx + 1) % 6; lastMove = millis(); }
      else if (vx > 700) { kx = (kx + 5) % 6; lastMove = millis(); }
      if (vy < 300) { ky = (ky + 1) % 4; lastMove = millis(); }
      else if (vy > 700) { ky = (ky + 3) % 4; lastMove = millis(); }
    }

    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 24; i++) {
      uint8_t r = i / 6, c = i % 6;
      int x = 1 + c * 21, y = 20 + r * 11;
      bool on = (ky == r && kx == c);
      uint16_t fg = on ? SSD1306_BLACK : SSD1306_WHITE;
      if (on) display.fillRoundRect(x, y, 20, 10, 2, SSD1306_WHITE); else display.drawRoundRect(x, y, 20, 10, 2, SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.setTextColor(fg);
      const char* lb = pg[page][i].lab;
      int cx = x + 10, cy = y + 5;
      if (!strcmp(lb, "/")) {                         // simbolo de division
        display.drawFastHLine(cx - 4, cy, 9, fg); display.fillRect(cx - 1, cy - 3, 2, 2, fg); display.fillRect(cx - 1, cy + 2, 2, 2, fg);
      } else if (!strcmp(lb, "*")) {
        display.drawLine(cx - 3, cy - 3, cx + 3, cy + 3, fg); display.drawLine(cx - 3, cy + 3, cx + 3, cy - 3, fg);
        display.drawLine(cx - 2, cy - 3, cx + 4, cy + 3, fg); display.drawLine(cx - 2, cy + 3, cx + 4, cy - 3, fg);
      } else if (!strcmp(lb, "+")) { display.drawFastHLine(cx - 3, cy, 7, fg); display.drawFastVLine(cx, cy - 3, 7, fg); }
      else if (!strcmp(lb, "-")) { display.drawFastHLine(cx - 3, cy, 7, fg); }
      else if (!strcmp(lb, "=")) { display.drawFastHLine(cx - 3, cy - 2, 7, fg); display.drawFastHLine(cx - 3, cy + 1, 7, fg); }
      else if (!strcmp(lb, ".")) { display.fillRect(cx - 1, cy + 1, 2, 2, fg); }
      else if (!strcmp(lb, "<")) { display.drawFastHLine(cx - 5, cy, 11, fg); display.drawLine(cx - 5, cy, cx - 2, cy - 3, fg); display.drawLine(cx - 5, cy, cx - 2, cy + 3, fg); }
      else if (!strcmp(lb, "sq")) {                   // raiz cuadrada
        display.drawLine(cx - 5, cy + 1, cx - 3, cy + 3, fg); display.drawLine(cx - 3, cy + 3, cx - 1, cy - 3, fg); display.drawFastHLine(cx - 1, cy - 3, 7, fg);
      } else if (!strcmp(lb, "x2")) {
        display.setCursor(cx - 5, y + 2); display.print('x'); display.setCursor(cx + 1, y + 1); display.print('2');
      } else if (!strcmp(lb, "pi")) {
        display.drawFastHLine(cx - 4, cy - 2, 9, fg); display.drawFastVLine(cx - 2, cy - 2, 6, fg); display.drawLine(cx + 2, cy - 2, cx + 2, cy + 3, fg); display.drawPixel(cx + 3, cy + 3, fg);
      } else {
        const char* t = !strcmp(lb, "DEG") ? (cDeg ? "DEG" : "RAD") : lb;
        display.setCursor(cx - (int)strlen(t) * 3 + 1, y + 1); display.print(t);
      }
    }
    display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);

    if (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW && millis() - lastOk > 190) {
      lastOk = millis();
      const char* ins = pg[page][ky * 6 + kx].ins;
      if (err && ins[0] != '@') { err = false; expr[0] = 0; just = false; }
      if (ins[0] == '@') {
        switch (ins[1]) {
          case 'C': expr[0] = 0; last[0] = 0; just = false; err = false; break;
          case 'B': if (just) { expr[0] = 0; just = false; } else calcBack(expr); err = false; break;
          case 'F': page ^= 1; break;
          case 'D': cDeg = !cDeg; break;
          case '=': {
            double v;
            if (expr[0] && !just) {
              if (calcEval(expr, &v)) {
                strcpy(last, expr); cAns = v; calcFmt(v, expr); just = true; err = false;
              } else err = true;
            }
          } break;
        }
      } else calcInsert(expr, ins, &just);
      dirty = true;
    }
    flushScreen();
  }
  currentApp = -1;
  settle();
}

// ===================== TIMER =====================
// ===================== RELOJ / CRONOMETRO / ALARMA =====================
#define PIN_BUZZER -1   // pon aqui el pin de un buzzer activo si algun dia le conectas uno

// clock globals arriba

uint8_t alMode = 0;             // 0 apagada, 1 "en 1 minuto" (usa millis), 2 a una hora (usa el reloj)
unsigned long alAtMs = 0;
time_t alAtEpoch = 0;
int alH = 7, alM = 0;
bool swRun = false; unsigned long swStart = 0, swEl = 0;
// clockFmtDone arriba

int alDH = 0, alDM = 1, alDS = 0;   // ultima duracion usada

void clockSave() {
  Preferences p; p.begin("clock", false);
  p.putBool("f24", clock24); p.putLong("tz", tzOffset); p.putBool("tza", tzAuto); p.putBool("fmt", clockFmtDone);
  p.end();
}

void ntpKick() { configTime(0, 0, "pool.ntp.org", "time.google.com", "time.nist.gov"); }

void clockInit() {
  Preferences p; p.begin("clock", true);
  clock24 = p.getBool("f24", true); tzOffset = p.getLong("tz", 0); tzAuto = p.getBool("tza", true); clockFmtDone = p.getBool("fmt", false);
  p.end();
  ntpKick();
#if PIN_BUZZER >= 0
  pinMode(PIN_BUZZER, OUTPUT); digitalWrite(PIN_BUZZER, LOW);
#endif
}

bool timeIsValid() { return time(nullptr) > 1700000000L; }

void getLocal(struct tm* o) { time_t t = time(nullptr) + tzOffset; gmtime_r(&t, o); }

bool fetchTimezone() {
  if (WiFi.status() != WL_CONNECTED) return false;
  WiFiClient cl; HTTPClient http; http.setTimeout(4000);
  if (!http.begin(cl, "http://ip-api.com/json/?fields=status,offset")) return false;
  bool ok = false;
  if (http.GET() == 200) {
    String body = http.getString();
    int i = body.indexOf("\"offset\":");
    if (i >= 0) { tzOffset = body.substring(i + 9).toInt(); ok = true; }
  }
  http.end();
  return ok;
}

long daysFromCivil(long y, int m, int d) {
  if (m <= 2) y--;
  long era = (y >= 0 ? y : y - 399) / 400;
  long yoe = y - era * 400;
  long doy = (153L * (m + (m > 2 ? -3 : 9)) + 2) / 5 + d - 1;
  long doe = yoe * 365 + yoe / 4 - yoe / 100 + doy;
  return era * 146097L + doe - 719468L;
}

void setManualTime(int y, int mo, int d, int h, int mi) {
  long e = daysFromCivil(y, mo, d) * 86400L + h * 3600L + mi * 60L - tzOffset;
  struct timeval tv; tv.tv_sec = e; tv.tv_usec = 0;
  settimeofday(&tv, NULL);
  timeManual = true;
}

const char* hourStr(char* b, int h, int m) {   // b >= 8 bytes; devuelve "", "AM" o "PM"
  if (clock24) { snprintf(b, 8, "%02d:%02d", h, m); return ""; }
  int h12 = h % 12; if (h12 == 0) h12 = 12;
  snprintf(b, 8, "%d:%02d", h12, m);
  return h >= 12 ? "PM" : "AM";
}

void waitEsc() { while (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == LOW) delay(10); delay(120); }

void clockMsg(const char* a, const char* b, int ms) {
  display.clearDisplay(); display.drawRect(0, 0, 128, 64, SSD1306_WHITE);
  display.setTextSize(1); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
  display.setCursor(64 - strlen(a) * 3, 22); display.print(a);
  display.setCursor(64 - strlen(b) * 3, 36); display.print(b);
  flushScreen(); delay(ms);
}

// ---------- ALARMA ----------
void alarmRing(uint8_t kind) {
  wakeDisplay();
  alarmBusy = true;
  uint8_t* bk = (uint8_t*)malloc(1024);
  if (bk) memcpy(bk, display.getBuffer(), 1024);
  while (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) delay(10);
  delay(600);                                   // evita que un pulso de otra app la quite al instante
  unsigned long t0 = millis();
  bool done = false;
  while (!done) {
    bool fl = ((millis() - t0) / 350) % 2;
    uint16_t fg = fl ? SSD1306_BLACK : SSD1306_WHITE;
    display.clearDisplay();
    if (fl) display.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 64, SSD1306_WHITE);
    else { display.drawRect(0, 0, 128, 64, SSD1306_WHITE); display.drawRect(2, 2, 124, 60, SSD1306_WHITE); }
    display.setTextColor(fg);
    display.setTextSize(2); display.setCursor(isEnglish ? 28 : 22, 7);
    display.print(isEnglish ? F("ALARM!") : F("ALARMA!"));
    display.setTextSize(1);
    const char* m = kind == 1 ? (isEnglish ? "One minute is up!" : "\255Ya pas\242 el minuto!")
                              : (isEnglish ? "It's time!" : "\255Ya es la hora!");
    display.setCursor(64 - strlen(m) * 3, 27); display.print(m);
    display.setCursor(12, 46); display.print(isEnglish ? F("Press") : F("Pulsa"));
    display.drawCircle(58, 49, 6, fg);
    display.setCursor(56, 46); display.print(F("1"));
    display.setCursor(70, 46); display.print(isEnglish ? F("to stop") : F("parar"));
    flushScreen();
#if PIN_BUZZER >= 0
    digitalWrite(PIN_BUZZER, fl ? HIGH : LOW);
#endif
    if (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) done = true;
    delay(20);
  }
#if PIN_BUZZER >= 0
  digitalWrite(PIN_BUZZER, LOW);
#endif
  while (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) delay(10);
  settle();
  if (bk) { memcpy(display.getBuffer(), bk, 1024); free(bk); }
  alarmBusy = false;
}

void alarmCheck() {
  if (alMode == 0 || alarmBusy) return;
  static unsigned long last = 0;
  if (millis() - last < 200) return;
  last = millis();
  bool due = false;
  if (alMode == 1) due = (long)(millis() - alAtMs) >= 0;
  else if (timeIsValid()) due = time(nullptr) >= alAtEpoch;
  if (due) { uint8_t k = alMode; alMode = 0; alarmRing(k); }
}

// Todas las apps dibujan con flushScreen(): asi la alarma suena en cualquier app
void flushScreen() {
  perfTick();
  checkAutoSleep();
  if (displayAsleep) {
    while (displayAsleep) {
      checkAutoSleep();
      alarmCheck();
      delay(30);
    }
  }
  alarmCheck();
  display.display();
}

// ---------- EDITOR DE NUMEROS (n=2: HH:MM, n=5: HH:MM DD/MM/AAAA) ----------
void drawNum(int x, int y, int val, uint8_t digs, bool sel) {
  char b[8]; snprintf(b, sizeof b, "%0*d", (int)digs, val);
  if (sel) { display.fillRect(x - 1, y - 1, digs * 12 + 1, 17, SSD1306_WHITE); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_BLACK); }
  else display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
  display.setTextSize(2); display.setCursor(x, y); display.print(b);
  display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
}

bool editNums(const char* title, int* v, const int* lo, const int* hi, uint8_t n) {
  uint8_t f = 0; settle();
  while (true) {
    if (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == LOW) { waitEsc(); return false; }
    int jx = getJoyX(), jy = getJoyY();
    if (jy > 700) { v[f] = v[f] >= hi[f] ? lo[f] : v[f] + 1; delay(140); }
    else if (jy < 300) { v[f] = v[f] <= lo[f] ? hi[f] : v[f] - 1; delay(140); }
    else if (jx < 300) { f = (f + 1) % n; delay(220); }
    else if (jx > 700) { f = (f + n - 1) % n; delay(220); }
    if (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) { settle(); return true; }
    display.clearDisplay(); display.setTextSize(1); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.setCursor(0, 0); display.print(title);
    int ty = n == 5 ? 14 : 24;
    if (n == 3) {
      drawNum(16, ty, v[0], 2, f == 0);
      display.setTextSize(2); display.setCursor(40, ty); display.print(F(":"));
      drawNum(52, ty, v[1], 2, f == 1);
      display.setTextSize(2); display.setCursor(76, ty); display.print(F(":"));
      drawNum(88, ty, v[2], 2, f == 2);
    } else {
      drawNum(34, ty, v[0], 2, f == 0);
      display.setTextSize(2); display.setCursor(58, ty); display.print(F(":"));
      drawNum(70, ty, v[1], 2, f == 1);
    }
    if (n == 5) {
      drawNum(4, 36, v[2], 2, f == 2);
      display.setTextSize(2); display.setCursor(28, 36); display.print(F("/"));
      drawNum(40, 36, v[3], 2, f == 3);
      display.setTextSize(2); display.setCursor(64, 36); display.print(F("/"));
      drawNum(76, 36, v[4], 4, f == 4);
    }
    drawHint(-1, 56, isEnglish ? "{1}: set  {2}: back" : "{1}: fijar  {2}: salir", SSD1306_WHITE);
    flushScreen();
  }
}

// Pista de boton: [texto] (n) [texto], centrada en la fila y
void drawKeyHint(int y, char digit, const char* pre, const char* post) {
  int wp = pre[0] ? (int)strlen(pre) * 6 + 5 : 0, wq = post[0] ? (int)strlen(post) * 6 + 5 : 0;
  int x0 = (128 - (wp + 13 + wq)) / 2;
  display.setTextSize(1); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
  if (pre[0]) { display.setCursor(x0, y + 3); display.print(pre); }
  int cx = x0 + wp + 6, cy = y + 6;
  display.drawCircle(cx, cy, 6, SSD1306_WHITE);
  display.setCursor(cx - 2, cy - 3); display.print(digit);
  if (post[0]) { display.setCursor(x0 + wp + 13 + 5, y + 3); display.print(post); }
}

void clockCurrent(int* y, int* mo, int* d, int* h, int* m) {
  *y = 2026; *mo = 1; *d = 1; *h = 12; *m = 0;
  if (timeIsValid()) { struct tm t; getLocal(&t); *y = t.tm_year + 1900; *mo = t.tm_mon + 1; *d = t.tm_mday; *h = t.tm_hour; *m = t.tm_min; }
  else {                                                   // sin hora: usa la fecha en que se compilo el programa
    static const char* const mn = "JanFebMarAprMayJunJulAugSepOctNovDec";
    char mon[4] = {0, 0, 0, 0}; int dd = 1, yy = 2026;
    if (sscanf(__DATE__, "%3s %d %d", mon, &dd, &yy) == 3) {
      const char* p = strstr(mn, mon); if (p) *mo = (int)(p - mn) / 3 + 1;
      *d = dd; *y = yy;
    }
  }
}

int daysInMonth(int y, int m) {
  static const uint8_t dim[12] = {31, 28, 31, 30, 31, 30, 31, 31, 30, 31, 30, 31};
  if (m == 2 && ((y % 4 == 0 && y % 100 != 0) || y % 400 == 0)) return 29;
  return dim[m - 1];
}

// Pantalla de hora: HH : MM (y AM/PM si el formato es de 12 h)
bool clockEditTime(int* h24, int* mm) {
  int pm = *h24 >= 12 ? 1 : 0, m = *mm, hv = *h24;
  if (!clock24) { hv = *h24 % 12; if (hv == 0) hv = 12; }
  uint8_t f = 0, n = clock24 ? 2 : 3; settle();
  while (true) {
    if (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == LOW) { waitEsc(); return false; }
    int jx = getJoyX(), jy = getJoyY();
    int lo = clock24 ? 0 : 1, hi = clock24 ? 23 : 12;
    if (jy > 700 || jy < 300) {
      int dir = (jy > 700) ? 1 : -1;
      if (f == 0) { hv += dir; if (hv > hi) hv = lo; if (hv < lo) hv = hi; }
      else if (f == 1) { m += dir; if (m > 59) m = 0; if (m < 0) m = 59; }
      else pm ^= 1;
      delay(150);
    } else if (jx < 300) { f = (f + 1) % n; delay(220); }
    else if (jx > 700) { f = (f + n - 1) % n; delay(220); }
    if (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) { settle(); break; }
    display.clearDisplay(); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.setTextSize(1);
    const char* t = isEnglish ? "SET THE TIME" : "PONER LA HORA";
    display.setCursor(64 - strlen(t) * 3, 2); display.print(t);
    int xh = clock24 ? 34 : 10, xm = clock24 ? 70 : 46;
    display.setCursor(xh + 6, 17); display.print(F("HH"));
    display.setCursor(xm + 6, 17); display.print(F("MM"));
    drawNum(xh, 28, hv, 2, f == 0);
    display.setTextSize(2); display.setCursor(xh + 24, 28); display.print(F(":"));
    drawNum(xm, 28, m, 2, f == 1);
    if (!clock24) {
      display.setTextSize(1); display.setCursor(82, 17); display.print(F("AM/PM"));
      if (f == 2) { display.fillRect(81, 27, 25, 17, SSD1306_WHITE); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_BLACK); }
      display.setTextSize(2); display.setCursor(82, 28); display.print(pm ? F("PM") : F("AM"));
      display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
    }
    drawKeyHint(50, '1', isEnglish ? "Press" : "Pulsa", "");
    flushScreen();
  }
  *h24 = clock24 ? hv : (hv % 12) + (pm ? 12 : 0);
  *mm = m;
  return true;
}

// Pantalla de fecha: DD / MM / AAAA
bool clockEditDate(int* yy, int* mo, int* dd) {
  int y = *yy, m = *mo, d = *dd; uint8_t f = 0; settle();
  while (true) {
    if (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == LOW) { waitEsc(); return false; }
    int jx = getJoyX(), jy = getJoyY();
    if (jy > 700 || jy < 300) {
      int dir = (jy > 700) ? 1 : -1;
      if (f == 0) { d += dir; int mx = daysInMonth(y, m); if (d > mx) d = 1; if (d < 1) d = mx; }
      else if (f == 1) { m += dir; if (m > 12) m = 1; if (m < 1) m = 12; }
      else { y += dir; if (y > 2099) y = 2024; if (y < 2024) y = 2099; }
      int mx = daysInMonth(y, m); if (d > mx) d = mx;
      delay(150);
    } else if (jx < 300) { f = (f + 1) % 3; delay(220); }
    else if (jx > 700) { f = (f + 2) % 3; delay(220); }
    if (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) { settle(); break; }
    display.clearDisplay(); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.setTextSize(1);
    const char* t = isEnglish ? "SET THE DATE" : "PONER LA FECHA";
    display.setCursor(64 - strlen(t) * 3, 2); display.print(t);
    display.setCursor(10, 17); display.print(F("DD"));
    display.setCursor(46, 17); display.print(F("MM"));
    display.setCursor(isEnglish ? 82 : 82, 17); display.print(isEnglish ? F("YYYY") : F("AAAA"));
    drawNum(4, 28, d, 2, f == 0);
    display.setTextSize(2); display.setCursor(28, 28); display.print(F("/"));
    drawNum(40, 28, m, 2, f == 1);
    display.setTextSize(2); display.setCursor(64, 28); display.print(F("/"));
    drawNum(76, 28, y, 4, f == 2);
    drawKeyHint(50, '1', isEnglish ? "Press" : "Pulsa", "");
    flushScreen();
  }
  *yy = y; *mo = m; *dd = d;
  return true;
}

void clockSetTimeOnly() {
  int y, mo, d, h, m; clockCurrent(&y, &mo, &d, &h, &m);
  if (clockEditTime(&h, &m)) { setManualTime(y, mo, d, h, m); clockMsg(isEnglish ? "Time saved" : "Hora guardada", "", 900); }
}

void clockSetDateOnly() {
  int y, mo, d, h, m; clockCurrent(&y, &mo, &d, &h, &m);
  if (clockEditDate(&y, &mo, &d)) { setManualTime(y, mo, d, h, m); clockMsg(isEnglish ? "Date saved" : "Fecha guardada", "", 900); }
}

// Primera configuracion manual: hora y despues fecha
void clockSetManual() {
  int y, mo, d, h, m; clockCurrent(&y, &mo, &d, &h, &m);
  if (!clockEditTime(&h, &m)) return;
  if (!clockEditDate(&y, &mo, &d)) return;
  setManualTime(y, mo, d, h, m);
  clockMsg(isEnglish ? "Saved" : "Guardado", "", 900);
}

void clockAlarmMenu() {
  uint8_t sel = 0; settle();
  while (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == HIGH) {
    uint8_t nIt = alMode ? 3 : 2;
    int jy = getJoyY();
    if (jy > 700) { sel = (sel + nIt - 1) % nIt; delay(180); } else if (jy < 300) { sel = (sel + 1) % nIt; delay(180); }
    if (sel >= nIt) sel = 0;
    if (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) {
      settle();
      if (sel == 0) {                                   // suena dentro de un tiempo
        int v[3] = {alDH, alDM, alDS}; const int lo[3] = {0, 0, 0}, hi[3] = {23, 59, 59};
        if (editNums(isEnglish ? "Ring in (H:M:S)" : "Sonar en (H:M:S)", v, lo, hi, 3)) {
          if (v[0] + v[1] + v[2] == 0) clockMsg(isEnglish ? "Pick a time" : "Pon un tiempo", isEnglish ? "above zero" : "mayor que cero", 1300);
          else {
            alDH = v[0]; alDM = v[1]; alDS = v[2];
            alAtMs = millis() + ((unsigned long)alDH * 3600UL + alDM * 60UL + alDS) * 1000UL; alMode = 1;
            char b[16]; snprintf(b, sizeof b, "%d:%02d:%02d", alDH, alDM, alDS);
            clockMsg(isEnglish ? "Alarm in" : "Alarma en", b, 1400);
          }
        }
      } else if (sel == 1) {                            // suena a una hora exacta
        if (!timeIsValid()) { clockMsg(isEnglish ? "Set the time" : "Primero pon", isEnglish ? "first (Time)" : "la hora (Hora)", 1600); }
        else {
          int v[2] = {alH, alM}; const int lo[2] = {0, 0}, hi[2] = {23, 59};
          if (editNums(isEnglish ? "Alarm at (24h)" : "Alarma a las (24h)", v, lo, hi, 2)) {
            alH = v[0]; alM = v[1];
            long nowE = (long)time(nullptr), loc = nowE + tzOffset, mid = loc - (loc % 86400L);
            alAtEpoch = (time_t)(mid + alH * 3600L + alM * 60L - tzOffset);
            if (alAtEpoch <= nowE) alAtEpoch += 86400L;
            alMode = 2;
            char b[8]; snprintf(b, sizeof b, "%02d:%02d", alH, alM);
            clockMsg(isEnglish ? "Alarm set for" : "Alarma puesta a", b, 1300);
          }
        }
      } else { alMode = 0; sel = 0; clockMsg(isEnglish ? "Alarm off" : "Alarma apagada", "", 900); }
    }
    const char* itES[3] = {"En un tiempo...", "A una hora...", "Cancelar alarma"};
    const char* itEN[3] = {"In some time...", "At a time...", "Cancel alarm"};
    drawMenu(isEnglish ? "ALARM" : "ALARMA", isEnglish ? itEN : itES, nIt, sel);
    char st[24];
    if (alMode == 1) { long sl = ((long)(alAtMs - millis())) / 1000; if (sl < 0) sl = 0; snprintf(st, sizeof st, "%ld:%02ld:%02ld", sl / 3600, (sl / 60) % 60, sl % 60); }
    else if (alMode == 2) snprintf(st, sizeof st, "%02d:%02d", alH, alM);
    else snprintf(st, sizeof st, "OFF");
    display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.setCursor(128 - (int)strlen(st) * 6, 0); display.print(st);
    flushScreen();
  }
  waitEsc();
}

void clockTzMenu() {
  settle();
  while (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == HIGH) {
    int jy = getJoyY();
    if (jy > 700) { if (tzOffset < 14 * 3600L) tzOffset += 1800; tzAuto = false; delay(160); }
    else if (jy < 300) { if (tzOffset > -12 * 3600L) tzOffset -= 1800; tzAuto = false; delay(160); }
    if (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) {
      settle();
      clockMsg(isEnglish ? "Detecting..." : "Detectando...", "", 1);
      if (fetchTimezone()) { tzAuto = true; tzDone = true; }
      else clockMsg(isEnglish ? "No internet" : "Sin internet", isEnglish ? "use stick" : "usa el joystick", 1400);
    }
    long a = tzOffset < 0 ? -tzOffset : tzOffset;
    char b[24]; snprintf(b, sizeof b, "UTC%c%d:%02d", tzOffset < 0 ? '-' : '+', (int)(a / 3600), (int)((a % 3600) / 60));
    display.clearDisplay(); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.setTextSize(1); display.setCursor(0, 0); display.print(isEnglish ? F("TIME ZONE") : F("ZONA HORARIA"));
    display.setTextSize(2); display.setCursor(64 - strlen(b) * 6, 18); display.print(b);
    display.setTextSize(1); display.setCursor(0, 40); display.print(tzAuto ? (isEnglish ? F("Auto-detected") : F("Auto (internet)")) : (isEnglish ? F("Manual") : F("Manual")));
    drawHint(-1, 55, isEnglish ? "{1}: auto  {2}: save" : "{1}: auto  {2}: guardar", SSD1306_WHITE);
    flushScreen();
  }
  waitEsc(); clockSave();
}

void clockSync() {
  if (WiFi.status() != WL_CONNECTED) { clockMsg(isEnglish ? "No Wi-Fi" : "Sin Wi-Fi", isEnglish ? "Connect in Settings" : "Conecta en Ajustes", 1600); return; }
  clockMsg(isEnglish ? "Syncing..." : "Sincronizando...", "", 1);
  ntpKick();
  if (tzAuto && fetchTimezone()) { tzDone = true; clockSave(); }
  unsigned long t0 = millis();
  while (!timeIsValid() && millis() - t0 < 6000) delay(100);
  if (timeIsValid()) { timeManual = false; clockMsg(isEnglish ? "Time updated" : "Hora actualizada", isEnglish ? "from internet" : "desde internet", 1300); }
  else clockMsg(isEnglish ? "No response" : "Sin respuesta", isEnglish ? "try again" : "intenta de nuevo", 1300);
}

void clockMenu() {
  uint8_t sel = 0; settle();
  while (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == HIGH) {
    int jy = getJoyY();
    if (jy > 700) { sel = (sel + 2) % 3; delay(180); } else if (jy < 300) { sel = (sel + 1) % 3; delay(180); }
    if (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) {
      settle();
      if (sel == 0) clockSetTimeOnly();
      else if (sel == 1) clockSetDateOnly();
      else {
        uint8_t f = pickTwo(isEnglish ? "TIME FORMAT" : "FORMATO DE HORA", isEnglish ? "24 hours" : "24 horas", isEnglish ? "12 hours (AM/PM)" : "12 horas (AM/PM)");
        if (f != 255) { clock24 = (f == 0); clockSave(); }
      }
      delay(150);
    }
    const char* it[3] = { isEnglish ? "Change time" : "Cambiar hora", isEnglish ? "Change date" : "Cambiar fecha", isEnglish ? "Change format" : "Cambiar formato" };
    display.clearDisplay(); display.setTextSize(1); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.setCursor(0, 0); display.print(isEnglish ? F("OPTIONS") : F("OPCIONES"));
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      int y = 16 + i * 15;
      if (i == sel) { display.fillRoundRect(4, y - 3, 120, 14, 3, SSD1306_WHITE); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_BLACK); }
      else display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.setCursor(64 - strlen(it[i]) * 3, y); display.print(it[i]);
    }
    display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
    flushScreen();
  }
  waitEsc();
}

// ---------- 1) CRONOMETRO (el de siempre) ----------
void runStopwatch() {
  settle();
  while (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == HIGH) {
    if (swRun) swEl = millis() - swStart;
    unsigned long elapsed = swEl / 1000;
    display.clearDisplay();
    display.drawRect(0, 0, 128, 64, SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.drawCircle(35, 32, 22, SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.drawCircle(35, 32, 19, SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.fillRect(33, 6, 5, 4, SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.drawPixel(35, 17, SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.drawPixel(35, 47, SSD1306_WHITE);
    float angle = (elapsed % 60) * 0.1047;
    display.drawLine(35, 32, 35 + cos(angle - 1.57) * 14, 32 + sin(angle - 1.57) * 14, SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.setTextSize(2); display.setCursor(72, 16);
    if (elapsed < 10) display.print(F("0"));
    display.print((int)elapsed);
    display.setTextSize(1); display.print(F("s"));
    display.drawRoundRect(70, 40, 50, 16, 3, SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.setCursor(swRun ? 79 : 76, 44);
    if (isEnglish) display.print(swRun ? F("STOP") : F("START"));
    else display.print(swRun ? F("PARAR") : F("INICIO"));
    if (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) {
      if (!swRun) { swStart = millis() - swEl; swRun = true; } else { swEl = millis() - swStart; swRun = false; }
      settle();
    }
    if (digitalRead(PIN_4) == LOW) { if (!swRun) swEl = 0; settle(); }
    flushScreen();
  }
  waitEsc();
}

// ---------- 2) HORA ----------
void clockDraw() {
  display.clearDisplay(); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
  static const char* const wdEs[7] = {"Domingo", "Lunes", "Martes", "Mi\202rcoles", "Jueves", "Viernes", "S\240bado"};
  static const char* const wdEn[7] = {"Sunday", "Monday", "Tuesday", "Wednesday", "Thursday", "Friday", "Saturday"};
  char b[32]; bool ok = timeIsValid();
  display.setTextSize(1);
  display.setCursor(2, 1); display.print(WiFi.status() == WL_CONNECTED ? F("NET") : (timeManual ? F("MAN") : F("---")));
  if (alMode) { display.drawCircle(121, 4, 3, SSD1306_WHITE); display.drawFastHLine(118, 8, 7, SSD1306_WHITE); }
  if (ok) {
    struct tm t; getLocal(&t);
    const char* ap = hourStr(b, t.tm_hour, t.tm_min);
    int wt = (int)strlen(b) * 18;                                  // hora grande, centrada con los segundos
    int x0 = (128 - (wt + 20)) / 2;
    display.setTextSize(3); display.setCursor(x0, 11); display.print(b);
    display.setTextSize(1);
    char sc[6]; snprintf(sc, sizeof sc, "%02d", t.tm_sec);
    display.setCursor(x0 + wt + 3, 26); display.print(sc);
    if (ap[0]) { display.setCursor(x0 + wt + 3, 14); display.print(ap); }
    snprintf(b, sizeof b, "%s %02d/%02d/%04d", (isEnglish ? wdEn : wdEs)[t.tm_wday], t.tm_mday, t.tm_mon + 1, t.tm_year + 1900);
    display.setCursor(64 - strlen(b) * 3, 38); display.print(b);
    display.drawFastHLine(20, 35, 88, SSD1306_WHITE);
  } else {
    display.setTextSize(3); display.setCursor(19, 11); display.print(F("--:--"));
    const char* m = isEnglish ? "No time set" : "Sin hora";
    display.setTextSize(1); display.setCursor(64 - strlen(m) * 3, 38); display.print(m);
  }
  drawKeyHint(50, '3', "", isEnglish ? "Options" : "Opciones");
}

// Lista de 2 opciones. Devuelve 0, 1 o 255 si se sale con el boton 2
uint8_t pickTwo(const char* title, const char* a, const char* b, uint8_t start) {
  uint8_t sel = start ? 1 : 0; settle();
  while (true) {
    if (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == LOW) { waitEsc(); return 255; }
    int jy = getJoyY();
    if (jy > 700 || jy < 300) { sel ^= 1; delay(200); }
    if (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) { settle(); return sel; }
    display.clearDisplay(); display.setTextSize(1); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.setCursor(0, 0); display.print(title);
    const char* it[2] = {a, b};
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
      int y = 18 + i * 17;
      if (i == sel) { display.fillRoundRect(4, y - 3, 120, 14, 3, SSD1306_WHITE); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_BLACK); }
      else { display.drawRoundRect(4, y - 3, 120, 14, 3, SSD1306_WHITE); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE); }
      display.setCursor(64 - strlen(it[i]) * 3, y); display.print(it[i]);
    }
    display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
    flushScreen();
  }
}

void runClockScreen() {
  if (!clockFmtDone) {                                   // primera vez: formato
    uint8_t f = pickTwo(isEnglish ? "TIME FORMAT" : "FORMATO DE HORA", isEnglish ? "24 hours" : "24 horas", isEnglish ? "12 hours (AM/PM)" : "12 horas (AM/PM)");
    if (f == 255) return;
    clock24 = (f == 0); clockFmtDone = true; clockSave();
  }
  if (!timeIsValid()) {                                  // sin hora: por red o manual
    uint8_t m = pickTwo(isEnglish ? "SET THE TIME" : "PONER LA HORA", isEnglish ? "From network" : "Por red (internet)", isEnglish ? "Manual" : "Manual");
    if (m == 255) return;
    if (m == 0) clockSync(); else clockSetManual();
  }
  if (tzAuto && !tzDone && WiFi.status() == WL_CONNECTED) {
    clockMsg(isEnglish ? "Getting time zone" : "Buscando zona", "", 1);
    if (fetchTimezone()) { tzDone = true; clockSave(); }
  }
  settle();
  while (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == HIGH) {
    if (digitalRead(PIN_3) == LOW) { settle(); clockMenu(); }
    clockDraw(); flushScreen();
  }
  waitEsc();
}

// ---------- APP RELOJ: 3 opciones ----------
void runAdvancedTimer() {
  uint8_t sel = 0; settle();
  while (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == HIGH) {
    int jy = getJoyY();
    if (jy > 700) { sel = (sel + 2) % 3; delay(180); } else if (jy < 300) { sel = (sel + 1) % 3; delay(180); }
    if (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) {
      settle();
      if (sel == 0) runStopwatch();
      else if (sel == 1) runClockScreen();
      else clockAlarmMenu();
      delay(150);
    }
    const char* it[3] = { isEnglish ? "Stopwatch" : "Cron\242metro", isEnglish ? "Time" : "Hora", isEnglish ? "Alarm" : "Alarma" };
    display.clearDisplay(); display.setTextSize(1); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
    display.setCursor(0, 0); display.print(isEnglish ? F("CLOCK") : F("RELOJ"));
    if (swRun) { display.setCursor(54, 0); display.print(isEnglish ? F("SW running") : F("Crono activo")); }
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      int y = 14 + i * 16;
      if (i == sel) { display.fillRoundRect(4, y - 3, 120, 14, 3, SSD1306_WHITE); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_BLACK); }
      else display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.setTextSize(1); display.setCursor(64 - strlen(it[i]) * 3, y); display.print(it[i]);
    }
    display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
    flushScreen();
  }
  currentApp = -1; settle();
}

// ---------- Para la barra de estado del escritorio ----------
bool clockStatusText(char* b) {            // b >= 12
  if (!timeIsValid()) return false;
  struct tm t; getLocal(&t);
  char hb[8]; const char* ap = hourStr(hb, t.tm_hour, t.tm_min);
  snprintf(b, 12, "%s%s", hb, ap);
  return true;
}

bool alarmIsOn() { return alMode != 0; }

// ===================== RENDIMIENTO (Config) =====================
static void perfBar(int x, int y, int w, int h, int pct) {
  if (pct < 0) pct = 0; if (pct > 100) pct = 100;
  display.drawRect(x, y, w, h, SSD1306_WHITE);
  display.fillRect(x + 1, y + 1, (w - 2) * pct / 100, h - 2, SSD1306_WHITE);
}
static void perfRow(int y, const char* l, const char* v) {
  display.setCursor(0, y); display.print(l);
  display.setCursor(128 - (int)strlen(v) * 6, y); display.print(v);
}
static void perfBytes(char* b, size_t n, uint32_t v) {
  if (v >= 1048576UL) snprintf(b, n, "%.1fMB", v / 1048576.0f);
  else if (v >= 1024UL) snprintf(b, n, "%.1fKB", v / 1024.0f);
  else snprintf(b, n, "%uB", (unsigned)v);
}

void runPerformance() {
  uint8_t pg = 0; unsigned long lm = 0, lastDraw = 0;
  settle();
  while (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == HIGH) {
    int vy = getJoyY();
    bool moved = false;
    if (millis() - lm > 180) {
      if (vy < 300) { pg = (pg + 1) % 4; lm = millis(); moved = true; }
      else if (vy > 700) { pg = (pg + 3) % 4; lm = millis(); moved = true; }
      else if (digitalRead(PIN_R) == LOW) { pg = (pg + 1) % 4; lm = millis(); moved = true; }
      else if (digitalRead(PIN_L) == LOW) { pg = (pg + 3) % 4; lm = millis(); moved = true; }
    }
    // limitar redibujado: evita trabarse por I2C del OLED
    if (!moved && (millis() - lastDraw) < 100) { delay(12); continue; }
    lastDraw = millis();
    display.clearDisplay(); display.setTextSize(1); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
    static const char* const tES[4] = {"CPU", "RAM", "MEMORIA FLASH", "SISTEMA"};
    static const char* const tEN[4] = {"CPU", "RAM", "FLASH MEMORY", "SYSTEM"};
    display.setCursor(0, 0); display.print((isEnglish ? tEN : tES)[pg]);
    char c[8]; snprintf(c, sizeof c, "%d/4", pg + 1);
    display.setCursor(128 - (int)strlen(c) * 6, 0); display.print(c);
    display.drawFastHLine(0, 9, 128, SSD1306_WHITE);
    char a[24], b[24];
    if (pg == 0) {
      display.setTextSize(2); snprintf(a, sizeof a, "%u%%", (unsigned)perfLoad); display.setCursor(0, 13); display.print(a);
      display.setTextSize(1);
      perfBar(52, 14, 76, 10, perfLoad);
      display.setCursor(52, 26); display.print(TR("carga del loop", "loop load"));
      snprintf(a, sizeof a, "%u MHz", (unsigned)getCpuFrequencyMhz()); perfRow(38, TR("Frecuencia", "Clock"), a);
      snprintf(a, sizeof a, "%u", (unsigned)perfFps); perfRow(47, TR("Pantalla FPS", "Screen FPS"), a);
      snprintf(a, sizeof a, "%.1fC", temperatureRead()); perfRow(56, TR("Temperatura", "Temperature"), a);
    } else if (pg == 1) {
      uint32_t tot = ESP.getHeapSize(), fr = ESP.getFreeHeap();
      perfBytes(a, sizeof a, tot - fr);
      snprintf(b, sizeof b, "%s", a); display.setCursor(0, 13); display.print(TR("En uso", "Used")); display.print(F(" ")); display.print(b);
      perfBar(0, 22, 128, 8, tot ? (int)((tot - fr) * 100ULL / tot) : 0);
      perfBytes(a, sizeof a, tot); perfRow(33, TR("Total", "Total"), a);
      perfBytes(a, sizeof a, fr); perfRow(41, TR("Libre", "Free"), a);
      perfBytes(a, sizeof a, ESP.getMinFreeHeap()); perfRow(49, TR("Minimo libre", "Lowest free"), a);
      perfBytes(a, sizeof a, ESP.getMaxAllocHeap()); perfRow(57, TR("Bloque mayor", "Largest block"), a);
    } else if (pg == 2) {
      uint32_t fl = ESP.getFlashChipSize(), sk = ESP.getSketchSize();
      perfBytes(a, sizeof a, sk); display.setCursor(0, 13); display.print(TR("Programa ", "Program ")); display.print(a);
      perfBar(0, 22, 128, 8, fl ? (int)(sk * 100ULL / fl) : 0);
      perfBytes(a, sizeof a, fl); perfRow(33, TR("Flash total", "Flash total"), a);
      perfBytes(a, sizeof a, ESP.getFreeSketchSpace()); perfRow(41, TR("Libre p/ prog.", "Free for prog."), a);
      nvs_stats_t st; unsigned un = 0, tn = 0;
      if (nvs_get_stats(NULL, &st) == ESP_OK) { un = st.used_entries; tn = st.total_entries; }
      snprintf(a, sizeof a, "%u/%u", un, tn); perfRow(49, TR("Datos (NVS)", "Data (NVS)"), a);
      snprintf(a, sizeof a, "%u%%", tn ? un * 100 / tn : 0); perfRow(57, TR("NVS lleno", "NVS full"), a);
    } else {
      snprintf(a, sizeof a, "%s r%u", ESP.getChipModel(), (unsigned)ESP.getChipRevision()); perfRow(12, "Chip", a);
      snprintf(a, sizeof a, "%u", (unsigned)uxTaskGetNumberOfTasks()); perfRow(20, TR("Tareas", "Tasks"), a);
      perfBytes(a, sizeof a, uxTaskGetStackHighWaterMark(NULL)); perfRow(28, TR("Pila libre", "Free stack"), a);
      unsigned long s = millis() / 1000UL;
      snprintf(a, sizeof a, "%lu:%02lu:%02lu", s / 3600, (s / 60) % 60, s % 60); perfRow(36, TR("Encendido", "Uptime"), a);
      if (WiFi.status() == WL_CONNECTED) snprintf(a, sizeof a, "%ddBm", (int)WiFi.RSSI()); else snprintf(a, sizeof a, "OFF");
      perfRow(44, "Wi-Fi", a);
      snprintf(a, sizeof a, "%s", WiFi.status() == WL_CONNECTED ? WiFi.SSID().c_str() : "-");
      if (strlen(a) > 12) a[12] = 0;
      perfRow(52, "SSID", a);
    }
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 4; i++) {                       // puntos de pagina
      int x = 52 + i * 8;
      if (i == pg) display.fillCircle(x, 62, 1, SSD1306_WHITE); else display.drawPixel(x, 62, SSD1306_WHITE);
    }
    flushScreen(); delay(25);
  }
  settle();
}

static void applyCountry(int idx) {
  if (idx < 0 || idx >= COUNTRY_N) {
    countryIdx = -1;
    saveSys();
    return;
  }
  countryIdx = (int8_t)idx;
  const Country& c = COUNTRIES[idx];
  tzOffset = c.tz;
  tzAuto = false;
  tzDone = true;
  tempFahrenheit = c.useF;
  {
    Preferences p; p.begin("wx", false);
    p.putUChar("city", 255);
    p.end();
  }
  clockSave();
  saveSys();
}

// ===================== CLIMA =====================
// Datos: Open-Meteo (sin clave). Ubicacion: por IP (ipwho.is) o ciudad elegida.
struct WxCity { const char* n; float la, lo; };
static const WxCity WX_CITIES[] = {
  {"Caracas", 10.48f, -66.90f}, {"Maracaibo", 10.63f, -71.64f}, {"Valencia", 10.16f, -68.00f},
  {"Barquisimeto", 10.07f, -69.32f}, {"Maracay", 10.25f, -67.60f}, {"Cd. Guayana", 8.35f, -62.65f},
  {"Barcelona", 10.13f, -64.68f}, {"Merida", 8.59f, -71.15f}, {"Bogota", 4.71f, -74.07f},
  {"Mexico DF", 19.43f, -99.13f}, {"Lima", -12.05f, -77.04f}, {"Santiago", -33.45f, -70.67f},
  {"Buenos Aires", -34.60f, -58.38f}, {"Madrid", 40.42f, -3.70f}, {"Miami", 25.76f, -80.19f},
  {"New York", 40.71f, -74.01f}, {"London", 51.51f, -0.13f}
};
#define WX_NC ((int)(sizeof(WX_CITIES) / sizeof(WX_CITIES[0])))

struct WxData {
  bool ok; float t, wind; int hum, code;
  float tmax[3], tmin[3]; int dcode[3], dwday[3];
  char city[20]; unsigned long ms;
};
static WxData wx;

static uint8_t wxCat(int c) {            // codigo WMO -> 0 sol 1 parcial 2 nube 3 lluvia 4 nieve 5 tormenta 6 niebla
  if (c == 0) return 0;
  if (c <= 2) return 1;
  if (c == 3) return 2;
  if (c == 45 || c == 48) return 6;
  if ((c >= 51 && c <= 67) || (c >= 80 && c <= 82)) return 3;
  if ((c >= 71 && c <= 77) || c == 85 || c == 86) return 4;
  if (c >= 95) return 5;
  return 2;
}

// Icono: x,y esquina; m = 1 (12 px) o 2 (24 px)
static void wxCloud(int cx, int cy, int m, uint16_t col, int g) {
  display.fillCircle(cx - 2 * m, cy + m, 2 * m + g, col);
  display.fillCircle(cx + m, cy, 3 * m + g, col);
  display.fillRect(cx - 2 * m, cy - g, 6 * m, 3 * m + 2 * g + 1, col);
}
static void wxIcon(uint8_t k, int x, int y, int m) {
  const uint16_t W = SSD1306_WHITE, B = SSD1306_BLACK;
  int cx = x + 6 * m, cy = y + 6 * m;
  if (k == 0 || k == 1) {
    int sx = k == 0 ? cx : cx + 2 * m, sy = k == 0 ? cy : cy - 2 * m, r = k == 0 ? (5 * m + 1) / 2 : 2 * m;
    display.fillCircle(sx, sy, r, W);
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
      float a = i * 0.7854f;
      display.drawLine(sx + (int)lroundf((r + 1) * cosf(a)), sy + (int)lroundf((r + 1) * sinf(a)),
                       sx + (int)lroundf((r + 1 + m) * cosf(a)), sy + (int)lroundf((r + 1 + m) * sinf(a)), W);
    }
    if (k == 1) { wxCloud(cx - m, cy + m, m, B, 1); wxCloud(cx - m, cy + m, m, W, 0); }
  } else if (k == 2) {
    wxCloud(cx, cy, m, W, 0);
  } else if (k == 6) {
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 3; i++) display.drawFastHLine(x + m * (i % 2), y + 2 * m + i * 3 * m, 10 * m, W);
  } else {
    wxCloud(cx, cy - 2 * m, m, W, 0);
    if (k == 3) for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 3; i++) display.drawLine(cx - 3 * m + i * 3 * m, cy + 3 * m, cx - 4 * m + i * 3 * m, cy + 5 * m, W);
    else if (k == 4) for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 3; i++) display.fillRect(cx - 3 * m + i * 3 * m, cy + 3 * m + (i % 2) * m, m, m, W);
    else { display.drawLine(cx + m, cy + 2 * m, cx - m, cy + 4 * m, W); display.drawLine(cx - m, cy + 4 * m, cx + m, cy + 4 * m, W); display.drawLine(cx + m, cy + 4 * m, cx - m, cy + 6 * m, W); }
  }
}

static const char* wxName(uint8_t k) {
  static const char* const es[7] = {"Despejado", "Parcial nublado", "Nublado", "Lluvia", "Nieve", "Tormenta", "Niebla"};
  static const char* const en[7] = {"Clear", "Partly cloudy", "Cloudy", "Rain", "Snow", "Thunderstorm", "Fog"};
  return (isEnglish ? en : es)[k];
}
static const char* wxDay(int wd) {
  static const char* const es[7] = {"Dom", "Lun", "Mar", "Mi\202", "Jue", "Vie", "S\240b"};
  static const char* const en[7] = {"Sun", "Mon", "Tue", "Wed", "Thu", "Fri", "Sat"};
  return (isEnglish ? en : es)[wd % 7];
}

static bool wxFetch(int cityIdx) {                 // 0=IP, 255=pais, 1..=ciudades
  float la, lo; String body;
  char name[20];
  if (cityIdx == 255 && countryIdx >= 0 && countryIdx < COUNTRY_N) {
    const Country& c = COUNTRIES[countryIdx];
    la = c.la; lo = c.lo;
    snprintf(name, sizeof name, "%s", c.name);
  } else if (cityIdx <= 0) {
    if (httpsGet("https://ipwho.is/?fields=success,latitude,longitude,city", body) != 200) return false;
    StaticJsonDocument<192> doc;
    if (deserializeJson(doc, body)) return false;
    if (!(doc["success"] | false)) return false;
    la = doc["latitude"] | 0.0f; lo = doc["longitude"] | 0.0f;
    snprintf(name, sizeof name, "%s", (const char*)(doc["city"] | "?"));
    utf8ToCp437(name);
  } else if (cityIdx >= 1 && cityIdx <= WX_NC) {
    const WxCity& c = WX_CITIES[cityIdx - 1];
    la = c.la; lo = c.lo; snprintf(name, sizeof name, "%s", c.n);
  } else {
    return false;
  }
  char url[256];
  snprintf(url, sizeof url, "https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast?latitude=%.2f&longitude=%.2f"
           "&current=temperature_2m,relative_humidity_2m,weather_code,wind_speed_10m"
           "&daily=weather_code,temperature_2m_max,temperature_2m_min&timezone=auto&forecast_days=4", la, lo);
  if (httpsGet(url, body) != 200) return false;
  StaticJsonDocument<256> filter;
  filter["current"]["temperature_2m"] = true; filter["current"]["relative_humidity_2m"] = true;
  filter["current"]["weather_code"] = true; filter["current"]["wind_speed_10m"] = true;
  filter["daily"]["time"] = true; filter["daily"]["weather_code"] = true;
  filter["daily"]["temperature_2m_max"] = true; filter["daily"]["temperature_2m_min"] = true;
  DynamicJsonDocument doc(2048);
  if (deserializeJson(doc, body, DeserializationOption::Filter(filter))) return false;
  JsonObject cur = doc["current"];
  if (cur.isNull()) return false;
  wx.t = cur["temperature_2m"] | 0.0f; wx.hum = cur["relative_humidity_2m"] | 0;
  wx.code = cur["weather_code"] | 0; wx.wind = cur["wind_speed_10m"] | 0.0f;
  for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    wx.tmax[i] = doc["daily"]["temperature_2m_max"][i + 1] | 0.0f;
    wx.tmin[i] = doc["daily"]["temperature_2m_min"][i + 1] | 0.0f;
    wx.dcode[i] = doc["daily"]["weather_code"][i + 1] | 0;
    const char* ds = doc["daily"]["time"][i + 1] | "2026-01-01";
    int y = 2026, m = 1, d = 1; sscanf(ds, "%d-%d-%d", &y, &m, &d);
    wx.dwday[i] = (int)(((daysFromCivil(y, m, d) % 7) + 11) % 7);
  }
  snprintf(wx.city, sizeof wx.city, "%s", name);
  wx.ok = true; wx.ms = millis();
  return true;
}

static void wxLoading() {
  display.clearDisplay(); display.setTextSize(1); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
  const char* m = TR("Cargando clima...", "Loading weather...");
  display.setCursor(64 - (int)strlen(m) * 3, 28); display.print(m);
  flushScreen();
}

static void wxDraw() {
  display.clearDisplay(); display.setTextSize(1); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
  display.setCursor(0, 0); display.print(wx.city);
  char b[24];
  unsigned long age = (millis() - wx.ms) / 60000UL;
  if (age == 0) snprintf(b, sizeof b, "%s", TR("ahora", "now")); else snprintf(b, sizeof b, "%lum", age);
  display.setCursor(128 - (int)strlen(b) * 6, 0); display.print(b);
  uint8_t k = wxCat(wx.code);
  wxIcon(k, 2, 9, 2);
  auto toUnit = [](float c) -> int {
    if (tempFahrenheit) return (int)lroundf(c * 9.0f / 5.0f + 32.0f);
    return (int)lroundf(c);
  };
  int t = toUnit(wx.t);
  snprintf(b, sizeof b, "%d", t);
  display.setTextSize(2); display.setCursor(34, 9); display.print(b);
  int tx = 34 + (int)strlen(b) * 12;
  display.drawCircle(tx + 2, 11, 2, SSD1306_WHITE);
  display.setTextSize(1); display.setCursor(tx + 7, 9); display.print(tempFahrenheit ? F("F") : F("C"));
  display.setCursor(34, 26); display.print(wxName(k));
  snprintf(b, sizeof b, "%s %d%%  %s %dkm/h", "Hum", wx.hum, TR("Viento", "Wind"), (int)lroundf(wx.wind));
  display.setCursor(0, 35); display.print(b);
  for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    int x0 = i * 43;
    display.setCursor(x0 + 2, 43); display.print(wxDay(wx.dwday[i]));
    wxIcon(wxCat(wx.dcode[i]), x0 + 1, 51, 1);
    snprintf(b, sizeof b, "%d", toUnit(wx.tmax[i])); display.setCursor(x0 + 16, 50); display.print(b);
    snprintf(b, sizeof b, "%d", toUnit(wx.tmin[i])); display.setCursor(x0 + 16, 57); display.print(b);
    if (i < 2) display.drawFastVLine(x0 + 41, 44, 19, SSD1306_WHITE);
  }
  flushScreen();
}

void runWeather() {
  settle();
  if (WiFi.status() != WL_CONNECTED) {
    while (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == HIGH) { drawNeedWifi(); flushScreen(); delay(30); }
    currentApp = -1; settle(); return;
  }
  int cityIdx;
  { Preferences p; p.begin("wx", true); cityIdx = p.getUChar("city", 0); p.end(); }
  if (cityIdx != 255 && (cityIdx < 0 || cityIdx > WX_NC)) cityIdx = 0;
  if (cityIdx == 255 && (countryIdx < 0 || countryIdx >= COUNTRY_N)) cityIdx = 0;
  if (!wx.ok || millis() - wx.ms > 600000UL || wx.ms == 0) {
    wxLoading();
    if (!wxFetch(cityIdx)) netErrorToast();
  }
  while (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == HIGH) {
    if (!wx.ok) {                                        // sin datos: reintentar con {1}
      display.clearDisplay(); display.setTextSize(1); display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
      const char* m = TR("Sin datos del clima", "No weather data");
      display.setCursor(64 - (int)strlen(m) * 3, 22); display.print(m);
      drawHint(-1, 40, TR("{1}: reintentar", "{1}: retry"), SSD1306_WHITE);
      flushScreen();
    } else wxDraw();
    if (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW || digitalRead(PIN_4) == LOW) {      // actualizar
      settle(); wxLoading();
      if (!wxFetch(cityIdx)) netErrorToast();
    }
    if (digitalRead(PIN_3) == LOW) {                                  // cambiar ciudad
      settle();
      const char* items[WX_NC + 1];
      items[0] = TR("Auto (por IP)", "Auto (by IP)");
      for (int i = 0; i < WX_NC; i++) items[i + 1] = WX_CITIES[i].n;
      int r = pickList(TR("CIUDAD", "CITY"), items, WX_NC + 1, cityIdx);
      if (r >= 0) {
        cityIdx = r;
        Preferences p; p.begin("wx", false); p.putUChar("city", (uint8_t)cityIdx); p.end();
        wxLoading();
        if (!wxFetch(cityIdx)) netErrorToast();
      }
    }
    delay(20);
  }
  currentApp = -1; settle();
}

// ===================== CALENDARIO / AGENDA =====================
#ifndef CAL_MAX
#define CAL_MAX 16
#define CAL_TXT 41
#endif
uint32_t calDate[CAL_MAX];
char calTxt[CAL_MAX][CAL_TXT];

void calLoad() {
  Preferences p; p.begin("cal", true);
  for (uint8_t i = 0; i < CAL_MAX; i++) {
    char kd[4], kt[4]; snprintf(kd, sizeof kd, "d%u", i); snprintf(kt, sizeof kt, "t%u", i);
    calDate[i] = p.getUInt(kd, 0);
    String s = p.getString(kt, "");
    strncpy(calTxt[i], s.c_str(), CAL_TXT - 1); calTxt[i][CAL_TXT - 1] = 0;
    if (!calDate[i]) calTxt[i][0] = 0;
  }
  p.end();
}
static void calSaveSlot(uint8_t i) {
  Preferences p; p.begin("cal", false);
  char kd[4], kt[4]; snprintf(kd, sizeof kd, "d%u", i); snprintf(kt, sizeof kt, "t%u", i);
  if (calDate[i]) { p.putUInt(kd, calDate[i]); p.putString(kt, calTxt[i]); }
  else { p.remove(kd); p.remove(kt); }
  p.end();
}

static void civilFromDays(long z, int* y, int* m, int* d) {
  z += 719468;
  long era = (z >= 0 ? z : z - 146096) / 146097;
  long doe = z - era * 146097;
  long yoe = (doe - doe / 1460 + doe / 36524 - doe / 146096) / 365;
  long yy = yoe + era * 400;
  long doy = doe - (365 * yoe + yoe / 4 - yoe / 100);
  long mp = (5 * doy + 2) / 153;
  *d = (int)(doy - (153 * mp + 2) / 5 + 1);
  *m = (int)(mp < 10 ? mp + 3 : mp - 9);
  *y = (int)(yy + (*m <= 2));
}
static int calWday(int y, int m, int d) { return (int)(((daysFromCivil(y, m, d) % 7) + 11) % 7); }   // 0 = domingo
static uint32_t calKey(int y, int m, int d) { return (uint32_t)y * 10000UL + m * 100 + d; }

// Pantalla de un dia: lista de eventos y "+ Nuevo"
static void calDay(int y, int m, int d) {
  uint32_t key = calKey(y, m, d);
  while (true) {
    int idx[CAL_MAX]; uint8_t n = 0;
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < CAL_MAX; i++) if (calDate[i] == key) idx[n++] = i;
    static char lab[CAL_MAX + 1][19];
    const char* items[CAL_MAX + 1];
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < n; i++) { snprintf(lab[i], sizeof lab[i], "%.17s", calTxt[idx[i]]); items[i] = lab[i]; }
    uint8_t total = n;
    if (n < CAL_MAX) { items[total++] = TR("+ Nuevo evento", "+ New event"); }
    char title[24]; snprintf(title, sizeof title, "%02d/%02d/%04d", d, m, y);
    int r = pickList(title, items, total, 0);
    if (r < 0) return;
    if (r < n) {
      uint8_t slot = idx[r];
      uint8_t v = viewText(title, calTxt[slot], TR("{3} editar {4} borrar", "{3} edit {4} del"), 3);
      if (v == 4) { calDate[slot] = 0; calTxt[slot][0] = 0; calSaveSlot(slot); }
      else if (v == 3) { if (textInput(TR("Evento:", "Event:"), calTxt[slot], CAL_TXT)) { if (!calTxt[slot][0]) calDate[slot] = 0; calSaveSlot(slot); } }
    } else {
      char buf[CAL_TXT] = "";
      if (textInput(TR("Evento:", "Event:"), buf, CAL_TXT) && buf[0]) {
        for (uint8_t i = 0; i < CAL_MAX; i++) if (!calDate[i]) {
          calDate[i] = key; strncpy(calTxt[i], buf, CAL_TXT - 1); calTxt[i][CAL_TXT - 1] = 0; calSaveSlot(i); break;
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

// Dibuja un mes del calendario en offset horizontal (para animacion L/R)
// Dibuja un mes del calendario en offset horizontal (animacion L/R)
// Calendario ligero: sin animacion doble (evita lag en OLED/I2C)
static void calDrawMonth(int yy, int mm, int ty, int tm, int td, int selD) {
  static const char* const mES[12] = {"ENE","FEB","MAR","ABR","MAY","JUN","JUL","AGO","SEP","OCT","NOV","DIC"};
  static const char* const mEN[12] = {"JAN","FEB","MAR","APR","MAY","JUN","JUL","AUG","SEP","OCT","NOV","DEC"};

  char hb[16];
  snprintf(hb, sizeof hb, "%s %d", (isEnglish ? mEN : mES)[mm - 1], yy);
  display.setTextSize(1);
  display.setCursor(64 - (int)strlen(hb) * 3, 0);
  display.print(hb);
  display.drawFastHLine(0, 9, 128, SSD1306_WHITE);

  static const char* const wES = "LMMJVSD";
  static const char* const wEN = "SMTWTFS";
  const char* wl = isEnglish ? wEN : wES;
  for (uint8_t c = 0; c < 7; c++) {
    display.setCursor(4 + c * 18, 11);
    display.write((uint8_t)wl[c]);
  }

  int off = calWday(yy, mm, 1);
  if (!isEnglish) off = (off + 6) % 7;
  int mx = daysInMonth(yy, mm);

  for (int n = 1; n <= mx; n++) {
    int i = off + n - 1;
    int r = i / 7, c = i % 7;
    int x = 1 + c * 18;
    int yrow = 20 + r * 7;

    bool isToday = (yy == ty && mm == tm && n == td);
    bool isSel = (n == selD);
    bool hasEv = false;
    uint32_t k = calKey(yy, mm, n);
    for (uint8_t e = 0; e < CAL_MAX; e++) if (calDate[e] == k) { hasEv = true; break; }

    if (isToday) {
      display.fillRoundRect(x + 1, yrow - 1, 15, 7, 1, SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.setTextColor(SSD1306_BLACK);
    } else {
      display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
    }
