    char nb[4];
    snprintf(nb, sizeof nb, "%d", n);
    display.setCursor(x + (18 - (int)strlen(nb) * 6) / 2, yrow);
    display.print(nb);
    display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);

    if (hasEv) {
      display.fillRect(x + 4, yrow + 5, 8, 1, isToday ? SSD1306_BLACK : SSD1306_WHITE);
    }
    if (isSel) {
      display.drawRoundRect(x, yrow - 1, 17, 7, 1, isToday ? SSD1306_BLACK : SSD1306_WHITE);
    }
  }
}

void runCalendar() {
  calLoad();
  int ty, tm, td, th, tmi;
  clockCurrent(&ty, &tm, &td, &th, &tmi);
  int y = ty, m = tm, d = td;
  unsigned long lm = 0, lb = 0;
  settle();
  while (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == HIGH) {
    unsigned long now = millis();

    if (now - lm > 140) {
      int vx = getJoyX(), vy = getJoyY(), delta = 0;
      if (vx < 280) delta = 1;
      else if (vx > 720) delta = -1;
      else if (vy < 280) delta = 7;
      else if (vy > 720) delta = -7;
      if (delta) {
        civilFromDays(daysFromCivil(y, m, d) + delta, &y, &m, &d);
        lm = now;
      }
    }
    if (now - lb > 220) {
      int dm = 0;
      if (digitalRead(PIN_R) == LOW) dm = 1;
      else if (digitalRead(PIN_L) == LOW) dm = -1;
      if (dm) {
        m += dm;
        if (m > 12) { m = 1; y++; }
        if (m < 1) { m = 12; y--; }
        if (y < 2024) { y = 2024; m = 1; }
        if (y > 2099) { y = 2099; m = 12; }
        int mx = daysInMonth(y, m);
        if (d > mx) d = mx;
        lb = now;
      }
    }
    if (digitalRead(PIN_3) == LOW) {
      y = ty; m = tm; d = td;
      settle();
    }
    if (digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW) {
      settle();
      calDay(y, m, d);
      lm = millis();
    }

    display.clearDisplay();
    display.setTextSize(1);
    display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE);
    calDrawMonth(y, m, ty, tm, td, d);
    flushScreen();
    delay(16);
  }
  currentApp = -1;
  settle();
}

// ===================== DIBUJO =====================
// Lienzo de 128x64 (1 bit). Guarda hasta 3 dibujos en la flash (comprimidos).
#define DRAW_SLOTS 3
// dcv[1024] declarado arriba (global, compartido con Archivos)
static uint8_t dundo[1024];
static bool dHasUndo = false, dDirty = false;

static inline void dPx(int x, int y, bool on) {
  if (x < 0 || x > 127 || y < 0 || y > 63) return;
  if (on) dcv[x + (y >> 3) * 128] |= (uint8_t)(1 << (y & 7)); else dcv[x + (y >> 3) * 128] &= (uint8_t)~(1 << (y & 7));
}
static inline bool dGet(int x, int y) {
  if (x < 0 || x > 127 || y < 0 || y > 63) return false;
  return (dcv[x + (y >> 3) * 128] >> (y & 7)) & 1;
}
static void dBrush(int x, int y, int s, bool on) {
  int a = s / 2, b = s - a - 1;
  for (int j = y - a; j <= y + b; j++) for (int i = x - a; i <= x + b; i++) dPx(i, j, on);
}
static void dLineBrush(int x0, int y0, int x1, int y1, int s, bool on) {
  int dx = abs(x1 - x0), dy = -abs(y1 - y0), sx = x0 < x1 ? 1 : -1, sy = y0 < y1 ? 1 : -1, err = dx + dy;
  while (true) {
    dBrush(x0, y0, s, on);
    if (x0 == x1 && y0 == y1) break;
    int e2 = 2 * err;
    if (e2 >= dy) { err += dy; x0 += sx; }
    if (e2 <= dx) { err += dx; y0 += sy; }
  }
}
// Dibuja una figura con las funciones de la pantalla: el buffer de la pantalla ya contiene el lienzo
static void dShape(uint8_t tool, int x0, int y0, int x1, int y1, bool fill) {
  const uint16_t W = SSD1306_WHITE;
  if (tool == 2) display.drawLine(x0, y0, x1, y1, W);
  else if (tool == 3) {
    int x = min(x0, x1), y = min(y0, y1), w = abs(x1 - x0) + 1, h = abs(y1 - y0) + 1;
    if (fill) display.fillRect(x, y, w, h, W); else display.drawRect(x, y, w, h, W);
  } else if (tool == 4) {
    int r = (int)lroundf(sqrtf((float)((x1 - x0) * (x1 - x0) + (y1 - y0) * (y1 - y0))));
    if (fill) display.fillCircle(x0, y0, r, W); else display.drawCircle(x0, y0, r, W);
  }
}
static void dFloodFill(int sx, int sy) {                    // relleno por lineas
  bool target = dGet(sx, sy), repl = !target;
  static int16_t stk[400];
  int sp = 0;
  stk[sp++] = sx; stk[sp++] = sy;
  while (sp > 0) {
    int y = stk[--sp], x = stk[--sp];
    if (dGet(x, y) != target) continue;
    int xl = x, xr = x;
    while (xl > 0 && dGet(xl - 1, y) == target) xl--;
    while (xr < 127 && dGet(xr + 1, y) == target) xr++;
    for (int i = xl; i <= xr; i++) dPx(i, y, repl);
    for (int dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy += 2) {
      int ny = y + dy; if (ny < 0 || ny > 63) continue;
      bool in = false;
      for (int i = xl; i <= xr; i++) {
        bool t = dGet(i, ny) == target;
        if (t && !in) { if (sp < 398) { stk[sp++] = i; stk[sp++] = ny; } in = true; }
        else if (!t) in = false;
      }
    }
  }
}

// Compresion simple (cuenta, valor). Devuelve 0 si no cabe en cap.
static size_t dEncode(const uint8_t* in, uint8_t* out, size_t cap) {
  size_t o = 0, i = 0;
  while (i < 1024) {
    uint8_t v = in[i]; size_t r = 1;
    while (i + r < 1024 && in[i + r] == v && r < 255) r++;
    if (o + 2 > cap) return 0;
    out[o++] = (uint8_t)r; out[o++] = v; i += r;
  }
  return o;
}
static bool dDecode(const uint8_t* in, size_t n, uint8_t* out) {
  size_t o = 0;
  for (size_t i = 0; i + 1 < n; i += 2) for (uint8_t r = 0; r < in[i]; r++) { if (o >= 1024) return false; out[o++] = in[i + 1]; }
  return o == 1024;
}
static bool dSlotUsed(uint8_t s) {
  Preferences p; p.begin("draw", true);
  char k[4]; snprintf(k, sizeof k, "s%u", s);
  bool u = p.getBytesLength(k) > 0;
  p.end(); return u;
}
static void dSaveSlot(uint8_t s) {
  static uint8_t tmp[1024];
  size_t n = dEncode(dcv, tmp, 1022);
  Preferences p; p.begin("draw", false);
  char k[4]; snprintf(k, sizeof k, "s%u", s);
  if (n) p.putBytes(k, tmp, n); else p.putBytes(k, dcv, 1024);
  p.end();
}
static bool dLoadSlot(uint8_t s) {
  static uint8_t tmp[1024];
  Preferences p; p.begin("draw", true);
  char k[4]; snprintf(k, sizeof k, "s%u", s);
  size_t n = p.getBytesLength(k), got = 0;
  if (n > 0 && n <= 1024) got = p.getBytes(k, tmp, n);
  p.end();
  if (got != n || n == 0) return false;
  if (n == 1024) { memcpy(dcv, tmp, 1024); return true; }
  return dDecode(tmp, n, dcv);
}
static void dDeleteSlot(uint8_t s) {
  Preferences p; p.begin("draw", false);
  char k[4]; snprintf(k, sizeof k, "s%u", s); p.remove(k);
  p.end();
}
// Lista de las 3 ranuras. onlyUsed: solo las guardadas. Devuelve la ranura o -1.
static int dPickSlot(const char* title, bool onlyUsed) {
  static char lab[DRAW_SLOTS][20];
  const char* items[DRAW_SLOTS]; uint8_t map[DRAW_SLOTS], n = 0;
  for (uint8_t s = 0; s < DRAW_SLOTS; s++) {
    bool u = dSlotUsed(s);
    if (onlyUsed && !u) continue;
    snprintf(lab[n], sizeof lab[n], "%s %u%s", TR("Dibujo", "Drawing"), s + 1, u ? "" : TR(" (vacio)", " (empty)"));
    items[n] = lab[n]; map[n] = s; n++;
  }
  if (n == 0) { clockMsg(TR("No hay dibujos", "No drawings"), TR("guardados", "saved"), 1200); return -1; }
  int r = pickList(title, items, n, 0);
  return r < 0 ? -1 : map[r];
}

void runDraw() {
  static const char* const toolES[5] = {"Lapiz", "Borrador", "Linea", "Rectangulo", "Circulo"};
  static const char* const toolEN[5] = {"Pencil", "Eraser", "Line", "Rectangle", "Circle"};
  // ---- pantalla inicial: nuevo o cargar ----
  bool ready = false;
  while (!ready) {
    uint8_t m = pickTwo(TR("DIBUJO", "DRAW"), TR("Nuevo dibujo", "New drawing"), TR("Cargar dibujo", "Load drawing"));
    if (m == 255) { currentApp = -1; settle(); return; }
    memset(dcv, 0, sizeof dcv);
    if (m == 0) ready = true;
    else {
      int s = dPickSlot(TR("CARGAR", "LOAD"), true);
      if (s >= 0) { if (dLoadSlot((uint8_t)s)) ready = true; else clockMsg(TR("Error al cargar", "Load error"), "", 1200); }
    }
  }
  dHasUndo = false; dDirty = false;
  // ---- editor ----
  // stick: mover | {1}: dibujar | {3}: herramienta | {4}: deshacer | L: grosor | R: relleno | stick(click): lapiz fijo | {2}: menu
  int cx = 64, cy = 32, px = 64, py = 32, ax = -1, ay = -1;
  uint8_t tool = 0, size = 1; bool fill = false, lock = false, wasDown = false;
  unsigned long lastStep = 0, hudUntil = millis() + 3000;
  bool p3 = true, p4 = true, pL = true, pR = true, pS = true, pOk = true;
  char hud[28]; snprintf(hud, sizeof hud, "%s", TR("OK dibuja 3 herram.", "OK draw 3 tool"));
  settle();
  while (true) {
    unsigned long now = millis();
    // --- mover el cursor ---
    if (now - lastStep >= 14) {
      lastStep = now;
      int vx = getJoyX(), vy = getJoyY();
      int sp = 1;
      if (vx < 120 || vx > 900 || vy < 120 || vy > 900) sp = 3; else if (vx < 220 || vx > 800 || vy < 220 || vy > 800) sp = 2;
      if (vx < 300) cx += sp; else if (vx > 700) cx -= sp;
      if (vy < 300) cy += sp; else if (vy > 700) cy -= sp;
      cx = constrain(cx, 0, 127); cy = constrain(cy, 0, 63);
    }
    bool ok = digitalRead(PIN_OK) == LOW, b3 = digitalRead(PIN_3) == LOW, b4 = digitalRead(PIN_4) == LOW;
    bool bL = digitalRead(PIN_L) == LOW, bR = digitalRead(PIN_R) == LOW, bS = digitalRead(PIN_STICK) == LOW;
    // --- botones (por flanco) ---
    if (b3 && !p3) { tool = (tool + 1) % 6; ax = -1; snprintf(hud, sizeof hud, "%s", tool == 5 ? TR("Rellenar", "Fill") : (isEnglish ? toolEN : toolES)[tool]); hudUntil = now + 1200; }
    if (b4 && !p4 && dHasUndo) { memcpy(dcv, dundo, 1024); dHasUndo = false; ax = -1; snprintf(hud, sizeof hud, "%s", TR("Deshecho", "Undone")); hudUntil = now + 900; }
    if (bL && !pL) { size = size % 3 + 1; snprintf(hud, sizeof hud, "%s %dpx", TR("Grosor", "Size"), size); hudUntil = now + 1000; }
    if (bR && !pR) { fill = !fill; snprintf(hud, sizeof hud, "%s %s", TR("Relleno", "Fill"), fill ? "ON" : "OFF"); hudUntil = now + 1000; }
    if (bS && !pS) { lock = !lock; snprintf(hud, sizeof hud, "%s %s", TR("Lapiz fijo", "Pen lock"), lock ? "ON" : "OFF"); hudUntil = now + 1000; }
    bool down = ok || (lock && (tool <= 1));
    if (tool <= 1) {                                       // lapiz / borrador
      if (down && !wasDown) { memcpy(dundo, dcv, 1024); dHasUndo = true; px = cx; py = cy; }
      if (down) { dLineBrush(px, py, cx, cy, tool == 1 ? size + 2 : size, tool == 0); px = cx; py = cy; dDirty = true; }
      wasDown = down;
    } else if (ok && !pOk) {                               // figuras y relleno
      if (tool == 5) { memcpy(dundo, dcv, 1024); dHasUndo = true; dFloodFill(cx, cy); dDirty = true; }
      else if (ax < 0) { ax = cx; ay = cy; }
      else {
        memcpy(dundo, dcv, 1024); dHasUndo = true;
        memcpy(display.getBuffer(), dcv, 1024);
        dShape(tool, ax, ay, cx, cy, fill);
        memcpy(dcv, display.getBuffer(), 1024);
        ax = -1; dDirty = true;
      }
    }
    pOk = ok; p3 = b3; p4 = b4; pL = bL; pR = bR; pS = bS;
    // --- menu de pausa ---
    if (digitalRead(PIN_ESC) == LOW) {
      settle();
      if (ax >= 0) { ax = -1; continue; }                  // primero cancela la figura en curso
      const char* itES[5] = {"Seguir", "Guardar", "Limpiar", "Borrar guardado", "Salir"};
      const char* itEN[5] = {"Resume", "Save", "Clear", "Delete saved", "Exit"};
      int r = pickList(TR("DIBUJO", "DRAW"), isEnglish ? itEN : itES, 5, 0);
      if (r == 1) {
        int s = dPickSlot(TR("GUARDAR EN", "SAVE TO"), false);
        if (s >= 0) { dSaveSlot((uint8_t)s); dDirty = false; clockMsg(TR("Guardado", "Saved"), "", 800); }
      } else if (r == 2) { memcpy(dundo, dcv, 1024); dHasUndo = true; memset(dcv, 0, 1024); dDirty = true; }
      else if (r == 3) { int s = dPickSlot(TR("BORRAR", "DELETE"), true); if (s >= 0) { dDeleteSlot((uint8_t)s); clockMsg(TR("Borrado", "Deleted"), "", 800); } }
      else if (r == 4) { if (!dDirty || confirmExit("Dibujo", "Drawing")) break; }
      pOk = true; p3 = p4 = pL = pR = pS = true; wasDown = false;
      continue;
    }
    // --- dibujar la pantalla ---
    memcpy(display.getBuffer(), dcv, 1024);
    if (ax >= 0) dShape(tool, ax, ay, cx, cy, fill);       // vista previa
    display.setTextSize(1);
    if (millis() < hudUntil) {                             // etiqueta de herramienta
      int w = (int)strlen(hud) * 6 + 4;
      display.fillRect(0, 0, w, 10, SSD1306_BLACK); display.drawRect(0, 0, w, 10, SSD1306_WHITE);
      display.setTextColor(SSD1306_WHITE); display.setCursor(2, 1); display.print(hud);
    }
    if (lock) display.fillRect(126, 0, 2, 2, SSD1306_INVERSE);
    for (int i = 2; i <= 3; i++) {                         // cursor en cruz (se ve sobre blanco y negro)
      display.drawPixel(cx - i, cy, SSD1306_INVERSE); display.drawPixel(cx + i, cy, SSD1306_INVERSE);
      display.drawPixel(cx, cy - i, SSD1306_INVERSE); display.drawPixel(cx, cy + i, SSD1306_INVERSE);
    }
    flushScreen();
    delay(2);
  }
  currentApp = -1; settle();
}
